# AINA CRM — Full Review: Bugs, Errors & Improvements

_Reviewed: 2026-10-03 · Commit `d8f6f3b` (branch `claude/relaxed-mendel-01jprq`) · ~2,400 source files, ~390k lines_

This document lists every bug, error and improvement found in a module-by-module review of the whole CRM. Every module was checked page by page, API route by API route (GET / POST / PATCH / DELETE) and action by action (buttons, forms, bulk actions, imports, exports, background jobs) for:

- **Does it work?** The UI calls an API that exists, with field names the API reads; the change is saved; state transitions are valid; double clicks and re-imports don't duplicate.
- **Who can do it?** Session, capability and the client/lead book scope inside a firm, plus isolation between firms.
- **Is the data right?** Money and tax maths, India dates (IST, April–March FY), "null is not zero", audit trail, orphaned records.
- **Does it fail well?** Validation, error handling, performance (unbounded queries, N+1), empty/loading states.

## How to read this

- **IDs** are stable and grouped by area: `SEC` security, `ACC` access inside a firm, `MSG` messaging/automation engine, `BIL` billing/integrations/AI, `NUM` money & metrics, then one prefix per module group (`CLI` clients, `LEAD`, `SRV` tasks & service, `PF` portfolio, `PLN` planning, `INS` insurance, `COM` comms & marketing, `ORG` firm & HR, `RPT` compliance & reports, `SET` settings & admin, `DSH` dashboards, `PUB` portal/public/mobile).
- **Severity:** **Critical** = exploitable now for account takeover or bulk data exposure · **High** = data leak, data loss, wrong money, or a core flow that is broken · **Medium** = wrong behaviour in a realistic scenario · **Low** = edge case or minor.
- Each finding gives the file and line, what goes wrong with a concrete scenario, and a one-line fix.
- **✔ verified** means the finding was re-checked line by line a second time. Every other finding was confirmed by reading the code path, but not executed — the app was not run against a database. Line numbers refer to commit `d8f6f3b`.
- Three findings were found independently by two reviewers and are marked _(Same issue as …)_.

**Bottom line**

- **Isolation between firms holds.** The database role cannot bypass row-level security, every `tenant_id` table has a policy, and none of the ~120 raw-SQL sites is injectable. The cross-firm gaps found are all outside the database: **file storage** (SEC-01, CLI-33) and a **server file read through email attachments** (SEC-24) that exposes the platform's own secrets.
- **The biggest risk is access *inside* a firm.** CI's `check:rbac` only checks that *mutation API routes* call a capability check. **Pages (server components) and GET routes are not checked at all**, and a large share of the High findings are pages or GET endpoints that load firm-wide data for any signed-in user (client 360, transactions, reports, service requests, complaints, meetings, leave, referrals, nurture, brokerage, audit history, employee page).
- **Several core flows are broken outright:** editing an insurance policy (INS-01), Excel transaction import beyond 200 rows (PF-02), SIP "Auto-recover" (PF-11), cadences (LEAD-37), client portfolio/onboarding links (PUB-01), campaign "Retry failed" (COM-01), the unsubscribe link (MSG-03).
- **Money numbers that disagree or are wrong:** tax harvesting/gains, SIP book on RM/Branch desks, reconciliation, reports that drop RTA-imported transactions (RPT-02), phantom SIPs from cancelled business entries (PF-07), duplicated payout income (ORG-31).

## Fix first — the top 31

Ordered by risk × ease. Each is a small, contained change.

| # | ID | What | Why first |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SEC-24 | Template attachment URLs reach nodemailer as file paths on the firm-SMTP path | Any self-signup firm can email itself the server's `.env` → every firm's data. Set `disableFileAccess`/`disableUrlAccess`; accept only own-storage keys |
| 2 | ORG-01 / SET-06 | Password hashes + TOTP secrets in audit rows, shown on an unguarded employee page | Any RM can read the owner's 2FA secret. Redact audit payloads, scrub stored rows, gate the page |
| 3 | SEC-01 | `/api/upload-local` serves private `firm/…` KYC files publicly and accepts SVG uploads | Public PAN/Aadhaar files + stored XSS → firm takeover |
| 4 | CLI-33 | Client document `storageKey` not bound to the firm | Read/delete another firm's files; path traversal on local disk |
| 5 | SEC-03 + SEC-04 | Reset links built from Host header; reset skips 2FA | Account takeover with one email |
| 6 | SEC-05 | `settings.users` holder can make themselves owner | Privilege escalation |
| 7 | SEC-02 | No CSP / frame / HSTS headers | Turns every XSS into a takeover; clickjacking |
| 8 | BIL-02 | RTA inbox deletes mail it failed to import (new in d8f6f3b) | Silent data loss, no copy |
| 9 | BIL-01 | Forged email can import fake RTA data into any firm | Corrupts client books from outside |
| 10 | PUB-01 | `/portfolio/` and `/onboarding/<token>` not public | Every client link sends clients to the firm login |
| 11 | INS-01 | Editing any insurance policy fails (strict schema rejects `investorId`) | Core flow broken |
| 12 | PF-02 | Excel transaction import commits only first 200 rows | Silent data loss |
| 13 | PF-11 | SIP "Auto-recover" always 500s (wrong column names hidden by `as any`) | Core button broken |
| 14 | LEAD-37 | Cadences never run (no worker calls the runner) | Feature silently does nothing |
| 15 | MSG-01 | Ad-hoc sends ignore "all" opt-out | Messaging opted-out clients |
| 16 | MSG-03 | Unsubscribe link goes nowhere | Bulk-mail compliance, deliverability |
| 17 | MSG-04 | Inbound WhatsApp phone match unnormalised → STOP lost | Consent |
| 18 | ACC-01, PF-01, RPT-01, RPT-33 | Transactions export/page, generic report viewer, record-history API unscoped | Firm-wide client data to any user |
| 19 | CLI-01 | Client 360 / statement pages skip client scope | Another RM's client (full PAN) by URL |
| 20 | SRV-20, SRV-25, SRV-34, SRV-35 | Service requests, complaints, meetings unscoped | Firm-wide PII to any user |
| 21 | COM-29 | Any staff member can read/post in any client conversation | Data exposure + impersonation |
| 22 | ACC-03 | AI assistant searches the whole firm | Data exposure |
| 23 | NUM-01, PLN-01, CLI-03 | Three ways around the MF suitability gate | Regulatory (SEBI suitability) |
| 24 | SET-24, MSG-10 | Razorpay events out of order / mandates never cancelled revive cancelled tenants | Billing integrity |
| 25 | COM-02, COM-01 | Campaigns can be re-launched; "Retry failed" duplicates | Double sends to clients |
| 26 | COM-05 | Campaign audiences include deleted/merged/closed clients | Duplicate and unwanted messages |
| 27 | PF-07 / ORG-25 | Cancelled business entries leave SIPs in the SIP book | Inflated headline number |
| 28 | ORG-31 | Pasting a payout statement twice doubles income, no undo | Wrong P&L |
| 29 | RPT-02 | Money-flow reports drop RTA-imported transactions | Wrong reports |

## Root causes & platform-wide improvements

These are the patterns behind many individual findings. Fixing them prevents the next hundred.

1. **Extend the RBAC ratchet to pages and GET routes.** `scripts/check-require-cap.mjs` only checks mutation routes. Add every `page.tsx` under `src/app/dashboard` and every `GET` handler, with an explicit allow-list for pages that are deliberately open. This alone would have caught about 20 of the High findings.
2. **One scope helper per subject, used everywhere.** `loadViewableInvestor` / `investorScopeSql` / `leadScopeSql` exist but are applied route by route; sibling routes disagree (GET checks, PATCH/DELETE don't). Add a lint rule or test that every route touching `investors`, `leads`, `proposals`, `goals`, `insurance_policies`, `tasks` by id goes through the helper. Note that in-firm scoping only applies when `tenants.restrict_user_data_scope` is on — decide whether that default should flip.
3. **Stop hiding schema errors behind `as any`.** There are **3,323** `as any` casts. PF-11 (inserts with non-existent columns) and several others type-check only because of them. Lower the `check:asany` baseline every sprint, starting with `tx.insert(...).values({...} as any)`.
4. **Central licence gate.** Enforce `assertActiveLicense` in one wrapper / middleware list for writes, and in `dispatch()` and the workers, instead of per route (BIL-04).
5. **Security headers + upload hygiene.** Add CSP (nonce-based), `frame-ancestors 'self'`, HSTS, nosniff; never serve user uploads inline from the app origin (use a separate download domain or `Content-Disposition: attachment`); restrict `images.remotePatterns`.
6. **Durable rate limiting.** Move `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` counters to Redis/DB, add per-user counters for 2FA, password re-check, OTP and public forms; add a captcha on public lead and complaint forms.
7. **One outbound URL guard.** A single resolve-and-pin SSRF guard (the `http_request` action already has a good one) used by logo fetches, workflow-rule webhooks and integrations.
8. **Every send through one gate.** Make `dispatch()` / `gateClientSend` the only importers of the channel adapters (lint rule), give leads a consent model, implement unsubscribe + `List-Unsubscribe`.
9. **Imports: all rows or none, and say so.** Several imports silently cap (200, 1,000), skip, or roll back the whole batch while reporting success (PF-02, INS-15, LEAD import, CLI import). Standardise on: validate all → report per-row errors → commit valid rows → show inserted / skipped / failed counts.
10. **Dates in IST, always.** Recurring bugs from `new Date("YYYY-MM-DD")` (UTC midnight = 05:30 IST), `::date` on `timestamptz`, and server-local `getMonth()`. Add a lint rule against `new Date(<date-only string>)` and a shared `istDate()` helper.
11. **Never let user input name a file or URL the server opens.** Set `disableFileAccess`/`disableUrlAccess` on every nodemailer transport; pass attachment bytes, not paths (SEC-24).
12. **Audit log hygiene.** Never write whole rows to `audit_log` (secrets, PAN); use per-entity allow-lists. Make `before`/`after` visible only to owner/compliance.
13. **Tests.** 32 test files for ~390k lines. Add route-level tests for authorisation (one per capability), import round-trips, and the money maths (tax, SIP book, FD, XIRR) with worked examples from this review.
14. **Dependencies** — `npm audit --omit=dev` reports 6 high / 4 moderate: `nodemailer` ≤10.0.8 (cross-tenant SMTP credential disclosure via DNS cache — relevant here because firms bring their own SMTP), `mailparser`, `sharp` (libvips CVEs), `postcss` via `next`, `nanoid`, `fflate`, `ip-address`. Upgrade nodemailer/mailparser/sharp now; plan the Next upgrade.
15. **Docs drift.** CLAUDE.md says merge values are HTML-escaped (they are not in three designer paths), that some components are "still used" (they are dead), and lists the RTA importer quota as the only quota gap (`convert_lead` is another). Update it as these are fixed.
16. **Before launch:** `src/lib/legal/company.ts` still carries `TODO` placeholders for the registered legal entity shown on Terms/Privacy/Refund and invoices.

## Not confirmed at runtime

These depend on framework/proxy behaviour and should be tested on a running instance:

- Whether `%2F` inside a catch-all segment reaches `key[0]` in `/api/upload-local/[...key]` (would widen SEC-01 to cross-firm writes).
- Whether the unanchored middleware matcher (SEC-15) can be used to forge `x-pathname` on a real route.
- Caddy forwarding an arbitrary `Host` over a TLS connection to the real domain (SEC-03) — test with `curl --resolve … -H 'Host: evil.tld'`.

# Findings by area

---

## Cross-cutting: security, access, messaging, billing, numbers

### Security — authentication, sessions & permissions

**Bugs**
- **[SEC-24] [Critical] Template attachments can read any file on the server through firm SMTP** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/comms/adapters/resend.ts:100-106,114`, `src/app/api/templates/route.ts:43`, `src/app/api/templates/[id]/route.ts:45` — attachment `url` is any string (`z.string()`), and on the firm-owned SMTP path it is passed to nodemailer as `path`; nodemailer reads local files (and fetches any URL) unless `disableFileAccess`/`disableUrlAccess` is set, which this code never does. A self-signup firm configures its own SMTP, adds an attachment `/proc/self/environ` (or the app's `.env`) to its own template, approves it and sends it to itself — receiving the platform's database credentials, `NEXTAUTH_SECRET` and encryption key, i.e. every firm's data. A URL attachment is also a blind SSRF from the server. _Fix:_ accept only keys in the firm's own storage (download server-side and pass `content`), and set `disableFileAccess: true, disableUrlAccess: true` on every transport.
- **[SEC-01] [Critical] Public file route serves private KYC / compliance files and accepts uploaded scripts** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/upload-local/[...key]/route.ts:37,52,106,144` — `keyMeta` only recognises `tenants/…` keys, so every other key (all `firm/<slug>-<id8>/…` uploads: client KYC scans, compliance vault, calculator PDFs) is served to anonymous callers; the `firm/…` prefix is printed in every firm's public logo URL. PUT accepts any non-`tenants/` key from any signed-in user (signup is free), and `.svg` is served inline as `image/svg+xml` with no CSP → stored XSS on the CRM origin → e.g. `POST /api/employees/invite {roles:["owner"]}` as the victim = firm takeover. Local disk is read before S3, so writes also shadow real S3 objects. _Fix:_ allowlist public prefixes (logos, `tenants/*/{general,marketing}`, `library/`), PUT only under `tenants/<session.tenantId>/`, serve non-images as attachment with `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`.
- **[SEC-02] [High] No security headers at all** ✔ _verified_ — `next.config.ts`, `src/middleware.ts`, `Caddyfile` — no CSP, `X-Frame-Options`/`frame-ancestors`, HSTS, `X-Content-Type-Options`. Clickjacking possible; every XSS bug becomes full account compromise. _Fix:_ add a `headers()` block (CSP with nonces, `frame-ancestors 'self'`, HSTS, nosniff, Referrer-Policy).
- **[SEC-03] [High] Password-reset / invite / verification links built from the attacker-controlled Host header** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/url/request-base.ts:14` (used by `api/auth/forgot`, `verify-email`, `employees/invite`) — `POST /api/auth/forgot` with `Host: evil.tld` emails the victim a genuine reset token pointing at the attacker. _Fix:_ build from `APP_URL` or a host validated with `isServableHost()`.
- **[SEC-04] [High] Password reset bypasses 2FA** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/auth/reset/route.ts:85-95` — a full session cookie is issued even when `two_fa_enabled`. With SEC-03 → account takeover without a second factor. _Fix:_ return a 2FA challenge (or redirect to /login).
- **[SEC-05] [High] A non-owner with `settings.users` can make themselves owner** — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:126,136,183`, `src/app/api/employees/invite/route.ts:57,88` — `owner` is an assignable role and there's no rule against editing your own roles or an owner's row. _Fix:_ only owners grant/remove `owner` or edit owners; forbid self role/permission edits.
- **[SEC-06] [High] Same email in another tenant locks the real user out (incl. platform admins)** — `api/auth/signup/route.ts:53`, `api/auth/login/route.ts:42`, `api/auth/forgot/route.ts:43`, `api/employees/invite/route.ts:105` — emails unique per tenant only; login picks the newest row, forgot-password an arbitrary one. _Fix:_ global unique `lower(email)` or a tenant picker at login.
- **[SEC-07] [Medium] Changing your password doesn't log out other sessions** — `src/app/api/me/password/route.ts:46-50` — `sessions_invalid_after` not stamped. _Fix:_ stamp it, revoke other `user_sessions`, re-issue current.
- **[SEC-08] [Medium] Password re-check and 2FA have no per-user rate limit; TOTP codes can be replayed** — `api/me/password`, `api/auth/2fa/disable/route.ts:132`, `api/auth/2fa/verify/route.ts:30`, `api/auth/mobile-2fa/route.ts:133`, `src/lib/auth/totp.ts:121` — limits are in-memory per-IP (10/15 min); no last-used TOTP step. _Fix:_ DB-backed per-user limiter; store last accepted step; require TOTP to disable 2FA.
- **[SEC-09] [Medium] Re-inviting an offboarded user restores their old custom permissions, then 500s** — `src/app/api/employees/accept-invite/route.ts:83-119` — upsert keeps `permissions`, `enabled_modules`, `deleted_at`, 2FA; uses generated `userId` instead of `RETURNING id` → FK failure. _Fix:_ reset those columns; use the returned id.
- **[SEC-10] [Low] Anyone can lock a user out of login** — `src/lib/auth/login-limit.ts:106` — budget per email only. _Fix:_ add (email, IP) bucket / progressive delay.
- **[SEC-11] [Low] Login reveals which accounts exist** — `api/auth/login/route.ts:51-65` (+ mobile-login) — inactive accounts answer 403 before password check; unknown emails skip bcrypt. _Fix:_ generic 401, dummy compare.
- **[SEC-12] [Low] Open redirect after login** — `src/components/auth/AuthScreen.tsx:33-40` — `safeNext` doesn't strip tab/CR/LF: `/login?next=/%09/evil.tld`. _Fix:_ `new URL(v, origin)` + same-origin check.
- **[SEC-13] [Low] Moving a user to another branch doesn't refresh their session** — `api/employees/[id]/route.ts:217` — branch lock uses `session.branchId` for up to 8 h. _Fix:_ include `branchId` in `accessChanged`.
- **[SEC-14] [Low] `/api/internal/*` and `/api/calendar/feed` unreachable by their callers; internal token compared with `!==`** — `src/middleware.ts` public list — Bearer tokens rejected as session JWTs. _Fix:_ add to public list; `timingSafeEqual`.
- **[SEC-15] [Low] Middleware matcher not anchored; `x-pathname` can be client-supplied** — `src/middleware.ts:37` — paths containing `.js`/`.png` etc. skip middleware; `x-pathname` is trusted by the licence carve-out and mobile boundary. _(Needs runtime confirmation.)_ _Fix:_ anchor with `$`; only trust headers middleware set.

### Security — public pages, uploads, tokens & SSRF

**Bugs**
- **[SEC-16] [Medium] Share-token row id works as the credential and leaks** — public `risk-profile/{submit,pdf,otp/send}`, `feedback`, `docs/{presign,submit}`, `proposals/*` — the UUID (not the hashed token) is accepted, and it sits in query strings, Caddy logs and `events_outbox.meta`; `risk-profile/pdf/route.ts:34-41` ignores expiry/used and returns the client's latest profile (PAN, address). `client-links.ts` stores plaintext share URLs in `payload.url`. _Fix:_ accept only the hashed plaintext token; bound the PDF window.
- **[SEC-17] [Medium] Share links can be minted for any subject; public pages load the subject without a tenant filter** — `src/app/api/share-tokens/route.ts:39-56`, `src/app/onboarding/[token]/page.tsx:35`, `src/app/d/[token]/page.tsx:44`, `src/app/r/[token]/page.tsx:39`, `api/public/docs/presign/route.ts:60` — an RM can mint a document-collection link for a colleague's client (submission overwrites KYC fields). _Fix:_ check subject visibility; add `tenant_id = info.tenantId` to every public lookup.
- **[SEC-18] [Medium] Stored XSS in the email-designer live preview** — `src/components/email-designer/EmailDesigner.tsx:493`, `src/lib/email-designer/render.ts:163` — unsandboxed same-origin `srcDoc`; raw `html` block. _Fix:_ `sandbox="allow-same-origin"` without scripts; sanitise.
- **[SEC-19] [Medium] Firm layout JSON can break out of `<script>`** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx:666` — `JSON.stringify` without escaping `<`; payload is `z.any()` from `POST /api/settings/ui-layout`. _Fix:_ escape `<` as `<`; real schema.
- **[SEC-20] [Medium] Public lead form can be used to make the firm message any phone/email** — `src/app/api/public/lead-forms/[slug]/route.ts` — per-IP in-memory cap only, no length caps; `lead.created` can fire welcome WhatsApp/email with attacker-chosen name. _Fix:_ verify contact before automations; captcha; global cap; length caps.
- **[SEC-21] [Medium] SSRF** — logo URLs fetched server-side with no private-IP check (`goal-pdf.ts:268`, `proposal-pdf.ts:782`, `valuation-pdf.ts:280`, `calculators/share-helpers.ts:53`; reachable unauthenticated via `/api/public/proposals/pdf`); workflow-rule webhook guard is string-only (`src/lib/workflow-rules/actions.ts:531`: `127.0.0.1.nip.io`, Docker hostnames pass). `images.remotePatterns: "**"` makes `/_next/image` an open proxy. _Fix:_ one shared resolve-and-pin guard; restrict logos to own storage.
- **[SEC-22] [Low] Rate limits are in-memory per process and keyed by first X-Forwarded-For** — `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` — reset on deploy, not shared across blue/green, spoofable behind another proxy. _Fix:_ Redis/DB counters.
- **[SEC-23] [Low] Public document upload has no size cap** — `api/public/docs/presign` — presigned PUT up to 5 GB; unsubmitted uploads never cleaned. _Fix:_ presigned POST with `content-length-range`; lifecycle rule.

### Security — tenant isolation & access inside a firm

_Cross-tenant isolation holds (non-BYPASSRLS role, per-transaction `set_config`, RLS on every `tenant_id` table, no injectable `sql.raw`). The gaps below are **inside** a firm: an RM reaching clients outside their book._

**Bugs**
- **[ACC-01] [High] Transactions export has no capability or RM scope** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/transactions/export/route.ts:29-56` — any signed-in user (even marketing) downloads up to 10,000 firm-wide transactions with names/folios/amounts. _Fix:_ `requireAny(investor.view.*)` + `investorScopeSql`.
- **[ACC-02] [High] Discovery has no client scope at all** — `src/app/api/discovery/route.ts:31-110`, `discovery/[id]/route.ts:30-80`, `discovery/[id]/share/route.ts:24` — list/get/patch/share any client's income, assets, goals; POST prefills from any client. _Fix:_ `loadViewableInvestor`/`loadViewableLead` on every verb; scope the list.
- **[ACC-03] [High] AI assistant sees the whole firm** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/ai/ask/route.ts:34,146,195`, `src/lib/ai/assistant/tools.ts:57,550`, `src/app/api/ai/client-brief/[id]/route.ts:22` — no capability check; returns top-10 clients & AUM by RM even without a model configured; tools search firm-wide. _Fix:_ capability + `investorScopeSql` in every tool.
- **[ACC-04] [High] Client merge search returns PAN/contacts firm-wide** — `src/app/api/investors/merge-search/route.ts:27`, `src/lib/investors/merged.ts:149` — _Fix:_ same gate as `/merge`, plus scope.
- **[ACC-05] [Medium] Insurance policy PATCH/DELETE skip the ownership check GET/renew make** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/route.ts:95-180`. _Fix:_ same `seesAll || soldBy || rmId` check.
- **[ACC-06] [Medium] Proposal DELETE skips the ownership check** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:220-250`.
- **[ACC-07] [Medium] Goals get/update/delete/create have no client scope** — `src/app/api/goals/[id]/route.ts:66-210`, `goals/route.ts:112`.
- **[ACC-08] [Medium] Signed risk-profile document downloadable for any client** — `src/app/api/risk-profiles/[id]/document/route.ts:31`.
- **[ACC-09] [Medium] Branch-locked head can merge another branch's client into theirs** — `src/app/api/investors/merge/route.ts:25,45` (+ `/merge/auto`, `/unmerge`).
- **[ACC-10] [Medium] Single activity readable by any web user** — `src/app/api/activities/[id]/route.ts:25-45` — call notes, phone, geo.
- **[ACC-11] [Medium] Calendar feed credential never expires and can't be revoked** — `src/app/api/calendar/feed/route.ts:30-60` — HMAC(secret, userId); offboarded users keep pulling. _Fix:_ require active user; versioned token.
- **[ACC-12] [Low] Other unscoped writes/reads** — `POST /api/sips` (`sips/route.ts:166`), `POST /api/leads/[id]/dispatch` (`:84`), `GET /api/review-meetings/[id]/calendar.ics` (`:51`), `GET /api/investors/kpis` (firm-wide totals to an RM).
- **[ACC-13] [Low] `withSystem` guard is not a real boundary** — `src/db/client.ts:171` — `ALLOW_SYSTEM_TX=1` is set for the web process; `withPlatformAdmin` has no guard. `content_library_assets` policy allows `tenant_id IS NULL` writes (`0120`); `platform_settings` has no RLS (`0085`). _Fix:_ separate DB role or lint restriction on importers.
- **[ACC-14] [Low] Super-admin support page builds SQL by string** — `src/app/super-admin/support/page.tsx:45`. Search boxes don't escape `%`/`_` in ILIKE.

### Messaging, consent & automation engine

**Bugs**
- **[MSG-01] [High] Ad-hoc sends ignore a blanket ("all") opt-out** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/comms/guarded-send.ts:57-70` — only the exact scope is checked; the consent API defaults to `scope:"all"`. Proposals, statements, acknowledgements still go to opted-out clients. _Fix:_ reuse `getConsent()` from `pipeline.ts`.
- **[MSG-02] [High] Two email routes skip every gate** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/wealth-story/route.ts:387`, `src/app/api/calculators/share/route.ts:98` — no consent, suppression, compliance, quota or `communications` row; calculators/share has no capability check and accepts any `to`. _Fix:_ route through `gateClientSend`/dispatch; add capability.
- **[MSG-03] [High] Unsubscribe links do nothing** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/email-designer/render.ts:454,506`, `src/lib/comms/email_renderer.tsx:230` — `unsubscribe_url` never set; no route; no `List-Unsubscribe` header. _Fix:_ signed per-recipient URL + public route + headers.
- **[MSG-04] [High] Inbound WhatsApp never matches existing clients, so STOP is lost** — `src/app/api/webhooks/vartify/route.ts:236-239` — raw `phone = from` compare (`9812345678` vs `919812345678`); duplicate "WhatsApp: 91…" leads created. _Fix:_ normalised last-10-digit match.
- **[MSG-05] [Medium] Leads have no consent model** — `src/db/schema/consents.ts` (`investor_id NOT NULL`) — lead opt-outs can't be recorded; marketing campaigns to lead segments end 100% `skipped_consent` (`campaign-dispatcher.ts:219`).
- **[MSG-06] [Medium] Merge values unescaped in email designer HTML** — `src/lib/email-designer/render.ts:55,164,327,337` — contradicts CLAUDE.md; hrefs not scheme-filtered.
- **[MSG-07] [Medium] Workflow loop can run forever** — `src/lib/workflow/schema.ts:429`, `actions/loop.ts` — `done` edge may route back into the loop. _Fix:_ restrict back-edges to the body handle; per-run step cap.
- **[MSG-08] [Medium] One workflow-runner run at a time for all tenants** — `src/workers/workflow-runner.ts:63-75` — 10k runs ≈ 83 min backlog for everyone. _Fix:_ batch + fair per-tenant claim + bounded concurrency.
- **[MSG-09] [Medium] Inbound-WhatsApp recipe runs with the wrong subject** — `src/workers/outbox-poller.ts:168` (both ternary branches `event.entity_id`).
- **[MSG-10] [Medium] Razorpay webhook applies events out of order** — `src/app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:87-140` — a late `activated` revives a cancelled subscription.
- **[MSG-11] [Low] Broken schedule triggers starve every other schedule** — `src/workers/master-scheduler.ts:23` — NULL/past `next_run_at` never advanced, `NULLS FIRST LIMIT 100`.
- **[MSG-12] [Low] Scheduled emails drain at one per minute platform-wide** — `src/workers/scheduled-comms.ts:22` (`BATCH = 1`).
- **[MSG-13] [Low] Rule-scheduler jobs lost on crash** — `src/workers/workflow-rules-scheduler.ts:20` — no lease/reaper.
- **[MSG-14] [Low] Reaper comment contradicts behaviour** — `src/workers/workflow-runner.ts:22-45` — re-queues expired runs; `http_request` side effects re-run.

### Billing, licensing, integrations & AI

**Bugs**
- **[BIL-01] [High] Forged email can import fake RTA data into any firm** — `src/workers/rta-inbox-poller.ts:308`, `src/lib/comms/imap.ts`, `src/lib/rta/classify-mailback.ts:183` — routed by sender-written headers; SPF/DKIM/SES verdicts ignored; ARN not checked. _Fix:_ per-firm unguessable `rta-<token>@` alias; require DKIM; reject foreign ARNs.
- **[BIL-02] [High] RTA inbox deletes mail it failed to import (new in d8f6f3b)** ✔ _verified_ — `src/workers/rta-inbox-poller.ts:399,496,640-644` — every outcome except `unroutable` is deleted; ingest errors are logged as "duplicate". _Fix:_ delete only on imported/duplicate/confirmation.
- **[BIL-03] [Medium] Forged "forwarding confirmation" puts a phishing link in Settings** — `rta-inbox-poller.ts:98,311,360`, `src/components/integrations/RtaMailbackCard.tsx:259`.
- **[BIL-04] [Medium] Lapsed firms keep writing, sending and using AI** — 272 of 408 mutation routes never call `assertActiveLicense`; `dispatch()` and workers never check subscription status.
- **[BIL-05] [Medium] Zip bombs can crash the shared worker** — `src/lib/rta/unzip.ts:38`.
- **[BIL-06] [Medium] Seat limit can be bypassed** — `api/employees/invite/route.ts:70` vs `api/employees/[id]/route.ts:83` — suspend → invite → reactivate; count-then-insert race.
- **[BIL-07] [Medium] No AI cost controls** — all `/api/ai/*` — no per-firm/user budget; chat defaults to the reasoning model with multi-round tools.
- **[BIL-08] [Medium] Email-designer test-send is an open relay** — `src/app/api/email-designer/test-send/route.ts:28` — any `to`, any HTML, no licence/rate limit.
- **[BIL-09] [Low] Firms can claim another firm's forwarding mailbox** — `setSourceAddresses` in `src/lib/rta/mailback-config.ts`.
- **[BIL-10] [Low] Client quota bypass** — `src/lib/workflow-rules/actions.ts:665` (`convert_lead`) and RTA import; count-then-insert race.
- **[BIL-11] [Low] Plan-swap race at onboarding; webhook doesn't check plan/amount** — `api/onboarding/select-plan/route.ts:59`, `webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:98`.
- **[BIL-12] [Low] S3 inbound mail retention not in Terraform** — `terraform/main.tf` — raw client mail kept forever; retry lists only first 200 keys.
- **[BIL-13] [Low] CSV formula injection** — `api/audit/export`, `attendance/export`, `portfolio-intelligence/export`, `dashboards/[id]/export` — use the `ops/export` escaper.
- **[BIL-14] [Low] AES-GCM auth tag length not enforced** — `src/lib/crypto.ts:55`.

### Money, tax & metrics correctness

**Bugs**
- **[NUM-01] [High] MF suitability gate bypassed by sending the proposal directly** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/send/route.ts:239-280` — no/provisional/expired risk profile only warns. _Fix:_ refuse unless `riskState === "current"`.
- **[NUM-02] [High] Tax-harvest planner and notional-gains report silently drop most lots** — `src/lib/tax/harvesting.ts:98-128`, `src/lib/tax/firm-gains.ts:101,172` — priced by `scheme_nav.scheme_code` only (RTA codes don't match). _Fix:_ `loadNavIndex` + `lookupNav(code, name)`.

- **[NUM-04] [Medium] Realised LTCG has no equity/debt split and a fixed ₹1.25L exemption** — `realized-gains.ts:55,168`, `firm-gains.ts:36,193` — post-Apr-2023 debt is slab; FY ≤ 2023-24 exemption was ₹1L; grandfathering not applied.
- **[NUM-05] [Medium] Capital-gains calculator uses pre-Budget-2024 rules** — `src/lib/calculators/capital-gains.ts:14-22,111,140` — 20% with indexation after 23-Jul-2024; CII stops at 2024; `>=` vs "more than".
- **[NUM-06] [Medium] Team Scorecard counts imports as new clients and uses this FY's targets for every period** — `src/lib/team/scorecard.ts:131,149`.
- **[NUM-07] [Medium] RM/Branch "New / Net SIP" disagree with Owner Dashboard** — `src/lib/dashboard/my/kpis.ts:150-165`, `pipeline.ts:462,511` — raw amounts, rejected included, null shown as 0.
- **[NUM-08] [Medium] Dividend reinvestment counted as fresh money in valuation/XIRR** — `src/lib/finance/valuation.ts:117-126`.
- **[NUM-09] [Medium] Goal recompute ignores SIP frequency** — `src/lib/goal-planning/recompute.ts:111`.
- **[NUM-10] [Low] Owner SIP health dates stops by `updated_at`; persistency counts completed as lapsed** — `src/lib/metrics/practice.ts:116-124`.
- **[NUM-11] [Low] Half-yearly SIPs scheduled monthly** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/sips/next-due.ts:96`.
- **[NUM-12] [Low] RTA rejection/brokerage amounts lose thousands separators** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/rta/parsers.ts:295,342-344` — `"1,250.00"` → 1.
- **[NUM-13] [Low] FD compounding over-accrues across 29 Feb** — `src/lib/fixed-deposits/terms.ts:153` (+₹204 on ₹10L).
- **[NUM-14] [Low] Purge and AI tool read only `related_investor_id`** — `src/lib/investors/purge.ts:92`, `api/investors/[id]/route.ts:618`, `src/lib/ai/assistant/tools.ts:581`.
