## Clients

### Clients: list, Client 360, create, edit, delete, health, statement
_Checked:_ /dashboard/investors (list, new, [id], [id]/edit, [id]/statement, [id]/review-report, [id]/mom, [id]/capital-gains); GET/POST /api/investors; GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/investors/[id]; /[id]/full, /restore, /avatar, /verify-contact, /portal-access, /statement/send|pdf, /notes; POST /api/investors/bulk; /recompute-tiers; /export; InvestorSidePeek, ClientDetailsForm, OnboardingWizard, VerifyContact, InvestorsTable bulk actions.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-01] [High] Client 360 page and Account Statement page skip the client-scope check** — `src/app/dashboard/investors/[id]/page.tsx:209-214`, `src/app/dashboard/investors/[id]/statement/page.tsx:57-61`. Both pages load the client by id and tenant only. Neither calls `loadViewableInvestor`/`investorScopeSql`, and neither calls `requireInvestorView`. The sibling pages (`capital-gains`, `mom`, `review-report`, `edit`) do call `assertInvestorAccess`. Scenario: a firm has `restrict_user_data_scope` switched on, and an RM pastes the URL of another RM's client (or a branch head opens a client from another branch). They see PAN (PanReveal), portfolio, communications, notes, tasks and meetings. The statement page also prints the full unmasked PAN. A marketing or team_member user with no investor capability can open both pages too. _Fix:_ load through `loadViewableInvestor(tx, session, id)` and call `notFound()` when it returns null, plus `requireInvestorView`. Validate the UUID on the statement page.
- **[CLI-02] [Medium] Creating a client with a duplicate PAN returns 500 instead of 409** — `src/app/api/investors/route.ts:320`. The handler matches `"investors_tenant_pan_unique"` only in `err.message`. Drizzle 0.45 wraps driver errors and puts the constraint on `err.cause`, which PATCH already handles (`[id]/route.ts:544`) and `handleApiError` documents. So the user sees "internal" with a reference id. Also, a client sitting in Trash still holds the PAN in the unique index, so re-creating them fails, and the message points at a record the user cannot find in any list. _Fix:_ check `err.cause?.constraint` / `err.cause?.message`. Pre-check the PAN, and when the clash is a trashed client, say "this client is in Trash — restore them".
- **[CLI-03] [High] Editing the risk category skips the risk-profile record and the MF suitability gate** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:100,394-450`; callers `ClientDetailsForm.tsx:222`, `OnboardingWizard.tsx:19`. PATCH writes `investors.risk_profile` directly. It does not create a `risk_profile_responses` row and does not touch `risk_profile_signed_at` or `risk_profile_provisional`. Create and import go through `applyRiskCategory`. Scenario: a client signed "conservative" 3 months ago, and the RM picks "very_aggressive" in the edit form. The opportunity gate (`lib/opportunities/journey.ts:160`) still sees a client-confirmed profile signed in the last 12 months, so an MF proposal proceeds on a category the client never confirmed. The Risk profile history shows no change. _Fix:_ route category changes through `applyRiskCategory({ provisional: true })`, or make the field read-only outside the questionnaire.
- **[CLI-04] [Medium] Changing an email or mobile keeps the old "verified" badge** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:436-450` (also `lib/investors/merge.ts:239-271`). PATCH and merge overrides never clear `email_verified_at`/`phone_verified_at` when the value changes. A newly typed, unconfirmed address shows as verified, and statements go to it on that trust. _Fix:_ null the matching `*_verified_at` (and `contact_verified_by`) whenever email, phone or WhatsApp changes.
- **[CLI-05] [Medium] The preview drawer breaks for colleagues' clients in shared-book firms** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:219-227`; `src/components/investors/InvestorSidePeek.tsx:65-70`. GET first passes `assertInvestorAccess`, which honours the per-tenant switch (off by default, so the whole book is visible). It then re-applies a hard rule: assigned-only users see only their own clients. An RM sees a colleague's client in the list, but the drawer gets 403. The drawer never checks `res.ok`, so it renders `{error}` as an empty record (no RM, branch or health). _Fix:_ drop the inline rule and rely on `assertInvestorAccess`. Handle `!res.ok` in the drawer.
- **[CLI-06] [Low] The JSON client APIs read tasks through one link only** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:239`, `src/app/api/investors/[id]/full/route.ts:54`. `[id]` uses `eq(tasks.subjectId, id)` with no `subject_type` and no `deleted_at` filter. `/full` uses only subject_type/subject_id. Tasks linked through `related_investor_id` (task form, meeting follow-ups, SIP defence) are missing from the mobile Client 360, and `[id]` also returns soft-deleted tasks. CLAUDE.md's two-link rule. _Fix:_ use `taskIsAbout()` plus `deleted_at IS NULL`.
- **[CLI-07] [Medium] POST /api/investors silently drops accepted fields** — `src/app/api/investors/route.ts:31-106` vs `206-277`. `status`, `alternatePhone`, `incomeRange`, `mandateStatus` and `mandateRefNo` pass validation but are never inserted. Status is always `"active"`. Initial health is scored with `kycStatus: "pending"` even when `kycStatus: "verified"` is sent. `familyId` is inserted with no check that the family is visible to the caller, no head/role handling and no family health recompute. _Fix:_ persist the fields or reject them. Score health from the real values. Validate `familyId` via `loadViewableFamily` and use the members route logic.
- **[CLI-08] [Medium] A new client lands in the creator's branch, not the assigned RM's branch** — `src/app/api/investors/route.ts:211`; `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:376`. `branchId: session.branchId` is used even when `rmId` assigns the client to an RM in another branch. Scenario: an owner at HQ adds a client for an RM in Branch B. Branch B's head (branch-locked) cannot see the client, and the Branch Head Dashboard counts it under HQ. _Fix:_ default `branch_id` to the assigned RM's `users.branch_id`.
- **[CLI-09] [Medium] "Recompute tiers" overwrites every manual tier and includes trashed clients** — `src/app/api/investors/recompute-tiers/route.ts:31-60`. Nothing in the codebase ever sets `service_tier_manual_override` (only this route reads it). So tiers set in the edit form, the bulk "tier" action or "Update a field → Tier" are wiped on the next recompute. Both the percentile query and the UPDATE also take `status='active'` rows that are in Trash (`deleted_at` is not filtered), so trashed AUM skews the cut-offs. _Fix:_ set the override (and `service_tier_locked_at`) on manual tier writes, and add `deleted_at IS NULL AND merged_into_id IS NULL`.
- **[CLI-10] [Low] Unvalidated date strings in PATCH and bulk cause 500s** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:150,179-186,404,426,433`; `src/app/api/investors/bulk/route.ts:165`. `new Date("31/12/2025")` gives an Invalid Date, and Drizzle's `toISOString` throws a RangeError, which returns 500. `dob`, `kycValidatedAt` and `ckycExpiryAt` go straight to `date` columns, so a Postgres cast error also returns 500. `name` may be `""`, and `notes` has no length cap. _Fix:_ use a zod `YYYY-MM-DD` refinement on every date field, `name.min(2).max(200)`, and `notes.max(…)`.
- **[CLI-11] [Low] The client page shows "Delete" to people who can't delete** — `src/app/dashboard/investors/[id]/page.tsx:508-515`. `RowActions` is rendered for everyone, but DELETE needs `investor.delete` (compliance or owner). Every RM who clicks it gets a 403 toast. _Fix:_ render it only when `can(…, "investor.delete")`.
- **[CLI-12] [Medium] A trashed client keeps a working investor-portal link** — `src/lib/portal/access.ts:48-66`, `src/app/portal/[token]/page.tsx:26`, `src/app/api/investors/[id]/portal-access/route.ts:17-33`. Neither soft delete nor bulk delete revokes `investor_portal_access`. The portal never checks `investors.deleted_at` or `merged_into_id`. `authorizeInvestor` also lets staff issue a new link for a client in Trash. Scenario: the firm deletes a client (for example on a DPDP erasure request). For up to 90 days the client can still open the portfolio and raise service requests. _Fix:_ revoke on trash, refuse issue for deleted clients, and join `investors` with `deleted_at IS NULL` in `resolvePortalAccess`.
- **[CLI-13] [Low] GET /api/investors ignores the plan field gate** — `src/app/api/investors/route.ts:157-163`. The query uses `.select()` with every column (PAN, Aadhaar last 4, bank details, …). The web list hides fields via `isFieldAllowed`, but the mobile/JSON list returns them all. `GET /api/investors/[id]` has the same issue. _Fix:_ project only the allowed columns.
- **[CLI-14] [Low] The setup wizard can't clear a field but reports success; VerifyContact ignores errors** — `src/components/investors/OnboardingWizard.tsx:71`, `src/components/investors/VerifyContact.tsx:24-33`. A cleared value becomes `undefined` and is dropped from the JSON, so the old value stays while the step shows as saved. VerifyContact never reads the response, so a 403 or 404 looks like it worked. _Fix:_ send `null` for cleared fields, and check `res.ok` and toast on failure.

**Improvements**
- `investor.bulk_export` is meaningless: export accepts `investor.view.assigned` too (`export/route.ts:74-77`), so with the data wall off any RM downloads the whole book's PII. Decide on the gate deliberately.
- The client page timeline merges sources with different caps (tasks 50, comms 50, activities 100, meetings 50). Older items from one source vanish while another's still show. Paginate the merged feed.
- `GET /api/investors/[id]/avatar` opens a separate transaction for `assertInvestorAccess` per image, so a 50-row list costs about 100 transactions. Batch, or sign avatars in the list loader.
- Search `ilike` doesn't escape `%`/`_` in user input (`list-filters.ts:92`).
- `/dashboard/investors/[id]/statement` uses a hand-rolled form and `btn-secondary` instead of `<Button>`.

### Client import (Excel/CSV)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/investors/import (chunked commit loop), POST /api/investors/import/parse, POST /api/investors/import/commit (add / update / both modes, buildUpdateSet, parseDate, resolveRm).

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-15] [High] Placeholder PANs from an import are auto-merged into one client** — `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:274,301,380-395,535-538` + `src/lib/investors/merge.ts:453-462`. Import stores any PAN cell verbatim ("NA", "N/A", "PANNOTAVBL", "0", "-") with `dedup_pan=false`. The update path likewise sets `pan` with no format check. "Client merged by system" groups live clients on `NULLIF(trim(pan),'') IS NOT NULL` with no format check. Scenario: a migrated book with "NA" in 300 rows. One click on auto-merge folds 299 different people (folios, transactions, SIPs, goals) into one survivor. The Duplicates page also flags them all as "Same PAN". _Fix:_ on import, keep a PAN only if it matches `/^[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]$/` (else null), and restrict `loadExactPanGroups` / the duplicate finder to valid PANs.
- **[CLI-16] [Medium] Update/Both modes can match trashed or merged-away records** — `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:163-170`. The match index loads every investor with no `deleted_at` or `merged_into_id` filter and no ORDER BY, and the first match wins. A PAN- or email-matched update can land on the retired duplicate or a trashed record instead of the live client. Add mode's "skip duplicates" also skips rows whose only match is in Trash. _Fix:_ filter to live clients (or rank live first).
- **[CLI-17] [Low] Error row numbers are wrong for files over 2,000 rows** — `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:201,…` (`rowIndex: i` is relative to the chunk); `src/app/dashboard/investors/import/page.tsx:116,333`. The page concatenates chunk errors without an offset, so an error on file row 2,500 shows as "Row 501". _Fix:_ send the chunk offset and add it, or offset on the client.
- **[CLI-18] [Low] The risk-profile column is ignored in Update mode** — `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:203-222` vs `237-241`. Only "both" calls `applyRiskCategory`, and `buildUpdateSet` has no riskProfile, so an Update-mode import with a mapped Risk Profile column silently changes nothing. _Fix:_ apply the same block in the update branch.
- **[CLI-19] [Low] Assigned-only RMs can import clients into another RM's book** — `src/app/api/investors/import/commit/route.ts:376,210`. The mapped "Assigned RM" cell is honoured for callers without `investor.edit.all`, on both insert and update. Single create pins such callers to themselves (`route.ts:232`). _Fix:_ ignore `rmId` unless `editsAll`.

**Improvements**
- Per-row errors return raw driver messages (`String(err.message)`, commit:445), which carry SQL and parameter dumps. Map them to readable reasons.
- `parseDate` treats a 4-digit cell such as "1990" as an Excel serial date (→ 1905). Require 5 digits or a plausible range.
- Updates write no per-client audit, no `investor.updated` outbox event and no health recompute, so KYC or status changes from a file don't refresh scores.
- `/api/investors/import/parse` has no capability check (any signed-in user can make the server parse 10 MB workbooks).

### Merge clients & duplicates
_Checked:_ /dashboard/merge-clients, /dashboard/duplicates, POST /api/investors/merge, /merge/preview, /merge/auto, /unmerge, GET /api/duplicates, POST /api/duplicates/ignore, lib/investors/merge.ts, MergeClientTool, DuplicateMerge, MergePicker, AutoMergeButton.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-20] [Medium] Merge leaves the duplicate's tasks, documents and share links on the retired record** — `src/lib/investors/merge.ts:73-85,180-223`. The generic pass moves only tables with an `investor_id` column. `tasks` (`related_investor_id`, `subject_id`), `compliance_documents` (`related_entity_id`), `share_tokens`/`financial_discoveries` (`subject_id`) and `investors.guardian_investor_id` are never re-pointed. After merging, the duplicate's open follow-ups and KYC documents are attached to a record that no list shows, and the survivor's Documents tab lacks them. _Fix:_ add these to the special-column pass, and record the moved ids so un-merge can reverse them.
- **[CLI-21] [Medium] Un-merge leaves some moved rows on the survivor and discards later edits** — `src/lib/investors/merge.ts:212-223`; `src/app/api/investors/unmerge/route.ts:98-110`. Lead referral/conversion links, the family head and `activities.subject_id` are moved but not recorded in `moved`, so after un-merge the loser's calls and notes stay on the survivor. Un-merge also writes the pre-merge snapshot over the survivor's name, phone, email and DOB unconditionally, wiping corrections made after the merge. _Fix:_ record the special-column moves, and restore a field only while it still equals the value the merge wrote.
- **[CLI-22] [Medium] In the Merge Client tool, one conflict choice applies to every duplicate** — `src/components/investors/MergeClientTool.tsx:397-410,191`. With several losers, all their radios bind to the same `takeFromLoser[field]` boolean, and that same map is sent for each loser in turn. Each merge overwrites the survivor, so it ends with the LAST loser's mobile or email, not the one the operator picked. `DuplicateMerge.tsx:256` does it correctly, per loser. _Fix:_ store the chosen loser id per field and send overrides only with that loser.
- **[CLI-23] [Low] The merge denylist uses stale table names** — `src/lib/investors/merge.ts:40`. `"risk_profiles"` and `"onboarding"` don't match `risk_profile_responses` or `onboarding_pipelines`. Risk responses move even though the preview lists them under "stays". `investor_portal_access` also moves, so the loser's portal link starts showing the survivor's portfolio (this matters when `allowPanMismatch` was used). _Fix:_ use the real names, and add `investor_portal_access` to the denylist or revoke it.
- **[CLI-24] [Low] No row lock in mergeInvestors** — `src/lib/investors/merge.ts:128-136`. Both records are read without `FOR UPDATE`. A manual A→B merge racing an auto-merge B→A can pass the `merged_into_id` check on both sides, leaving two retired records pointing at each other with zero AUM. _Fix:_ `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` both rows in id order.
- **[CLI-25] [Low] The mobile Duplicates feed lists trashed clients** — `src/app/api/duplicates/route.ts:33-35,52-58`. The client base filters `merged_into_id` but not `deleted_at`, unlike the web loader (`duplicate-clusters.ts:62`). Family clusters count deleted members. Mobile users are offered merges into Trash records. _Fix:_ add `deleted_at IS NULL`.

**Improvements**
- Auto-merge runs up to 200 merges in one transaction, each re-reading `information_schema` three times. Cache the discovery once per call.
- The family-clusters query in `/api/duplicates` is O(n²) (correlated `count(*)` per row). Use a window count.

### Families
_Checked:_ /dashboard/families (list, new, [id], unmapped, at-risk, settings); GET/POST /api/families; GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/families/[id]; POST/PATCH/DELETE /[id]/members; /[id]/full, /[id]/statement/pdf; /merge, /cleanup-empty, /suggestions, /settings, /relationship-types; AddMemberDialog, FamilySuggestionsPanel, investors/new?familyId.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-26] [Medium] Family merge leaves two heads and has no scope check** — `src/app/api/families/merge/route.ts:56-76`. The loser's members move with their `family_role` (`self`/`head`) intact while the survivor keeps its own primary, so the merged household shows two "Head" members. CLAUDE and the members route enforce exactly one. The route checks only owner/branch_head, so a branch head can merge (and hard-delete) families of another branch. _Fix:_ demote the loser's head to `other` (or run `makeHead`), and require `loadViewableFamily` on both families.
- **[CLI-27] [Low] Deleting or cleaning up a family strands trashed members** — `src/app/api/families/[id]/route.ts:220-223`; `src/app/api/families/cleanup-empty/route.ts:54`. A family whose only members are in Trash is deletable, and their `family_id` is cleared. Restoring the client later brings them back unmapped with no trace of the household. Cleanup treats merged-away members as live, unlike the single DELETE. _Fix:_ keep trashed members' links (or block deletion), and align the two "no clients" predicates.
- **[CLI-28] [Low] "Add client" from a family ignores the link failure** — `src/app/dashboard/investors/new/page.tsx:196-203`. After creating the client, the members POST response is never checked, so the user is sent to the family page even when linking failed (for example a 404 or 409). The new client is silently outside the family. _Fix:_ check `res.ok` and surface the error (or pass `familyId` to the create API once CLI-07 is fixed).
- **[CLI-29] [Low] Missing UUID validation and stale members in family health** — `src/app/api/families/[id]/members/route.ts:207-220` (an `investorId` query that isn't a UUID → Postgres cast error 500); `src/app/api/families/[id]/route.ts:163` (PATCH scores health over every member including deleted and merged ones, unlike `recomputeFamilyHealth`). _Fix:_ use a zod uuid, and call `recomputeFamilyHealth`.

**Improvements**
- `/api/families/suggestions` loads every live client in scope into memory on each open. Fine at 2k, heavy at 50k. Move the grouping to SQL.
- When a linked client's family is outside the viewer's family scope, suggestions treat the client as unlinked and propose a new family. The members POST would then move them out of a family the viewer can't see.
- POST /api/families links the head but scores health with `[]` members. Call `recomputeFamilyHealth(tx, id)` after the link.

### Trash (restore / purge)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/trash, GET /api/trash, POST /api/investors/[id]/restore, DELETE /api/investors/[id] (soft and `?purge=1`), POST /api/investors/bulk (`delete`, `purge`), lib/investors/purge.ts, daily-ops auto-purge, TrashActions.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-30] [High] Permanent erase does not require the client to be in Trash** — `src/lib/investors/purge.ts:56-63`; `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:563-636`; `src/app/api/investors/bulk/route.ts:81-108`. `purgeInvestorsChunk` and `DELETE ?purge=1` hard-delete any id, live or not. One `{action:"purge", ids:[…]}` call erases up to 5,000 LIVE clients with their folios, transactions and SIPs, skipping the 30-day recovery window the UI promises. The bulk audit row is written with `actorUserId: null` (purge.ts:68), so it records no actor. _Fix:_ add `isNotNull(investors.deletedAt)` to the purge scope (both paths) and pass the actor into the audit.
- **[CLI-31] [Medium] The daily auto-purge skips the cleanup and writes no audit** — `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:1300-1325`. The retention purge deletes `compliance_documents`, then `DELETE FROM investors` directly. It does not delete the non-FK tasks, review meetings, SIP rejections, onboarding pipelines, risk responses, activities, share tokens or discoveries. It does not detach `leads.converted/referred_by`, `families.primary_investor_id` or `guardian_investor_id`, and writes no `audit_log` row. `purge.ts` says a purged client "must not leave behind rows". It is also capped at 200 per day, so a 25k Trash takes months. _Fix:_ call `purgeInvestorsChunk(tx, tenantId, ids)` per tenant batch.
- **[CLI-32] [Medium] The Trash countdown can disagree with the real purge date** — `src/app/dashboard/trash/page.tsx:37,134,195`; `src/app/api/trash/route.ts:30`. Both use the constant `DEFAULT_SOFT_DELETE_RETENTION_DAYS` (30), while the worker purges by the platform-configurable `readSoftDeleteRetentionDays`. With retention set to 7 days, the page says "23 days left" for a client that is erased tomorrow. _Fix:_ read `readSoftDeleteRetentionDays(tx)` on both surfaces.

**Improvements**
- Soft delete (single and bulk) doesn't pause open tasks, review reminders or workflow runs about the client. Consider cancelling or flagging them, or excluding trashed clients from schedulers.
- Restore doesn't re-check the PAN unique index or family existence. Surface "restored but unmapped" when the family was deleted meanwhile.

### Client documents
_Checked:_ InvestorDocumentsSection; GET/POST/DELETE /api/investors/[id]/documents; GET /[id]/documents/download; /[id]/doc-completeness; /dashboard/documents (+ /upload); POST /api/documents/extract; storage helpers (presignedGetUrl, deleteObject).

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-33] [High] Client-supplied storage keys are never bound to the firm** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/documents/route.ts:42,107-120,165`; `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:93` (avatarUrl); `src/app/api/investors/[id]/avatar/route.ts:36`; `src/lib/storage/s3.ts:271-279`. POST documents and PATCH `avatarUrl` store whatever key the caller sends. Download and avatar then presign it, and document DELETE runs `deleteObject(key)`. With a known key (`firm/<slug>-<tenant8>/…`), a user can read or delete another firm's file. In local-storage mode `path.join(cwd, "node-local/uploads", key)` with `../../…` unlinks arbitrary server files. An `http(s)` avatarUrl turns the avatar endpoint into an open redirect. _Fix:_ accept only keys under the caller's tenant prefix, reject `..`/absolute paths, and resolve-and-verify in `deleteObject`. Allow-list avatar hosts.
- **[CLI-34] [Medium] Any uploader can permanently delete a client's KYC documents** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/documents/route.ts:141-176`. `document.upload` (RM, team_member, service team…) is enough to hard-delete the row and the S3 object, including files the client uploaded. There is no soft delete or retention hold, and the object is removed before the DB change commits. SEBI KYC records need multi-year retention. _Fix:_ soft-delete (supersede) and restrict hard delete to compliance. Delete the object only after the transaction commits.

**Improvements**
- When registration fails after a successful upload (`InvestorDocumentsSection.tsx:144-152`), an orphan object is left in S3. Clean it up or register server-side in one call.
- `/api/documents/extract` `toIso` accepts 31/02. Validate with `Date.UTC` round-trip.

### KYC
_Checked:_ POST /api/investors/[id]/kyc/verify, lib/kyc/verify.ts, KYC fields in PATCH / ClientDetailsForm / KycDataViewer.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-35] [High] "Verify KYC" downgrades verified clients whenever the provider fails** — `src/app/api/investors/[id]/kyc/verify/route.ts:29-35,64`; `src/lib/kyc/verify.ts:57,70,74-83`. `STATUS_MAP` maps `error` and `not_found` to `"pending"` and always writes `kycStatus`. In production with no provider configured, `verifyKyc` returns `status: "error"`, as it does on any provider HTTP or network failure. So clicking Verify on a KYC-verified client sets them to pending. That blocks the MF transaction gate and flips dashboards and health. _Fix:_ on `error` (and on a `pending` not returned by a real provider), leave `kycStatus` untouched and return the message.

**Improvements**
- The provider HTTP call runs inside the DB transaction, holding a pool connection for the provider latency. Call it first, then open the transaction.
- Verify doesn't recompute health, and it is allowed for any viewer with an edit capability even on another RM's client (PATCH forbids that). Align with PATCH's row check.
- A `MINOR:<guardian>:<name>` PAN key is sent to the provider as-is. Use `ownPan()`.

### Client onboarding pipelines (legacy API)
_Checked:_ GET/POST /api/onboarding, GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/onboarding/[id], POST /[id]/stage, OnboardingStageActions, retired /dashboard/onboarding pages.

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-36] [Medium] GET /api/onboarding exposes every client's PAN to any signed-in user** — `src/app/api/onboarding/route.ts:33-46`; `src/app/api/onboarding/[id]/route.ts:31-43`. There is no capability check and no client scope. A marketing or team_member session gets up to 500 pipelines with `investor_name` and full `investor_pan` firm-wide. The UI is retired, but the API is live. _Fix:_ require `onboarding.manage` (or investor view) and apply `investorScopeSql`, or remove the legacy routes.
- **[CLI-37] [Medium] Stage automation piles up duplicate open tasks** — `src/app/api/onboarding/[id]/stage/route.ts:147-161`. Each stage marked done inserts a new "Onboarding: <next stage>" task and never closes the previous one. Marking stages out of order re-creates a task for the same next stage, and abandoned or on-hold pipelines still advance and spawn tasks. Notifications link to `/dashboard/onboarding/<id>`, which now redirects to Leads. _Fix:_ close or replace the open onboarding task for the pipeline, refuse non-`in_progress` pipelines, and point links at the client page.

**Improvements**
- `POST /api/onboarding` returns an abandoned pipeline as "alreadyExisted", so onboarding can't restart. Racing creates hit the unique index and return 500. Use `ON CONFLICT`.
- DELETE writes an audit row even when the id doesn't exist.

### Custom fields & validation rules
_Checked:_ PATCH/POST /api/custom-fields/values, GET/POST /api/settings/custom-fields, PATCH/DELETE /[id], lib/custom-fields/values.ts, CustomFieldsCard; GET/POST/PUT /api/validation-rules, lib/validation/engine.ts, SalesOpsManager (validation tab).

**Bugs**
- **[CLI-38] [Medium] Validation rules are never enforced and can't be removed** — `src/app/api/validation-rules/route.ts:38-61`; `src/components/sales-ops/SalesOpsManager.tsx:60-65`. `validateRecord` is called only from the PUT "check" endpoint. No create or update path (investor POST/PATCH, import, lead save) calls it, so a "block" rule blocks nothing. There is also no PATCH or DELETE route, and `RuleRow.del` returns early for the validation tab, so a rule can be neither edited nor deleted. _Fix:_ call `validateRecord` in the investor and lead write paths (409 on block), and add a `[id]` route with toggle and delete.
- **[CLI-39] [Low] "Required" custom fields are client-side only, and value edits are unaudited** — `src/app/api/custom-fields/values/route.ts:44-73`; `src/lib/custom-fields/values.ts:14-52`. The API never enforces `required`. It accepts any string for `email`, `phone`, `date` and `url` types, replaces the whole bag without an `audit_log` row, and edits trashed clients. _Fix:_ enforce `required` and format per type server-side, and record an audit diff.

**Improvements**
- Custom-field definition delete leaves values in `custom_fields` jsonb until the next save silently drops them. Say so in the delete confirm, or keep orphaned values exportable.
- Custom-field GET exposes a firm's field definitions to every role. Harmless, but could follow `settings.*`.

---

## Leads & sales

### Leads (board/list, create/edit/delete, bulk, check-duplicate, convert, journey, import, documents, nurture)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/leads (+ /new, /[id], /import, /nurture); API /api/leads GET/POST, /[id] GET/PATCH/DELETE, /[id]/stage, /won, /convert, /call-outcome, /notes GET/POST, /pipeline PATCH, /full, /proposal, /avatar, /documents GET/POST/DELETE (+download), /capture-kyc GET/POST, /send-doc-checklist, /blueprint GET + /advance + /can-advance + /sync, /bulk, /check-duplicate, /import/parse + /commit; LeadsKanban drag/drop + Lost/Convert dialogs, LeadsTable sort + FloatingBulkBar, LeadEditPanel, GuidedStageActions navigate/call_api, lib/leads/{access,assign,normalize,stages,score}.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-01] [High] Lead Nurturing page is unscoped — every role sees the whole firm's leads with phones and notes** — `src/app/dashboard/leads/nurture/page.tsx:12-29` — only `requireSession()`; no `requireLeadView`, no `leadScopeSql`. Any RM, marketing, insurance or service user who opens the page sees up to 200 of the firm's leads, with phone numbers, RM names and `follow_up_notes`. The main Leads page had this exact leak and was fixed (comment at `leads/page.tsx:159-164`); this page, linked from the Leads "⋯" menu, was missed. _Fix:_ `requireLeadView(session)` + `AND ${leadScopeSql(session,'l')}`, and exclude `lost_reason='__deleted__'`.
- **[LEAD-02] [Medium] Nurture page filters on retired stage names, so most leads that need nurturing never show** — `src/app/dashboard/leads/nurture/page.tsx:21` — `stage IN ('follow_up','contacted','meeting_scheduled','meeting_done')`. The blueprint now writes `call_done`, `call_back_later`, `call_not_connected`, `contact_in_future`, `meeting_pending` and `proposal_sent`, so a lead parked as "Contact in future" or "Call back later" is missing from the page meant for it. A lead with `last_contact_at IS NULL` is never "dormant" either. _Fix:_ use `LEAD_PIPELINE` column members (lib/leads/stages.ts) and treat a NULL last contact as dormant.
- **[LEAD-03] [Medium] One bad row in a lead import silently rolls back the whole import, and the response still reports rows as inserted** — `src/app/api/leads/import/commit/route.ts:86-171` — every INSERT runs in one `withTenant` transaction with a JS try/catch per row and no SAVEPOINT. The first failing row aborts the Postgres transaction; for example, an `expected_business_amount` above numeric(16,2) such as `1e20`, or a NUL byte from an Excel export. Every later INSERT then fails with "current transaction is aborted", and the final COMMIT turns into a ROLLBACK. The user is told `inserted: N` while nothing was saved. _Fix:_ wrap each row in `tx.transaction()` (a savepoint) or validate all rows before inserting, and return 4xx if the commit didn't happen.
- **[LEAD-04] [Medium] Imported leads skip normalisation, so duplicate checks and matching never find them** — `src/app/api/leads/import/commit/route.ts:28-32,130-166` — the import never writes `phone_normalized` / `email_normalized`, and there is no trigger that fills them. `check-duplicate` and every phone/email match then miss imported leads. PAN is only upper-cased, never validated. The import has its own legacy whitelists, so `social_media`, `existing_client`, `google`, `cold_call` and `seminar` become `manual`, and canonical stages (`call_done`, `meeting_done`, `won_lead`) become `new`. It also emits no `lead.created` event, so assignment and workflows don't fire. _Fix:_ reuse `normalizePhoneLast10/normalizeEmail/normalizePan/normalizeLeadSource/normalizeLeadStage` and emit `lead.created`.
- **[LEAD-05] [Medium] A multi-hop "advance" skips the target stage's prerequisite and role gates** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/blueprint/advance/route.ts:266-327` — the walk is skipped only when the direct hop failed with `prereq|role|terminal`. When there is no direct transition (`no_transition`), every hop, including the final target, runs with `systemBypass: true`. Example: a lead at `call_done` POSTed with `{toStageKey:"documents_received"}` reaches Documents Received with no documents on file, which is exactly what the P1 gate in `engine.ts:305-316` exists to stop. _Fix:_ run `checkPrerequisites` and the role check for the final target before walking, or use `systemBypass` only on the intermediate hops.
- **[LEAD-06] [Medium] `force: true` (the "admin override") is accepted from any lead editor, including RMs** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/blueprint/advance/route.ts:51,225-233` — there is no role or capability check on `force`. Any RM can jump a lead or onboarding client to any stage (for example `kyc_validated`, `transaction_successful`, or `won_lead` without the product choice) and skip the SOP graph, role gates and prerequisites. _Fix:_ honour `force` only for a manager capability (e.g. `lead.edit.all` without branch-lock, or a dedicated cap); the kanban drop can use the normal path for adjacent moves.
- **[LEAD-07] [Medium] KYC, UCC and mandate details only reach the client record on a single-hop advance** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/blueprint/advance/route.ts:225-233,243-263,329-331` — the force path returns before any side effects. Dragging a card on the Clients Kanban (`InvestorsKanban.tsx:148-158`, always `force:true`) to "KYC Validated" leaves `investors.kyc_status` as pending, while the opportunity gates and dashboards read `kyc_status`. On a multi-hop walk to `ucc_activated` or `mandate_activated`, the `uccNumber` and mandate fields the user typed are dropped. _Fix:_ run the store* side effects after any successful move to that target, whichever path was taken.
- **[LEAD-08] [Medium] A lead takes the creator's branch, not its owner's, so branch heads miss leads they own** — `src/app/api/leads/route.ts:300-305`, `src/app/api/leads/[id]/route.ts:86`, `src/app/api/leads/bulk/route.ts:72`, `src/app/api/public/lead-forms/[slug]/route.ts:199-216`, `import/commit/route.ts:140` — on desktop, `branch_id = session.branchId` even when the lead is auto- or manually assigned to an RM in another branch. On reassignment, only mobile updates `branchId`. Public-form leads get `branch_id NULL`, and imported leads get the importer's branch. Branch-head visibility (`isBranchScoped` → `leads.branch_id`) then hides a branch's own RMs' leads and shows other branches' leads. _Fix:_ always derive `branch_id` from the assigned owner on create, PATCH, bulk assign and public form.
- **[LEAD-09] [Medium] Bulk lead actions ignore the branch lock and accept any lead id** — `src/app/api/leads/bulk/route.ts:43,112-116` — the route only checks `lead.edit.all`, and `branch_head` holds that capability (`rbac.ts:216`). The update is `WHERE id IN (ids)` with no `leadScopeSql`, so a branch head can bulk reassign, re-stage or delete leads in any branch, and converted leads too (a bulk "stage" on a converted lead moves an onboarding client back to New). The assign target isn't checked for `status='active'`. _Fix:_ add `AND ${leadScopeSql(session)} AND converted_investor_id IS NULL` and check that the assignee is active and inside the caller's branch.
- **[LEAD-10] [Medium] Stage edits outside the blueprint desynchronise the journey, and an unknown stage silently resets a lead to New** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/route.ts:102-105`, `src/app/api/leads/[id]/stage/route.ts:39-41`, `src/app/api/leads/bulk/route.ts:81-89` — PATCH, /stage and bulk "stage" write `leads.stage` directly. `lead_blueprint_instances.current_stage_key`, stage history and on-enter automations stay at the old stage, and the next blueprint move starts from the wrong place. `normalizeLeadStage()` falls back to `"new"`, so PATCH `{stage:"won"}` or `/stage {stage:"won"}` passes the CANONICAL check and quietly resets a lead to New (the advance route guards against this at lines 199-203; these routes don't). Bulk also lets a not-yet-converted lead move into onboarding stages (`kyc_validated`…). _Fix:_ route every stage change through `transitionTo`, reject unknown keys with 400, and limit bulk to lead-phase stages.
- **[LEAD-11] [Medium] Two concurrent "Convert" clicks create two client records** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/convert/route.ts:76-80,97-121` — the `convertedInvestorId` check reads the lead without `FOR UPDATE`. Two requests (a double click, the kanban dialog plus LeadActions, or a retry) both see NULL and both insert an investor. Only a supplied PAN hits the unique index; a PAN-less conversion, which is allowed, duplicates the client. _Fix:_ `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the lead (or `UPDATE leads SET converted_investor_id=… WHERE converted_investor_id IS NULL RETURNING`) before inserting the investor.
- **[LEAD-12] [Medium] Converting a lead doesn't move its tasks, activities or cadence enrolments to the new client** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/convert/route.ts:148-245` — the carryover moves risk profiles, proposals, documents, comms, meetings and share tokens, but leaves `tasks` (`subject_type='lead'` / `related_lead_id`), `activities` (`subject_type='lead'`) and `cadence_enrollments`. Open follow-ups stay attached to the lead and never appear on the client page, the RM desk's client view or the client's health/contact facts. _Fix:_ set `related_investor_id` and `subject_type/subject_id` on the lead's open tasks, and repoint activities and enrolments in the same savepoint.
- **[LEAD-13] [Medium] Rule-based "convert lead" never moves the lead's meetings** — `src/lib/workflow-rules/actions.ts:750-756` — the meetings carryover is `WHERE investor_id = ${leadId}`; it should be `lead_id = leadId`, as the manual route has (`convert/route.ts:204-211`). The rule action also doesn't carry `kycData`, writes no audit_log row, and has no savepoint, so a failed step aborts the rule's whole transaction. _Fix:_ share one `convertLead()` helper between the route and the rule action.
- **[LEAD-14] [Medium] After conversion, the client's onboarding journey is controlled by the old lead owner, not the client's RM** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/blueprint/advance/route.ts:208`, `capture-kyc/route.ts:94,140`, `InvestorsKanban.tsx:148` — onboarding moves go through `/api/leads/[leadId]/…` with `loadEditableLead`, which checks `leads.owner_id`. When the RM is changed at conversion (`body.rmId`) or the client is later reassigned, the client's actual RM gets a 403 on the Clients Kanban, the stage bar and KYC capture. The former lead owner can still rewrite the client's PAN, bank accounts and nominees through `capture-kyc` POST, with no `investor.edit` check and no audit_log row. _Fix:_ for a converted lead, authorise with `loadViewableInvestor` + `investor.edit.*` on `converted_investor_id`, and audit KYC master changes.
- **[LEAD-15] [Medium] The duplicate check shows any firm lead's name, phone, email and stage to any signed-in user** — `src/app/api/leads/check-duplicate/route.ts:35-86` — the client half is carefully scoped ("outside the book" hides name and RM), but the lead half has no capability check, no `leadScopeSql`, and no `__deleted__` filter. An RM typing a number sees another RM's or another branch's lead in full. If `phone` is given with no digits (e.g. `"abc"`), `conds` is empty and `WHERE ()` is a SQL error (500). _Fix:_ `requireLeadView`, and report out-of-scope lead matches as "already a lead in another book" with no details; return early when `conds` is empty.
- **[LEAD-16] [Medium] Lead attachments and avatars accept any storage key, so a user can get a signed download link for any stored object** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/documents/route.ts:35-40,122-134` → `documents/download/route.ts:60`; `src/app/api/leads/route.ts:122` (`avatarUrl`) → `leads/[id]/avatar/route.ts:31` — `storageKey` / `avatarUrl` are stored verbatim (only a length cap), then presigned for download. A user who knows or guesses a key (`firm/<slug>-<tenant8>/<uploader>/<client>/<doctype>/<ts>-<name>`, or `tenants/<id>/…`) can register it on a lead they own and download that object, including another branch's or another firm's KYC file. Avatar URLs that start with `https://` are also open redirects. _Fix:_ accept only keys under this tenant's prefix that were issued by this session's upload (or look up the key in the upload ledger); reject absolute URLs.
- **[LEAD-17] [Medium] The leads list API (mobile app) drops every lead in Proposal Sent** — `src/app/api/leads/route.ts:159-164,195` — the route keeps its own `LEAD_PHASE_STAGES` without `proposal_sent` (migration 0222) or the other folded aliases (`proposal_shared`, `risk_profiled`…). The mobile app's lead list therefore hides the leads closest to winning, and its `total` disagrees with the web board. _Fix:_ use `LEAD_PIPELINE_STAGE_VALUES` and `leadWhere` from lib (as the web page does).
- **[LEAD-18] [Medium] Journey "Sync" marks a lead Won from a draft proposal or just a risk profile** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/blueprint/sync/route.ts:89-113` — a proposal in `draft/ready`, a `sent/viewed` proposal, or a risk profile on file all advance the lead to `won_lead`. Proposal Sent (`proposal_sent`) is never used. One click on the stage bar's Sync inflates Won counts and conversion rates on every dashboard. The `transitionTo` result is ignored, so a refused move still reports `advancedTo`. _Fix:_ draft or risk profile → `meeting_done`, sent → `proposal_sent`, accepted → `won_lead`; return the real outcome.
- **[LEAD-19] [Medium] "Send doc checklist" can message any lead in the firm, while ops and back office are blocked** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/send-doc-checklist/route.ts:31-43` + `lib/onboarding/send-doc-checklist.ts:60-62` — the route requires exactly `lead.edit.assigned` and loads the lead by id with no ownership check. Any RM can trigger an email or WhatsApp to another RM's lead, while back office, ops and marketing (who hold `lead.edit.all`) get a 403. _Fix:_ `requireLeadEdit` + `loadEditableLead` before sending.
- **[LEAD-20] [Medium] "Update Client Information" on the journey opens a 404** — `src/lib/blueprints/stage-actions.ts:757` + `src/components/leads/GuidedStageActions.tsx:538-539` — `hrefPattern "/dashboard/investors/{id}"` is filled with the LEAD id (the blueprint subject), so the button goes to `/dashboard/investors/<leadId>`, which calls `notFound()` (`investors/[id]/page.tsx:394`). The business-entries links work only because that page resolves a lead id itself. _Fix:_ pass `convertedInvestorId` to GuidedStageActions and substitute it for investor routes.
- **[LEAD-21] [Medium] Auto-assignment counts lost, deleted and converted leads as an RM's open workload** — `src/lib/leads/assign.ts:52` (also used by the public form) — `stage NOT IN ('converted','lost','cold','not_interested')` uses retired names. Canonical lost is `not_qualified`, deleted leads carry `__deleted__`, and converted leads move on to `request_documents…`. An RM who closed many leads looks "most loaded" and gets no new leads. `assignment/engine.ts:55-56` gets this right. _Fix:_ count `converted_investor_id IS NULL AND stage <> 'not_qualified' AND lost_reason IS DISTINCT FROM '__deleted__'`.
- **[LEAD-22] [Low] The list view's "Created" column sort doesn't work** — `src/components/leads/LeadsTable.tsx:107-113` — clicking "Created" *deletes* `sort`, so the server falls back to `score` (`leads/page.tsx:133`). Clicking it never sorts by date, and the direction toggle compares against `"recent"`, which is never set. _Fix:_ set `sort=recent` for Created and delete `sort` for Score.
- **[LEAD-23] [Low] The "All open" saved view leaves out Proposal Sent and includes Won** — `src/app/dashboard/leads/page.tsx:429` — `stage=new,call_done,meeting_scheduled,meeting_done,won_lead`. _Fix:_ `…,meeting_done,proposal_sent`.
- **[LEAD-24] [Low] The lead edit panel can't clear a field, and never saves a lost reason** — `src/components/leads/LeadEditPanel.tsx:73-84` — empty phone, city, notes, amount and follow-up date are sent as `undefined`, so clearing a next follow-up date "saves" but keeps the old date. `lead.stage === "lost"` is never true (lost is `not_qualified`), so the lost-reason field never renders or saves. Editing `followUpNotes` lets a user overwrite the whole timestamped activity history. _Fix:_ send `null`/`""` for cleared fields, check `leadPipelineColumn(stage)?.key === 'not_qualified'`, and make the notes log append-only.
- **[LEAD-25] [Low] Deleting a lead leaves its open follow-ups behind** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/route.ts:172-203`, `bulk/route.ts:96-104` — after a soft delete, the lead's open tasks keep showing on the RM's desk, calendar (see LEAD-49) and NBA, and active cadence enrolments stay active. A converted lead can also be "deleted", which re-stages it to `not_qualified`. _Fix:_ cancel open lead tasks and enrolments on delete, and refuse delete when `converted_investor_id` is set.
- **[LEAD-26] [Low] The lead page and the mobile detail API find the lead's tasks through only one of the two task links** — `src/app/dashboard/leads/[id]/page.tsx:100-105`, `src/app/api/leads/[id]/full/route.ts:39` — both filter `subject_type='lead' AND subject_id=id`, so a task tied to the lead only by `related_lead_id` (task form with both a client and a lead, meeting action items when the meeting has a client) is missing from the timeline and "open tasks". `/full` also counts cancelled tasks as open. _Fix:_ use `taskLead()` / `taskIsAbout` from `lib/dashboard/my/window.ts`.
- **[LEAD-27] [Low] Lead date filters break on bad input or use UTC days** — `src/app/api/leads/[id]/route.ts:106-108`, `bulk/route.ts:79`, `leads/route.ts:203,208`, `leads/page.tsx:227` — `new Date("garbage")` writes Invalid Date (Postgres error → 500). A non-uuid `owner=` makes the `::uuid` cast fail (500). The "Follow-up today" chip uses UTC `current_date` while `followup_due` uses IST, so between midnight and 05:30 IST it shows yesterday's follow-ups. _Fix:_ validate with zod datetime / uuid and use `(… AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata')::date`.

**Improvements**
- `/api/leads/[id]/won`: validate `primaryProduct ∈ products` and `LEAD_PRODUCTS`; an unknown product silently becomes the MF pipeline.
- Lead notes and call-outcome entries are stamped with `toISOString()` (UTC) in `follow_up_notes`; stamp in IST.
- Kanban "Won" drop converts the lead immediately through `/convert`, skipping the product choice (`/won`); consider opening ProductInterestDialog first so `pipeline` is set.
- The Leads "Owner (RM)" filter lists every active user in the firm, even for a branch-locked head; limit it to the viewer's scope.
- `/api/leads/[id]` PATCH writes an audit row and an outbox event even for an empty patch; skip them when nothing changed.
- Import runs up to 10k sequential INSERTs in one transaction; batch with multi-row INSERT.

### Opportunities
_Checked:_ /dashboard/opportunities (board/list, KPIs, filters), /dashboard/opportunities/[id]; API /api/investors/[id]/opportunities GET/POST, /api/opportunities/[id] GET/PATCH, /advance, /sync; lib/opportunities/{access,journey,board,products,stages}; OpportunitiesPanel, OpportunityWorkspace.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-28] [Medium] Any newly recorded insurance policy counts as the result of any insurance opportunity** — `src/lib/opportunities/journey.ts:121-133` — the policy fact picks the client's newest policy created after the opportunity opened, without matching `policy_type` to the product. A motor policy recorded for a client with an open Health or Life opportunity skips its proposal-form and underwriting stages, and once that policy is active, closes the opportunity as **Won**. The Cross-sell card then marks health/life as "held with us". _Fix:_ filter policies by the product's line (life / health / general), as `lib/insurance/book.ts` derives it.
- **[LEAD-29] [Medium] Changing an opportunity's owner leaves the follow-up task with the old owner** — `src/app/api/opportunities/[id]/route.ts:90,98-100` + `src/lib/opportunities/journey.ts:381-389` — PATCH `ownerId` updates the row only. `setFollowUp` moves the open task's `due_at`, not its `assignee_id`. The new owner never sees the follow-up on their worklist, and the old owner keeps working an opportunity that is no longer theirs. _Fix:_ when `ownerId` changes, reassign the opportunity's open `meta.opportunityId` tasks (and notify `task.assigned`).
- **[LEAD-30] [Low] Closing an opportunity marks its open tasks as completed** — `src/lib/opportunities/journey.ts:514-518` — on a loss or win, the open follow-ups get `status='completed', completed_at=now()`, so a lost opportunity's untouched follow-up counts as work done in task-completion and productivity metrics. _Fix:_ use `cancelled` (with a remark) unless the task was actually done.
- **[LEAD-31] [Low] Two quick "New opportunity" clicks for the same product return a 500** — `src/lib/opportunities/journey.ts:327-334` — check-then-insert. The partial unique index `client_opps_one_open_per_product` stops the duplicate row, but the second click gets a 500 instead of the documented `200 {created:false}`. _Fix:_ `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING`, then re-select the existing row.
- **[LEAD-32] [Low] Opportunities headline tiles don't match the lists they link to** — `src/app/dashboard/opportunities/page.tsx` (tile `status:"won"`) — "Won this month" drills to *all-time* Won. "Open … expected" sums monthly SIP amounts and lump sums as one rupee figure. `totalCount={rows.length}` caps silently at 500. _Fix:_ add a `won_month` filter, split SIP and lumpsum value, and say "showing first 500".

**Improvements**
- `listOpportunities` runs about 8 queries per opportunity (instance, history, stage row, loadFacts' 4–5 queries) for up to 50 rows on every client-page load, and `ensureInstanceFor` writes inside GET. Batch the history and facts queries, and create instances at creation only.
- OpportunitiesPanel fires a parallel `/sync` POST for every stale opportunity on mount; one batched server-side sync would avoid N transactions.
- PATCH lets any work-capable user make someone who cannot see the client the owner; check the new owner's access to the client.
- Validate that `revisitOn` is today or later (follow-up dates are, revisit dates are not).

### Lead capture forms (builder + public submit)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/settings/lead-forms + LeadFormsAdmin, /lf/[slug] + LeadCaptureFormClient, API /api/lead-forms GET/POST, /api/public/lead-forms/[slug] GET/POST.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-33] [Medium] A lead-capture form can't be edited, switched off or deleted once created** — `src/app/api/lead-forms/route.ts` (GET/POST only), `src/components/lead-forms/LeadFormsAdmin.tsx:31` — there is no PATCH or DELETE route and the admin screen is create-only, though the schema has `enabled`, `fields`, `defaultRmId` and `rateLimitPerHour`. A form whose link leaked or is being spammed cannot be retired without a DB edit, and its fields can never be changed. _Fix:_ add PATCH (enabled, fields, branding, default RM) and DELETE/disable, with an audit_log row.
- **[LEAD-34] [Medium] Web-form leads bypass assignment rules and land in no branch** — `src/app/api/public/lead-forms/[slug]/route.ts:170-216` — the route calls `pickRmForNewLead` directly, so Assignment Rules for source `website` (the most common rule) never apply. `branch_id` is never set, so branch heads never see web leads (see LEAD-08). `defaultRmId` is stored at creation with no check that the user is in the tenant or active (`lead-forms/route.ts:92`), so a deactivated default RM keeps receiving leads. _Fix:_ call `pickAssignee` first, set `branch_id` from the chosen owner, and validate `defaultRmId` (and skip it if inactive).

**Improvements**
- No audit_log row on form creation.
- The public form doesn't validate email format, and dumps form-specific answers into `notes` rather than `custom_fields`.

### Referrals
_Checked:_ /dashboard/referrals, API /api/referrals GET.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-35] [High] Referral tracking shows the whole firm's clients, phones, emails and AUM to any user** — `src/app/dashboard/referrals/page.tsx:18-82`, `src/app/api/referrals/route.ts:23-84` — the page has no capability check at all, and both the page and the API ignore `investorScopeSql` / `leadScopeSql`. Any RM, or a marketing user with no investment-data access, sees every referrer client firm-wide with phone and email, converted AUM, and the "advocates" list: the firm's eight highest-AUM clients with phone numbers. _Fix:_ `requireInvestorView` + `investorScopeSql(tx, session, 'i')` on all three queries, and lead scope on `l`.
- **[LEAD-36] [Medium] Referral "lost" and "in pipeline" counts use retired stage names** — `src/app/dashboard/referrals/page.tsx:41-46`, `api/referrals/route.ts:44-47` — `stage IN ('lost','not_interested')` misses canonical `not_qualified`. Lost referrals therefore count as "in pipeline", "lost" is almost always 0, and soft-deleted leads (`__deleted__`) are counted as referrals. _Fix:_ use `leadPipelineColumn`/`leadWhere` (lost = `not_qualified` + aliases; exclude deleted).

**Improvements**
- `grid-cols-4` stat row has no responsive breakpoint (overflows at 375px); use `StatGrid`.

### Assignment rules, cadences, macros (sales-ops)
_Checked:_ SalesOpsManager (assignment, macros, validation, cadences drawers + delete), RecordAutomations; API /api/assignment-rules GET/POST, /[id] PATCH/DELETE, /api/cadences GET/POST, /[id]/enroll, /run-due, /api/macros/[id]/run; lib/assignment/engine, lib/cadences/runner.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-37] [High] Cadences never run** ✔ _verified_ — `src/lib/cadences/runner.ts:12` is only called from `POST /api/cadences/run-due` (`src/app/api/cadences/run-due/route.ts:14`), and no worker in `src/workers/` and no UI calls that endpoint. Enrolling a lead from RecordAutomations succeeds and shows as active, but no step (task, reminder or touch) is ever created. _Fix:_ add a repeatable BullMQ job (per tenant, `withSystem` → `withTenant`) calling `advanceDueCadences`.
- **[LEAD-38] [Medium] Cadence enrolment and macro runs work on any lead or client in the firm, and macros set stages outside the journey** — `src/app/api/cadences/[id]/enroll/route.ts:160-167`, `src/app/api/macros/[id]/run/route.ts:~40-59` — the subject is looked up by id only (no `loadEditableLead` / `loadViewableInvestor`), so an RM can enroll or run a macro on another RM's lead or client. Deleted and converted leads are accepted too. A macro "set stage" step runs `UPDATE leads SET stage = ${c.stage}` with an unvalidated string, which skips the blueprint (same desync as LEAD-10). _Fix:_ authorise the subject with the scoped loaders, and run stage steps through `transitionTo` with a canonical-key check.
- **[LEAD-39] [Medium] Assignment rules keep routing leads to deactivated users** — `src/lib/assignment/engine.ts:36-47`, `src/app/api/assignment-rules/route.ts:18,45` — `targetUserIds` are not checked to be active or in the tenant at creation, and `pickAssignee` never re-checks status. When an RM leaves, a round-robin, "specific" or load-balanced rule still assigns new leads to them, and nobody active sees those leads. _Fix:_ filter targets to `users.status='active'` at pick time (skip the rule if none are left), and validate at creation.

**Improvements**
- Cadences have no update or delete endpoint (`isActive` can never be turned off). Enrolments are never stopped when the lead converts, is lost or is deleted. The `log_touch` step writes an activity as though a touch happened, which inflates "calls/touches logged".
- Cadence tasks go to `enrolled_by_user_id`, not the record's current owner or RM.
- Assignment-rule PATCH/DELETE write no audit_log row. SalesOpsManager's delete ignores the response, so a 403 looks like success. `isManager` checks role names, not capabilities, and a branch head's rules apply firm-wide.
- Round-robin `rr_cursor` is read and written without a lock; concurrent creates pick the same RM.

### Next best action
_Checked:_ /dashboard/next-best-action (filters, focus mode, row actions), API /api/nba/draft, lib/nba/engine.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-40] [Medium] Branch heads and compliance get the whole firm's NBA worklist** — `src/app/dashboard/next-best-action/page.tsx:36-43` + `src/lib/nba/engine.ts:129-132,217,325` — `canFirm = dashboard.view.owner`, which `rbac.ts:319` grants to `branch_head` and `compliance`. A branch-locked head therefore gets `firmWide=true`: every branch's at-risk clients, SIP failures, KYC breaks and leads, with phone numbers and AUM. The user picker lists every user in the firm. _Fix:_ resolve scope with `resolveDataAccess` / `isBranchScoped` (branch → `i.branch_id` / `l.branch_id`), as the dashboards do.

**Improvements**
- `/api/nba/draft` has no capability gate (every signed-in role can spend AI tokens); require a client or lead view capability.
- Lead amounts are parsed by stripping non-digits from `intended_amount` ("1.5 Cr" → ₹1.5); prefer `expected_business_amount`.

### Activities
_Checked:_ /dashboard/activities, API /api/activities GET/POST, /api/activities/[id] GET/PATCH/DELETE, ActivityLogger (log + check-in).

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-41] [Medium] Branch heads and compliance can see, edit and delete everyone's activity logs firm-wide** — `src/app/api/activities/route.ts:58,79`, `src/app/dashboard/activities/page.tsx:33-45`, `src/app/api/activities/[id]/route.ts:65-75,117-127` — `dashboard.view.owner` (branch_head, compliance) is treated as "manager". It lifts the own-logged filter across all branches, and allows PATCH/DELETE of any user's call and visit records. Compliance, a read role, can delete the interaction trail it is meant to audit, and a branch head can rewrite another branch's logs. _Fix:_ branch-scope by `activities.branch_id` for a branch-locked head, and limit edit/delete to the author plus owner.
- **[LEAD-42] [Medium] Logging a call or visit against a lead doesn't mark the lead as contacted** — `src/app/api/activities/route.ts:180-186` — only `investor` subjects get `last_contact_at`; a `lead` activity updates nothing, and `nextFollowupAt` is not copied to `leads.next_follow_up_at`. A lead called from the Activities page, the NBA "Log" button or a field check-in stays "stuck 14+ days", "follow-up overdue" and low-scored. The lead page's own Log button (`/notes`) does update these. _Fix:_ for `subjectType='lead'`, update `last_contact_at`, `next_follow_up_at` and the score (and call `autoAdvance("call.logged")` for an answered call).

**Improvements**
- KPI `calls_today` / `followups_due` use UTC `current_date`; an invalid `to=` makes the `::date` cast fail (500).
- Family-subject activities have no access check beyond tenant.

### Discovery (UI flow)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/discovery (+ /[id]), NewDiscoveryDialog, DiscoveryEditor, API /api/discovery GET/POST, /[id] GET/PATCH, /[id]/share POST. (In-firm scope gaps are already reported.)

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-43] [Low] Discovery accepts unchecked input and has no audit or delete** — `src/app/api/discovery/route.ts:72-97`, `src/app/api/discovery/[id]/route.ts:128-185`, `share/route.ts:222` — POST creates a discovery for a `subjectId` that doesn't exist (title NULL, no 404). PATCH takes `data: z.any()` unbounded, can set a client-`submitted` discovery back to `draft` and overwrite the client's answers, and writes no audit_log row. `share` uses `Body.parse` (a ZodError becomes a 500). There is no DELETE. _Fix:_ 404 a missing subject, cap and validate `data`, make status changes forward-only, add audit, and use `safeParse`.

### Calendar
_Checked:_ /dashboard/calendar, InteractiveCalendar, API /api/calendar GET, /api/calendar/feed (ICS), lib/calendar/items.

**Bugs**
- **[LEAD-44] [Medium] The calendar and its iCal feed show follow-ups for lost and deleted leads** — `src/lib/calendar/items.ts:92` — `stage NOT IN ('lost')` only. Canonical lost is `not_qualified` and soft-deleted leads carry `lost_reason='__deleted__'`, so their stale `next_follow_up_at` keeps appearing on the in-app calendar and every RM's subscribed phone calendar. Branch heads also see firm-wide lead follow-ups and reviews (`leadsAll` = `lead.view.all`, which branch_head holds), ignoring the branch lock. _Fix:_ use `leadOpenSql` + the deleted filter, and branch-scope via `isBranchScoped`.

**Improvements**
- `?month=abc` produces `NaN-NaN-01` bounds (SQL error 500); validate `YYYY-MM`.
- Date buckets use `due_at::date` in the DB session timezone (UTC); an IST 00:00–05:30 item lands on the previous day.
- Each source is silently capped (`LIMIT 500/200`); merged and closed clients' reviews are not excluded.

## Tasks, service & meetings

### Tasks
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/tasks` (list, kanban, tabs, filters, bulk bar, "select all across pages"), `/dashboard/tasks/[id]` (TaskDetailDrawer page variant: save, quick reschedule, complete, cancel, approval, checklist, reminder), `/dashboard/tasks/new`; API `GET/POST /api/tasks`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/tasks/[id]`, `POST /api/tasks/[id]/restore`, `GET/POST comments`, `DELETE comments/[commentId]`, `GET/POST/DELETE watchers`, `GET/POST attachments`, `DELETE attachments/[attId]`, `GET …/download`, `GET/POST time`, `GET related`, `GET /api/tasks/kpis`, `GET /api/tasks/ids`, `POST /api/tasks/bulk`; `lib/tasks/recurrence.ts`, `lib/tasks/watchers.ts`, `lib/tasks/kpis.ts`, sidebar overdue badge (`dashboard/layout.tsx`), `workers/task-reminder-scanner.ts` (reset semantics only).

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-01] [High] "Select all N across pages" selects a different set than the list shows — bulk actions hit the wrong tasks** — `src/app/api/tasks/ids/route.ts:44-74` vs `src/app/dashboard/tasks/page.tsx:121-171` (called from `TaskList.tsx:190`). The ids route ignores `tab` (overdue/today/upcoming/completed), `type`, `fin`, `state`, `branch`, and defaults to `status = 'open'` while the page defaults to open+in_progress+blocked. Scenario: on the **Overdue** tab (12 rows) a manager clicks "Select all across pages" → the route returns up to 500 *open* tasks regardless of due date → "Mark done" / "Delete" / "Reassign" is applied to hundreds of tasks that were never on screen. On the **Completed** tab, "select all" selects open tasks. _Fix:_ extract the page's WHERE builder into one shared function and use it in the page, `/api/tasks/ids` (and `/api/tasks` GET/kpis).
- **[SRV-02] [High] Date-only due dates are stored as 00:00 UTC (05:30 IST) — a task "due today" is overdue the moment it is created; every Save also rewrites the due/SLA time** — `src/app/dashboard/tasks/new/page.tsx:300,308,852-856`; `TaskDetailDrawer.tsx:158-164,239-240`. `new Date("2026-10-03").toISOString()` is 05:30 IST, so a task created at 10:00 with the "Today" chip already satisfies `overdueTaskSql` (`due_at < now()`), lands on the Overdue tab (never on "Today", which requires `due_at >= now()`), bumps the sidebar badge, and the overdue scanner alerts the assignee at 05:30 on the due day. `defaultDueDate()` also uses the UTC date, so between 00:00–05:30 IST "Today" means yesterday. In the drawer, *any* Save re-sends `dueAt` and `slaDueAt` from the date inputs, collapsing a 16:00 IST due time or a 4-hour urgent SLA to 05:30 IST of that date (instantly "SLA breached"); "Remind at due" fires at 05:30. _Fix:_ treat a picked date as end-of-day (or 10:00) IST (`${d}T18:30:00Z`-style helper shared with opportunities' 10:00 IST rule), and only send `dueAt`/`slaDueAt` when the user changed them.
- **[SRV-03] [Medium] Approval sign-off can be bypassed by the assignee** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/route.ts:69-72,144-146,222-230`; `src/app/api/tasks/bulk/route.ts:54-58`; `src/app/api/tasks/route.ts:50,335`. `requiresApproval` is documented as creator/owner-only and the drawer hides the toggle (`TaskDetailDrawer.tsx:554`), but the API applies it from any editor: an assignee PATCHes `{requiresApproval:false}` then `{status:"completed"}` and the gate (which checks `before.requiresApproval`) is gone. Bulk "Mark done" ignores `requiresApproval` entirely (an assignee bulk-completes their own approval-gated tasks), and POST accepts `status:"completed"` together with `requiresApproval:true`. Assignees can also move their own `slaDueAt` to hide a breach. _Fix:_ only `isApprover` may change `requiresApproval`/`slaDueAt`; bulk "completed" must route approval-gated rows to `approvalStatus='pending'` like the single PATCH.
- **[SRV-04] [Medium] Sub-task delegates get full edit of the parent task but cannot see its comments, files, time log or watchers** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/route.ts:193-197` vs `comments/route.ts:42,83`, `attachments/route.ts:44,85`, `attachments/[attId]/download/route.ts:209`, `time/route.ts:48,94`, `watchers/route.ts:43,75`. `isSubtaskAssignee` lets a delegate PATCH *every* field (title, reassign, cancel/complete the whole task, rewrite other people's checklist items, submit for approval), while every sub-resource still uses `ownsTask` only, so the task page they are linked to (`?focus=` notification) shows "not found" errors in Comments/Attachments/Time. _Fix:_ restrict delegates to their own checklist items (+ comment), and apply the same access predicate to all sub-resources.
- **[SRV-05] [Medium] Recurring tasks: reopen + re-complete spawns duplicate occurrences; spawned occurrence changes creator** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/route.ts:412-415`, `src/lib/tasks/recurrence.ts:19-29,44,73`. The spawn guard is only `before.status !== 'completed'`, so completed → reopened → completed creates a second "next" occurrence each time (no link/idempotency check on `recurrence_parent_id` + due). The new occurrence's `createdById` is the *completer* (usually the assignee), so the original creator loses "Assigned by me"/status pings and the assignee becomes the approver of the next occurrence (`isApprover = createdById === userId`) — approval self-bypass from occurrence 2 on. Monthly cadence uses `setMonth` (Jan 31 → Mar 3, then drifts to the 3rd forever), and the next due is anchored on the old due date, so a task completed late spawns an already-overdue occurrence. _Fix:_ carry `createdById` from the root, skip spawn if a child of this occurrence exists, clamp month-end, and roll forward past `now()`.
- **[SRV-06] [Medium] Bulk complete / cancel / delete skip the Business-entry sync the single routes do** — `src/app/api/tasks/bulk/route.ts:95-130` vs `[id]/route.ts:404-410,449`. A financial-request task bulk-completed leaves its Business entry in **draft** (its SIPs never join the SIP book); bulk-cancelled or bulk-deleted leaves a draft/confirmed entry live. _Fix:_ load the affected rows and call `syncTaskBusinessEntry` per task in the bulk route.
- **[SRV-07] [Medium] Creating a task does not check the linked client/lead is in the viewer's book (PATCH does)** — `src/app/api/tasks/route.ts:261-263,295-308`. On the web only `assertMobileSubjects` runs, so an RM can POST `relatedInvestorId` of another RM's client (or any meeting/SR id); `bookBusinessFromTask` (`lib/tasks/book-business.ts:40-53`) then books a Business entry against that client, and the list/Related section shows the client's name. _Fix:_ reuse the `loadViewableInvestor` / `loadViewableLead` / tenant checks from PATCH (lines 171-184) in POST.
- **[SRV-08] [Medium] "Cancel task" actually trashes the task** — `src/app/dashboard/tasks/TaskDetailDrawer.tsx:331-345,844-848,873-877`. The button and dialog say "The task will be marked cancelled", but `doCancelTask` calls `DELETE /api/tasks/[id]`, which soft-deletes to Trash (`[id]/route.ts:436-457`). The task vanishes from every list (including the "Cancelled" status filter and completion reports), the audit says `task.delete`, and the RM believes it was cancelled. _Fix:_ PATCH `{status:"cancelled"}` for Cancel; keep Delete as a separate action.
- **[SRV-09] [Low] Task notifications link to a view that does not exist and never to the task** — e.g. `src/app/api/tasks/route.ts:389`, `[id]/route.ts:244,262,279,327,342`, `comments/route.ts:126,141`, `watchers/route.ts:88`, `lib/tasks/watchers.ts:41`, `lib/review-meetings/sync.ts:180` (13 sites). They use `?view=assigned_to_me` / `assigned_by_me`, but the page reads `to_me` / `by_me` (`page.tsx:82-85`), so the click opens the unfiltered all-open list (firm-wide for view-all users). _Fix:_ link to `/dashboard/tasks/${taskId}` (the id is already in `meta.task_id`).
- **[SRV-10] [Low] "Due today" filter and the KPI ribbon disagree with the tabs/badge** — `page.tsx:176`, `ids/route.ts:64`, `api/tasks/route.ts:182`, `lib/tasks/kpis.ts:12-13`. They use `due_at::date = current_date` (UTC date; wrong from 00:00–05:30 IST), while the "Today" tab uses the IST date; KPI "overdue" counts only `status='open'` whereas `overdueTaskSql` (badge, tab, dashboards) includes in_progress/blocked. _Fix:_ use `IST_TODAY`/`DUE_DAY` and `overdueTaskSql` everywhere.
- **[SRV-11] [Low] Task attachment storage keys collide** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/attachments/route.ts:75-76`. The "random segment" is `buf.length ^ (name.length*2654435761)` — deterministic. Uploading the same-named, same-sized file twice (a common double-upload) writes one S3 object for two rows; deleting either row (`[attId]/route.ts:153`) deletes the object the other row still points to, whose download then fails. _Fix:_ use `randomUUID()` in the key.
- **[SRV-12] [Low] Watchers: anyone can remove anyone; watchers cannot open what they follow** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/watchers/route.ts:104-118`. DELETE takes `?userId=` with no task-access or self/owner check (and no task existence check). Conversely a watcher with only `task.view.assigned` gets 404 on `GET /api/tasks/[id]` and the task page, so "following" only yields notifications they cannot act on. _Fix:_ DELETE others only for task owners/edit-all; include watchers in the view predicate (or forbid adding users who cannot view).
- **[SRV-13] [Low] Rescheduling or reassigning a task in the drawer never re-arms its overdue alert** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/route.ts:150-164`. Only bulk reschedule clears `overdue_notified_at` (`bulk/route.ts:68`); a task that was flagged overdue, moved to next week via the drawer/quick-reschedule, or handed to a new assignee, never alerts again when it goes overdue. _Fix:_ set `overdueNotifiedAt = null` when `dueAt` or `assigneeId` changes.
- **[SRV-14] [Low] Related section reveals out-of-book client details** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/related/route.ts:186-196`. For a task the viewer owns about a colleague's client it returns the client's category, RM name and a link — the "Not in your book" rule (CLAUDE.md, RM dashboard) says only the name the task carries. _Fix:_ run the client/lead through `investorScopeSql`/`leadScopeSql` and blank sub/href when out of scope.
- **[SRV-15] [Low] `DELETE ?permanent=1` hard-deletes live tasks and is open to assignee-only editors** — `src/app/api/tasks/[id]/route.ts:439-451`. The comment says permanent removal is "only from the Trash bin", but nothing checks `deleted_at IS NOT NULL`; an assignee can permanently delete a task their manager created (comments/attachments go with it), skipping Trash. _Fix:_ require `before.deletedAt` for permanent and limit purge to edit-all or the creator.
- **[SRV-16] [Low] Kanban quick-add into "Completed" creates a completed task with no `completed_at`** — `TaskKanban.tsx:100-104` → `api/tasks/route.ts:326` (POST never sets `completedAt`). The card is invisible to completed-in-period counts, the DJT "completed yesterday" list and the Completed tab's sort. _Fix:_ stamp `completedAt` when `status==='completed'` on POST (or disallow quick-add there).

**Improvements**
- `GET /api/tasks/[id]` returns trashed tasks (no `deleted_at` filter) while the page 404s them; align.
- `blockedByTaskId` is not validated (same tenant, not self, no cycles); completing a blocked-by-open task is not prevented.
- Time tracker is per task, not per user: whoever clicks Stop gets the whole span logged as theirs; no audit rows for time logs.
- Comment @mentions accept any firm user, even one who cannot open the task (they get a title + body preview they could not otherwise read).
- POST lets assignee-only users create only self-assigned tasks, but PATCH/bulk let them reassign to anyone — pick one policy.
- Kanban silently caps at 300 cards with no "showing 300 of N" notice.
- The sidebar Tasks badge counts *my* overdue tasks but links to `/dashboard/tasks` (all open, firm-wide for view-all) — link to `?view=to_me&tab=overdue`.

### Task templates
_Checked:_ `GET/POST /api/task-templates`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/task-templates/[id]`, `GET/POST /api/task-templates/library`, template apply in `/dashboard/tasks/new`.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-17] [Low] Template writes are gated by a read capability** — `src/app/api/task-templates/route.ts:116`, `[id]/route.ts:92,184`. The header says writes need `settings.users`, and the library install does (`library/route.ts:58`), but create/edit/**hard delete** accept `task.view.all` — held by marketing, branch_head, compliance, ops, back_office, service_team. Any of them can delete the firm's SOP templates (no soft delete). _Fix:_ require `settings.users` (or a dedicated `task_template.manage`) on all writes.

**Improvements**
- Applying a template uses the UTC-date `defaultDueDate` (see SRV-02).
- Template `taskType` allows only 5 types while the New Task form has many request types; templates cannot pre-fill a financial request.

### Daily tracker (DJT) & service tracker
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/tasks/daily`, `GET/POST /api/daily-tracker`, `lib/daily-tracker.ts`, `lib/service-tracker.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-18] [Medium] DJT windows are UTC days and include trashed tasks** — `src/lib/daily-tracker.ts:43-78`; `src/app/api/daily-tracker/route.ts:18`. "Completed yesterday" is `completed_at` in `[date-1, date)` cast in UTC (05:30→05:30 IST), "due today" is `due_at::date` in UTC, and none of the three queries filter `t.deleted_at IS NULL`, so trashed tasks appear in the lists and in the productivity %. The API's default date is the UTC date (yesterday until 05:30 IST). _Fix:_ compare `(x AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata')::date`, add `deleted_at IS NULL`, default to the IST date.
- **[SRV-19] [Low] DJT input handling** — `src/app/dashboard/tasks/daily/page.tsx:45-53`; `api/daily-tracker/route.ts:14-56`. `?user=` is not UUID-checked (`::uuid` cast → 500), and any `dashboard.view.owner` holder (incl. branch heads) can open any user's tracker firm-wide, not just their branch. GET/POST have no try/catch: a regex-valid but impossible date (`2026-02-31`) → unhandled 500; `taskId` in `todayFocus` is not validated. _Fix:_ validate UUID/date with Zod + `handleApiError`; branch-limit the user picker.

**Improvements**
- `lib/service-tracker.ts` (`loadServiceTasks`) has no callers — dead code (and lacks a `deleted_at` filter); remove or wire it up.
- Past-date DJT views compute "carried over" from *current* task status, so history shifts as tasks are closed; store a snapshot on save.

### Service requests
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/service-requests` (tabs, filters, bulk), `/new`, `/[id]` (ServiceRequestDetailEditor); `GET/POST /api/service-requests`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/service-requests/[id]`, `POST /api/service-requests/bulk`; `lib/service-requests/open.ts`, `lib/service/create-request.ts` (WA/email intake).

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-20] [High] Service requests have no view authorisation — every signed-in user reads every ticket** — `src/app/dashboard/service-requests/page.tsx:72-126`, `src/app/dashboard/service-requests/[id]/page.tsx:30-45`, `src/app/api/service-requests/route.ts:61-71`, `src/app/api/service-requests/[id]/route.ts:46-58`. The capabilities `service_request.view.all` / `service_request.view.assigned` exist (`rbac.ts:228-229`) but none of these check them or scope to the viewer. An RM, marketing user or insurance user lists all firm tickets — including complaints *about that RM* (the very reason website complaints are routed to compliance), client names and resolution notes. `GET /api/service-requests` is also unbounded (every row, every column). _Fix:_ `requireAny(view.all, view.assigned)`; for `.assigned` scope to `assigned_to_user_id = me` OR client in `investorScopeSql`, and exclude complaints from non-compliance roles; paginate the API.
- **[SRV-21] [Medium] Ticket numbers are `COUNT(*)+1` — concurrent tickets get the same number** — `src/lib/service-requests/open.ts:82-87` (and the copy in `lib/service/create-request.ts:58-62`); index `svc_ticket_idx` is not unique. A website complaint arriving while staff key a ticket (or two WA intake messages) produce two `SR-2026-00042`s; SEBI complaint register references become ambiguous. The year uses UTC (`date_trunc('year', now())`), so tickets opened 00:00–05:30 IST on 1 Jan get the previous year's series. _Fix:_ a per-tenant/year counter row updated with `UPDATE … RETURNING` (or a sequence) + unique index on `(tenant_id, ticket_no)`; IST year.
- **[SRV-22] [Medium] A second ticket-opening path exists, and it routes WhatsApp complaints to the client's own RM** — `src/lib/service/create-request.ts:49-104`, callers `lib/comms/auto-responder.ts:50-68`, `lib/comms/email-to-ticket.ts:23`. CLAUDE.md: "A ticket starts in ONE place: `openServiceRequest`". The duplicate copies the SLA table, numbering, event and ack, and the WA intake assigns a detected *complaint* to `investors.rm_id` — the person the complaint may be about — with no compliance notification. `email-to-ticket` swallows an insert error inside the caller's transaction (Postgres aborts the tx, so the inbound-email logging then fails at commit). _Fix:_ delete `createServiceRequest`, call `openServiceRequest`, and route `type='complaint'` through `complaintHandler()` like the website form.
- **[SRV-23] [Medium] Resolution timestamps are wrong after reopen, close or bulk** — `src/app/api/service-requests/[id]/route.ts:92,160-180`; `bulk/route.ts:55-60`. `resolvedAt` is stamped only the first time and never cleared, so a complaint reopened and resolved again keeps the original resolution date (TAT and "resolved in 30 days" understated; breach flags use status so it reads "not breached"). `DELETE` ("close") sets status only — no `resolvedAt`, no timeline entry, and returns 200 for an unknown id — so closed tickets show TAT "still running" in the register. Bulk status overwrites `resolvedAt` on already-resolved tickets and writes no timeline entries. _Fix:_ clear `resolvedAt` on moving back to an open state, set it on every transition into resolved/closed, and share one transition helper between PATCH, DELETE and bulk.
- **[SRV-24] [Low] Reassignment is unvalidated and silent; fields cannot be cleared** — `[id]/route.ts:28,81`, `bulk/route.ts:47-48`; `ServiceRequestDetailEditor.tsx:119-123`. `assignedToUserId` is not checked to be an active firm user, the new assignee gets no notification, and the editor sends `undefined` for an emptied assignee/remarks/resolution, so they can never be cleared. Status flow has no validation (closed → open → escalated without `escalated_at`). _Fix:_ validate + notify on assign; send `null` to clear.

**Improvements**
- `/dashboard/service-requests/new` is reachable by RMs (view.assigned) but POST needs `service_request.manage` → form submits and 403s; hide or gate the button.
- `investorId` on POST is not checked against the viewer's book.
