- No `service_request.updated` / resolved events are emitted though `workflow-rules/modules.ts:404` advertises `service_request.updated`; resolution-triggered CSAT surveys cannot be built.

### Complaints (register + website form admin)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/complaints` (register, stats, ResolutionEditor), `/dashboard/complaints/website-form`, `/dashboard/complaints/disclosure` (redirect), `PATCH /api/complaints/website-form`, `lib/complaints/web-form.ts` (link, handler, client match).

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-25] [High] The complaint register has no capability check** — `src/app/dashboard/complaints/page.tsx:43-140`. Any signed-in user (RM, marketing, insurance) sees every complaint with the client's **PAN**, SEBI category, assignee and resolution notes, including complaints against themselves. _Fix:_ require `compliance.view` / `service_request.view.all` (owner implicit) and redirect others.
- **[SRV-26] [Low] Register is truncated and its stats disagree with its rows** — `complaints/page.tsx:105-140`. Rows are `LIMIT 200` with no pager (older complaints silently disappear from the register), the stat "breached" counts only open/in_progress while the row flag counts escalated/waiting too, and "open" omits `waiting_customer`. Stats also ignore every filter. _Fix:_ paginate; derive stats from the same predicate as the rows.
- **[SRV-27] [Low] Website complaint client match ignores closed/exited clients' status** — `src/lib/complaints/web-form.ts:136-148`. "Live client" (CLAUDE.md) excludes closed/exited, but `matchClient` only excludes deleted/merged, so a complainant's email matching an exited client links the ticket (and sends the ack) to that old record. _Fix:_ add the `aliveClient` predicate.

**Improvements**
- The website-form page builds the public link from `x-forwarded-host` (same class as the known reset-link poisoning) — use the configured app/tenant domain.
- `ensureComplaintFormLink` runs an `UPDATE tenants` on every page view; only write when the token is null (check first).

### SOPs
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/sops`, `/new`, `/[id]` (PublishVersionForm, AcknowledgeButton); `POST /api/sops`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/sops/[id]`, `POST /api/sops/[id]/version`, `POST /api/sops/[id]/acknowledge`.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-28] [Low] A deleted SOP's key can never be reused** — `src/app/api/sops/route.ts:69-71`; index `sops_tenant_key_unique` (`0000_base_schema.sql:2162`) is not partial. After a soft delete (`[id]/route.ts:174-178`) recreating "complaint_handling" returns 409 "key already exists", and there is no restore UI. _Fix:_ make the unique index `WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` (new migration) or offer restore.
- **[SRV-29] [Low] Acknowledge signs whatever version is current at click time, and works on deleted SOPs** — `src/app/api/sops/[id]/acknowledge/route.ts:122-149`. If v2 is published while a user is reading v1, their click records an acknowledgement of v2 (unread) — weak DDQ evidence. No `deleted_at` filter; "SOP not found" is a plain `Error` → 500. _Fix:_ post the `versionId` shown and reject if it is not current; filter deleted; return 404.

**Improvements**
- No UI to edit title/category/required roles (PATCH is unused; RowActions "edit" just opens the detail page).
- Show who in `requiredRoles` has *not* acknowledged the current version — the count alone is not inspection-ready.
- `versionNumber` may be supplied explicitly and can duplicate an existing label.

### Desks
_Checked:_ `GET/POST /api/desks`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/desks/[id]`, `POST /api/desks/assign`, `lib/org/desks.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-30] [Medium] Desk management is not branch-scoped and unaudited** — `src/lib/org/desks.ts:86-126`; routes `api/desks/*`. `settings.desks` is granted to branch heads, back office and ops (`rbac.ts:365`), yet a branch head can create desks in another branch, rename/delete other branches' desks, and `assignUserDesk` moves any user of the firm into any desk — the "must be in the same branch" rule in its comment is not enforced. Desks drive dashboard "team" shares (`allowedShares`), so this widens what people see. No `recordAudit` on any desk change; unknown ids return 200. _Fix:_ for branch-scoped users require `desk.branch_id = session.branchId` and `user.branch_id = desk.branch_id`; audit; 404 on no row.

**Improvements**
- `loadDeskBoard` lists deleted/inactive users and counts them as desk members.

### Macros
_Checked:_ `GET/POST /api/macros`, `POST /api/macros/[id]/run`, callers `SalesOpsManager`, `RecordAutomations`.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-31] [Medium] Running a macro is not scoped to the viewer's book and writes raw lead stages** — `src/app/api/macros/[id]/run/route.ts:85-113`. The subject is loaded by id only (no `investorScopeSql`/`leadScopeSql`, no `deleted_at`), so an RM with `task.edit.assigned` can run a macro on a colleague's lead: add notes, create tasks, and `advance_stage` sets `leads.stage = String(c.stage)` — any string from the macro config (`z.record(z.any())`), bypassing the blueprint journey and stage history; an unknown stage drops the lead off every board column. The macro's `subject_type` is not compared with the request's. `dueInDays`/`days` that are not numbers → `'NaN days'::interval` → 500. _Fix:_ load via `loadViewableLead`/`loadViewableInvestor`, validate stages against `LEAD_PIPELINE` and advance through the blueprint engine, check subject type, validate numbers in the create schema.

**Improvements**
- No route to edit, deactivate or delete a macro (only create); `GET` has no capability check (lists every macro to any user).

### My Day (API) & notifications
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/my-day` (redirect), `GET /api/dashboard/my-day`; `/dashboard/notifications`, `GET /api/notifications`, `POST /api/notifications/mark-read`, `lib/notifications/create.ts`, NotificationBell.

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-32] [Medium] My Day API ignores the branch lock and the attendee rule** — `src/app/api/dashboard/my-day/route.ts:30,40,50-52,99`. `branch_head` is in `FIRM_WIDE`, so on the web a branch-locked head gets every branch's meetings, follow-ups, tasks and client birthdays **with phone numbers**; tasks use `(assignee = me OR true)`. Meetings are scoped on `m.rm_id` only, so joint meetings the user attends are missing — the documented rule is "never read only rm_id … use `attendeeIs`". All day windows are UTC (`date_trunc('day', now())`, `current_date`), so 00:00–05:30 IST shows yesterday. `rows()` swallows errors inside one shared transaction, so one failed query aborts the tx and every later block silently returns `[]`. _Fix:_ use `isBranchScoped`/`resolveDataAccess`, add `OR attendeeIs('user', uid)`, IST dates, and per-block savepoints.
- **[SRV-33] [Low] Birthday reminder in the bell is off by a day for five and a half hours and not branch-scoped** — `src/app/api/notifications/route.ts:27,51-60`. `to_char(current_date,'MM-DD')` is the UTC date; 29 Feb birthdays never show in non-leap years; `dashboard.view.owner` (branch heads) gets firm-wide names. _Fix:_ IST date, Feb 29 → 28 Feb/1 Mar, branch scope.

**Improvements**
- There are no notification preferences at all (no table/route); every kind is always on — the module brief expects per-kind in-app/push choices.
- The history page shows only the latest 50 (API limit, no paging) and there is no archive endpoint although queries filter `archived_at`; no retention job, so `notifications` grows forever.
- `createNotification` does not skip deactivated users.

### Review meetings
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/review-meetings` (table, kanban, bulk table, cancel/delete buttons), `/new` (ScheduleMeetingForm), `/[id]` (ReviewMeetingDetailEditor, attendees, action items, attachments, prep), `/[id]/portfolio`; API `GET/POST /api/review-meetings`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/review-meetings/[id]`, `DELETE …/purge`, `POST …/prep`, `GET/PUT …/portfolio`, `POST …/files`, `GET/DELETE …/files/[fileId]`, `POST /bulk`, `GET /subjects`, `GET /attendee-search`, `GET /team`; `lib/review-meetings/{sync,attendees,conflicts,files}.ts`, CompleteMeetingModal, MeetingsKanban. (calendar.ics scope gap already known.)

**Bugs**
- **[SRV-34] [High] The meetings list and meeting pages have no capability or ownership check** — `src/app/dashboard/review-meetings/page.tsx:68-170`, `src/app/dashboard/review-meetings/[id]/page.tsx:23-48`. The API was locked down ("Was wide-open to any session", `api/review-meetings/route.ts:78-81`), but the pages were not: any signed-in user sees every meeting in the firm with client **PAN**, discussion points, meeting notes and RM, and can open any meeting's detail page (prep brief: AUM, SIPs, open SRs, bounces) by id. The stats ribbon is firm-wide too. _Fix:_ apply the same `requireAny(manage.*)` + `(m.rm_id = me OR attendeeIs('user', me))` predicate as `GET /api/review-meetings`, and `notFound()` on the detail page.
- **[SRV-35] [High] The schedule-meeting subject lists expose 300 firm-wide clients with PAN to any user** — `src/app/api/review-meetings/subjects/route.ts:108-160`, `src/app/dashboard/review-meetings/new/page.tsx:24-50`. No capability check and no `investorScopeSql`/`leadScopeSql`: an RM (or any role) gets the firm's clients with PAN and leads with phone numbers; `?investorId=` returns any client's PAN by id. _Fix:_ require `review_meeting.manage.*`, scope both lists, drop PAN from the payload.
- **[SRV-36] [Medium] A firm with more than 300 active clients cannot schedule a meeting for the rest from the form** — same files, `LIMIT 300`; `ScheduleMeetingForm.tsx:265` filters that list locally. Client #301 onward (and every `redemption_risk` client — `status = 'active'` only) cannot be picked; leads likewise cap at 300 most-recent. _Fix:_ use the server-side `InvestorLookup` the SR form already uses ("the largest firm here has 10,341").
- **[SRV-37] [Medium] Cancelling through PATCH/bulk bypasses the owner rule and leaves the reminder and action-item tasks open** — `src/app/api/review-meetings/[id]/route.ts:187-191`, `bulk/route.ts:150-156`, UI `ReviewMeetingDetailEditor.tsx:49-55,270`, `MeetingsKanban.tsx:85`, `MeetingsBulkTable.tsx:99`. CLAUDE.md: attendees may edit, "cancelling stays with the owner" — but PATCH spreads `status` from the body, so any attendee picks "Cancelled" in the editor. Only `DELETE` cancels the T-1 reminder task; PATCH, Kanban drag and the row "Cancel" button (which uses bulk) leave "Reminder: meeting tomorrow" open, and no path (DELETE included) cancels the meeting's open action-item tasks. The row Cancel for an attended (not owned) meeting returns `count: 0` but toasts "Meeting cancelled". _Fix:_ one `cancelMeeting()` helper (owner/manage.all only) that cancels reminder + open `related_meeting_id` tasks, used by PATCH-to-cancelled, bulk and DELETE; check `count`.
- **[SRV-38] [Medium] Bulk "Set status → Completed" skips every completion automation** — `src/app/api/review-meetings/bulk/route.ts:152-180` (offered in `MeetingsBulkTable.tsx:140`). It only updates `status`, so the client's `last_review_at`/`next_review_at`, family propagation, family health, action-item fan-out, the auto-scheduled next review and the lead blueprint advance never happen — the client stays "review overdue" after the RM marked the meeting done. Same for bulk `no_show` (no re-engage task). _Fix:_ run the PATCH completion path per meeting, or drop completed/no_show from bulk.
- **[SRV-39] [Medium] Scheduling a meeting on the web is not scoped to the viewer's book** — `src/app/api/review-meetings/route.ts:173-198`. The investor/lead visibility check runs only for `session.mobile`; on the web an RM can schedule against any client or lead of the firm. `rmId` then defaults to the subject's RM, so the meeting and its reminder task land on a colleague's calendar, and for a lead `autoAdvance(…"meeting.scheduled.lead")` moves the colleague's lead journey. `parentMeetingId`/`familyId` are not validated. _Fix:_ always use `loadViewableInvestor`/`loadViewableLead`; validate parent/family ids in-tenant.
- **[SRV-40] [Medium] Every meeting save rewrites its action-item tasks, undoing changes made in Tasks** — `src/lib/review-meetings/sync.ts:114,134-149`. The editor and CompleteMeetingModal always send all `actionItems`; for each open task the sync sets title, assignee and `due_at` again. An assignee who rescheduled or reassigned the task in the Task module has it reverted the next time anyone edits the meeting (e.g. adds an attendee). Items with no due date get `now()+7d` recomputed on each save, so their due date walks forward forever. Date-only `dueAt` is stored at 05:30 IST (SRV-02). _Fix:_ only update a task when that row's title/assignee/dueAt changed versus the stored row; persist the computed default due date in the row.
- **[SRV-41] [Low] Reminder tasks are created overdue and resurrected on reschedule** — `src/app/api/review-meetings/route.ts:237-252`, `[id]/route.ts:228-240`. The T-1 reminder is `meeting − 24h` even for a meeting later today (born overdue, title says "tomorrow"); rescheduling sets the reminder back to `status: 'open'` even if it was completed or the meeting was cancelled; the POST reminder has no `related_meeting_id`, so it is not linked to its meeting. The auto-scheduled next review is `new Date(`${d}T11:00:00`)` in server time (`[id]/route.ts:440`) — on a UTC server that is 16:30 IST. _Fix:_ skip/clamp past reminders, only reopen if the meeting is live, set `relatedMeetingId`, and build 11:00 IST explicitly.
- **[SRV-42] [Low] Meeting PATCH/portfolio validation gaps** — `[id]/route.ts:150` (`meetingMode: z.string()` stored verbatim — the enum is TS-only, so a bad value breaks labels and mode filters); reschedule via PATCH runs no double-booking check (`findRmConflicts` has an unused `excludeId` and ignores co-attendees); `portfolio/route.ts:158-230` accepts any firm `folioId` (another client's folio can be written into this client's review record) and throws plain `Error("meeting not found")` → 500; attendees allowed to edit the meeting get 403 on `prep` and `portfolio` (`prep/route.ts:52`, `portfolio/route.ts:95,170`). _Fix:_ `z.enum(REVIEW_MEETING_MODES)`, conflict check on date change, verify `folio.investor_id = meeting.investor_id`, use `hasUserAttendee` consistently.

**Improvements**
- Rescheduling does not notify team attendees (only newly-added ones are told); send `meeting.rescheduled`.
- Action items created at completion (`[id]/route.ts:383-398`) have no `createdById`, so "Assigned by" is blank and the creator gets no status pings.
- Meeting stats (`this_week`, `completed_mtd`) use UTC week/month boundaries.
- `bulk/route.ts:190` `recordAudit(...).catch(() => {})` inside the tx cannot actually recover — a failed insert aborts the Postgres transaction; drop the catch.
- `prep/route.ts` `sip_monthly_total` sums raw SIP amounts regardless of frequency — use `sipMonthlySql`.

---

## Portfolio, transactions & products

### MF Transactions (list, import, mobile feed)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/transactions` (page, KPIs, cursor paging, filters), `/dashboard/transactions/import` (ImportWizard), `GET /api/transactions`, `POST /api/transactions/import/parse`, `POST /api/transactions/import/commit`, `lib/transactions/list-filters.ts` (export route already reported).

**Bugs**
- **[PF-01] [High] MF Transactions page has no capability gate and no viewer scope** — `src/app/dashboard/transactions/page.tsx:105-196` — `baseConds` are tenant + URL filters only; there is no `requireCap(...)` and no `investorScopeSql` / own-book slice (compare `/dashboard/sips`, which requires `transactions.view` and narrows non-managers). Any signed-in user (RM, marketing, insurance team) sees every client's transactions, amounts, folios and the firm-wide inflow/outflow KPIs, and can page through the whole ledger. (The export route's gap is already known; the page itself is a separate entry point.) _Fix:_ require `transactions.view` and AND `investorScopeSql(tx, session, "investors")` into `baseConds` (stats + rows).
- **[PF-02] [High] Excel transaction import silently commits only the first 200 rows** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/transactions/import/parse/route.ts:99`, `src/components/imports/ImportWizard.tsx:68,191` — parse returns `rows: rows.slice(0, 200)` (a preview), and the wizard commits `parsed.rows` while its button reads "Import {parsed.total} rows" and the help text says "Max 5,000 rows". A 3,000-row file reports success with ≤200 inserted; the rest are dropped with no error. _Fix:_ return all rows (≤ the 10k cap) for commit, or keep the file server-side and commit from it; show inserted vs total.
- **[PF-03] [Medium] One bad row aborts the whole Excel import (no savepoints)** — `src/app/api/transactions/import/commit/route.ts:126-268` — every row runs inside one `withTenant` tx; the per-row `try/catch` (and the "best-effort" folio upsert) cannot recover from a Postgres error because the tx is already aborted. E.g. a date that passes the `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}` check but is invalid (`2024-13-45`), or a numeric overflow → every later statement fails "current transaction is aborted", the final `recordAudit` throws, the request 500s and nothing is saved, after the user saw the row-level preview succeed. _Fix:_ wrap each row in `SAVEPOINT`/`ROLLBACK TO` as `lib/rta/process.ts` does.
- **[PF-04] [Medium] Excel import stores unnormalised direction/type/amount/date values** — `src/app/api/transactions/import/commit/route.ts:66-78,132-140` — (a) `direction = pick(row, …) || inferDirection(type)` writes the sheet's raw text ("Inflow", "IN", "Credit"); the column has no DB CHECK, so those rows vanish from every `direction='inflow'/'outflow'` sum (page KPIs, reconciliation) and reconciliation subtracts them; (b) unknown types default to `lumpsum`, so "Switch Out", "Dividend Reinvestment", "STP Out" become gross purchases in Net business; (c) `parseFloat("1,00,000")` = 1 and unparseable amounts become 0 rows; negative redemption amounts are kept negative; (d) an empty/unparseable date silently becomes **today** (UTC), and SheetJS `raw:false` emits US `m/d/yy` strings that fall to `new Date()` — wrong day/month for Indian dates and shifted a day back on an IST server. _Fix:_ map direction/type through an explicit table (reject unknowns), strip commas and abs() amounts, and reject rows with no parseable date instead of defaulting to today.
- **[PF-05] [Medium] PAN → client lookups ignore trashed and merged-away records** — `src/app/api/transactions/import/commit/route.ts:144-146`; also `src/lib/rta/process.ts:56` (`resolveInvestorId`), `:143` (`buildInvestorIndex`), `:663` (folio master), `:830` (SIP book), `:958` (mandates) — `SELECT id FROM investors WHERE pan = … LIMIT 1` has no `deleted_at IS NULL` / `merged_into_id IS NULL`. A merged loser keeps its PAN (merge.ts:277 only sets status/merged_into_id), and `dedup_pan=false` rows can share a PAN, so imported transactions/SIPs/mandates can land on a Trash or merged-away record and disappear from the live client (and from AUM/SIP book). _Fix:_ prefer live, unmerged rows (`ORDER BY (deleted_at IS NULL AND merged_into_id IS NULL) DESC`) or follow `merged_into_id` to the survivor.
- **[PF-06] [Low] Mobile `GET /api/transactions` scopes by `rm_id` only and includes trashed clients** — `src/app/api/transactions/route.ts:25-30` — branch heads / team leads without `investor.view.all` see only their personal clients, and rows of deleted or merged clients are returned. _Fix:_ use `investorScopeSql` + `clientRecordWhere` like the web list.

**Improvements**
- Map a transaction-id column (`trnsId`/`orderId`) to `order_id`; today `reverse.orderId` is never mappable (not in `OUR_FIELDS`), so dedup always falls back to the natural-key hash, which omits folio (two same-day same-amount buys in different folios collapse).
- Commit does a per-row `SELECT … WHERE pan` (N+1 over up to 10k rows); bulk-resolve PANs first as `buildInvestorIndex` does.
- The Excel import lets any `transactions.execute` holder write transactions for clients outside their book; scope the PAN lookup to `investorScopeSql`.

### SIPs (create, book, installment watch, defend)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/sips`, `/sips/new` (ManualSipForm), `/sips/new-watch`, `/sips/at-risk`, `GET/POST /api/sips`, `POST /api/sips/installment-mark`, `POST /api/sips/[id]/defend`, every writer of `sips` (api/sips, rta/process.ts SIP book, business/create-entry.ts, finance/sip-lifecycle.ts) for `refreshActiveSipAmount`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-07] [High] SIPs created from a Business entry stay active after the entry is cancelled or edited** — `src/lib/business/create-entry.ts:166-188`, `src/app/api/business-entries/[id]/route.ts:108-136,213-218` — `registerEntrySips` inserts `sips` rows (status active) once per entry, but DELETE (soft-cancel) and PATCH (`status: cancelled`, or replacing `lines` with a new amount/frequency) never touch those SIPs. The sips rows don't even record the entry id (only the outbox meta does). A cancelled ₹50,000/month booking stays in the SIP book, the client's Monthly SIP and every RM/Branch SIP KPI forever. _Fix:_ store `business_entry_id` on the SIP; on cancel set those SIPs `stopped`, on line edits update/replace them, then `refreshActiveSipAmount`.
- **[PF-08] [Medium] There is no way to edit, stop, pause or delete a SIP** — `src/app/api/sips/` (only `route.ts`, `installment-mark`, `[id]/defend`) — no `PATCH/DELETE /api/sips/[id]` and no UI action exists. A manually keyed SIP with a typo (₹50,000 for ₹5,000), a client who stops, or a duplicate from PF-07/PF-34 can only be fixed in the database; `completeExpiredSips` only closes SIPs with an end date and a folio history. _Fix:_ add PATCH (amount, frequency, status stopped/paused, end date) + soft delete with audit and `refreshActiveSipAmount`.
- **[PF-09] [Medium] Manual SIP create: next-instalment date and dates unvalidated** — `src/app/api/sips/route.ts:145-147,201-203` — `nextInstallmentAt = startDate` ignores `sipDate` (start 1 Oct, SIP day 15 → next due 1 Oct) and keeps a past start date as the "next" instalment, which feeds installment-watch/reminder workflows; `startDate`/`endDate` are free strings (a bad value → 500 from the `date` cast; `endDate < startDate` accepted); a Trash/merged client is accepted; the frequency enum omits `half_yearly`, which the table and SIP book support. _Fix:_ reuse `nextInstallmentDate()` from create-entry.ts, validate yyyy-mm-dd and order, reject non-live clients, add `half_yearly`.
- **[PF-10] [Low] installment-mark returns 500 for bad input and ignores permission overrides** — `src/app/api/sips/installment-mark/route.ts:30-44` — `Body.parse` throws a ZodError (500, not 400) and "SIP not found" is a plain Error (500, not 404); the gate is a hard-coded role list, so a custom `permissions` grant/deny is ignored. _Fix:_ `safeParse` → 400, status 404, gate on a capability.

**Improvements**
- `/dashboard/sips` "Lost MTD" uses `updated_at` of stopped/rejected SIPs, so any later edit of an old stopped SIP re-counts it as lost this month.
- `GET /api/sips` (mobile) uses `rm_id` only, like PF-06; switch to `investorScopeSql`.

### SIP rejections
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/sip-rejections`, `SipRejectionsTable` (bulk, auto-recover, defend), `NewSipRejectionButton`, `RecoveryStatusUpdater`, `RemarkEditor`, `GET/POST /api/sip-rejections`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/sip-rejections/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/auto-recover`, `POST …/bulk`, RTA rejection import (`processSipRejectionReport`).

**Bugs**
- **[PF-11] [High] "Auto-recover" always fails with a 500** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/sip-rejections/[id]/auto-recover/route.ts:56-91` — the `events_outbox` insert passes `id: uuid()` (column is bigserial) and `eventKind/subjectType/subjectId/payload/status`, none of which exist; the NOT NULL `kind`, `entity_type`, `entity_id` columns are left empty. The task insert likewise uses `taskType` (real column `type`, NOT NULL), `investorId` (not a column), no `subjectType`, and `status: "todo"` (not a task status). The tx throws, so no WhatsApp, no task, no status change: the row's main action button does nothing but error. _Fix:_ insert `{kind, entityType:"investor", entityId, after, meta}` and a task with `type`, `subjectType/subjectId`, `relatedInvestorId`, `status:"open"`.
- **[PF-12] [Medium] Rejection list, KPIs and API are firm-wide for every user** — `src/app/api/sip-rejections/route.ts:40-56`, `src/app/dashboard/sip-rejections/page.tsx:43-145` — no capability and no client scope: any signed-in user gets every client's rejections with PAN, amounts and reasons, and any `transactions.execute` holder can PATCH/bulk-close/reassign rejections of clients outside their book. Rows of trashed/merged clients are included too. _Fix:_ require `transactions.view`, apply `investorScopeSql(tx, session, "i")` + live-client rule to the list, stats and the write routes.
- **[PF-13] [Medium] Closing or recovering a rejection leaves its follow-up task open** — `src/app/api/sip-rejections/route.ts:111-126`, `[id]/route.ts:58`, `[id]/route.ts:84-89`, `bulk/route.ts:44-62` — POST creates a `sip_rejection_followup` task with no link back (no `meta.rejectionId`, not stored on the rejection), so marking "recovered"/"not recoverable" (single, bulk or DELETE) never closes it, and "assign" to a new RM leaves the task with the old RM. Overdue-task counts and the RM's My Day keep showing settled rejections. _Fix:_ store the task id (or `meta.rejectionId`) and close/reassign it alongside the rejection.
- **[PF-14] [Medium] Importing a historical rejection report messages clients about old bounces** — `src/lib/rta/process.ts:466-505` — every newly inserted row emits `transaction.failed.sip_rejection` (which drives the recovery recipe's WhatsApp) regardless of `rejection_date`. Uploading a back-dated or since-inception rejection report, or re-importing after a client merge (the dedup key includes `investor_id`, so a merged client's rows insert again), fires a recovery message per old rejection. _Fix:_ only emit for rejections within N days (e.g. 7) and key dedup on folio/scheme/date/amount, not the investor id.
- **[PF-15] [Low] KPI cards ignore the page's filters, and manual rejections bypass the recipe** — `src/app/dashboard/sip-rejections/page.tsx:96-128`, `src/app/api/sip-rejections/route.ts:138-145` — the stats query is tenant-only, so a branch/RM drill-down (`?branch=`, `?rm=`) shows firm totals above a filtered table; recovery rate prints 0% when nothing was rejected (null is not zero). Manually logged rejections emit `sip.rejection.created`, not the `transaction.failed.sip_rejection` the recipe listens to, so they never get the automated client message. _Fix:_ apply the same filters to stats, show "—" for no base, emit the recipe's event kind.

**Improvements**
- `POST /api/sip-rejections`: `rejectionDate`/`followUpDate` are free strings (bad value → 500); `assignedRmId` (and bulk `value`) isn't checked to be an active user of this firm.
- `NewSipRejectionButton` defaults the date with `toISOString()` (UTC): between 00:00 and 05:30 IST it pre-fills yesterday.
- `DELETE /api/sip-rejections/[id]` returns `ok` for an unknown id; return 404.

### Expected inflows
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/expected-inflows`, `ExpectedInflowsCard` (client page: add / mark / remove), `GET/POST /api/expected-inflows`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/expected-inflows/[id]`, `lib/inflows/reminder.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-16] [Medium] Removing an FD-maturity inflow fails with a foreign-key error, silently** — `src/app/api/expected-inflows/[id]/route.ts:96-110`, `drizzle/migrations/0226_fixed_deposits.sql:40`, `src/components/investors/ExpectedInflowsCard.tsx:110-113` — `fixed_deposits.inflow_id` references `expected_inflows(id)` with no ON DELETE action, so DELETE on any inflow an FD owns raises an FK violation → 500 (the reminder-task cancel in the same tx rolls back). The card's `remove()` only handles `res.ok`, so the trash icon does nothing with no message. _Fix:_ refuse delete of `fd_maturity` inflows that have an FD ("change the FD instead") or null `fixed_deposits.inflow_id` first; show the error in the card.
- **[PF-17] [Medium] Any inflow or FD edit re-opens a completed reminder task** — `src/lib/inflows/reminder.ts:83-98` — when the inflow is still open, the existing task is updated with `status: "open"`, `reminderFiredAt: null` and `dueAt = max(now, date − N)`. An RM who completed the "FD maturity — call the client" task, then edits the FD (adds a nominee, fixes the FD number) or changes the inflow's notes/confidence, gets the task back as open and re-notified, now due "now". _Fix:_ only re-arm when `expectedOn`/`remindDaysBefore` changed and leave a completed task alone (or create a new one).
- **[PF-18] [Medium] Inflows page and API are unscoped, and the KPIs read a truncated, oldest-first slice** — `src/app/dashboard/expected-inflows/page.tsx:43-75`, `src/app/api/expected-inflows/route.ts:19-56` — `investor.view.assigned` is accepted, but rows are only tenant-filtered, so an RM sees every client's expected money; trashed clients are included. The page loads `LIMIT 1000 ORDER BY expected_on ASC` and computes "Expected (next 90 days)", "Total upcoming" and "Captured" from that array, so once a firm has >1000 inflows (every FD creates one) the upcoming ones are the ones cut off. _Fix:_ apply `investorScopeSql` + live clients; compute KPIs in SQL, page the list.
- **[PF-19] [Low] FD-owned inflow fields are editable, then silently overwritten** — `src/app/api/expected-inflows/[id]/route.ts:30-36`, `src/lib/fixed-deposits/hooks.ts:62-68` — PATCH accepts `expectedAmount`/`expectedOn`/`ownerId` for an `fd_maturity` inflow, and the next FD write overwrites them; an inflow the RM marked `cancelled` is revived to `expected` on the next FD save. _Fix:_ lock amount/date/owner for FD-linked inflows; don't revive a user-cancelled one unless the FD's own status changed.

**Improvements**
- `reminderDateFor` builds `new Date("<date>T09:00:00")` in the server's zone: on a UTC host reminders fire at 14:30 IST.
- `today` on the page is a UTC date, so the "Date passed, not actioned" split is wrong for 5½ hours each night; use `indiaToday()`.
- The card's Remove has no confirmation (`confirmDialog`).

### Fixed deposits
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/fixed-deposits` (+ `/new`, `/new?renew=`, `/[id]`, `/[id]/edit`), `FdForm`, `FdActions`, `ClientFdPanel`, `GET/POST /api/fixed-deposits`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/fixed-deposits/[id]`, `lib/fixed-deposits/{service,hooks,terms,schema,form}.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-20] [Low] FD `rmId` is not checked against the firm's staff** — `src/lib/fixed-deposits/service.ts:57,140-143` — any uuid of an existing user (the FK is to the global `users` table, so including another tenant's user) is accepted as the deposit's RM; `syncFdAutomations` then makes that id the inflow owner and the reminder task's assignee, so the maturity reminder goes to nobody in the firm. _Fix:_ validate `rmId` is an active, non-investor user of `session.tenantId`.
- **[PF-21] [Low] A renewal can mark someone else's deposit "renewed"** — `src/lib/fixed-deposits/service.ts:82-90` — `renewedFromId` is only checked to be viewable, not to belong to the same client or to be live; a crafted POST (or a stale `?renew=` link after the old FD was already renewed) links the new FD to an unrelated or already-renewed deposit and cancels that deposit's maturity inflow. _Fix:_ require `prev.investorId === input.investorId` and `isLive(prev.status)`.
- **[PF-22] [Low] The commission estimate is frozen as an entered figure** — `src/components/fixed-deposits/FdForm.tsx:123`, `src/app/dashboard/fixed-deposits/[id]/edit/page.tsx:53` — on create, a blank commission amount is saved as `rate × principal`; the edit form then pre-fills that stored number, so a later principal or rate correction leaves the commission at the old figure. _Fix:_ store null when not typed (compute on read), or track `commission_amount_entered` like the maturity amount.

**Improvements**
- `listFds` caps rows at 1000 with no paging indicator; the FD list should page like other registers.
- `syncFdAutomations` never handles a live FD whose maturity date has passed (status still `active`): its inflow stays "expected" with a past date. The list counts these as `overdue`, so add a nightly nudge to update the status.

### Portfolio Intelligence & portfolio valuation
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/portfolio-intelligence`, `GET /api/portfolio-intelligence`, `GET /api/portfolio-intelligence/drill`, `lib/portfolio/intelligence.ts`, `lib/finance/aum-persist.ts` (valuation persistence), `PortfolioDrillBar` (export route's CSV issue already known).

**Bugs**
- **[PF-23] [Low] Drill-down on the "Unknown" AMC slice returns nothing, and its % is misleading** — `src/lib/portfolio/intelligence.ts:118`, `src/app/api/portfolio-intelligence/drill/route.ts:45-50,63-72` — folios with a NULL `amc_name` are labelled "Unknown", but the drill queries `f.amc_name = 'Unknown'` → empty list. Each holder's `pct` is computed against the sum of the top 25 returned, not the slice total, so it overstates concentration. _Fix:_ treat "Unknown" as `amc_name IS NULL`; compute pct against the slice total.
- **[PF-24] [Low] Book scope is either firm or `rm_id = me`** — `src/lib/portfolio/intelligence.ts:60-62`, `drill/route.ts:39-42` — a branch head or team lead without `dashboard.view.owner` sees only clients where they personally are RM (not their branch/team), and the firm view includes closed and merged-away clients (only `deleted_at` is excluded). _Fix:_ use `investorScopeSql` and `clientRecordWhere`.

**Improvements**
- `CLASS_CASE` is copy-pasted into the drill route; import the exported one so the panel and drill can't drift.
- Name-based classification puts "Tax Advantage"/"Retirement"/"Children's" equity schemes in Hybrid (hybrid tokens are checked first); prefer `scheme_master.coarse_category` when the scheme is mapped.

### Schemes / Scheme Master / scheme shortlist / MF search
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/schemes` (SchemesCatalog: shortlist toggle, bulk, add/edit, evaluation), `/dashboard/mf-schemes`, `GET/POST /api/schemes`, `PATCH /api/schemes/[id]`, `PUT …/evaluation`, `POST /api/schemes/sync`, `POST/PATCH /api/scheme-shortlist`, `GET /api/mf/schemes`, `/api/mf/amcs`, `lib/finance/nav-feed.ts` (NAV parse).

**Bugs**
- **[PF-25] [Medium] Duplicate scheme / AMC empanelment returns a 500 instead of a 409** — `src/app/api/schemes/route.ts:94`, `src/app/api/amc-empanelments/route.ts:169-170` — both match the constraint name in `err.message`, but drizzle-orm 0.45 throws a `DrizzleQueryError` whose message is "Failed query: insert into …"; the constraint name is on `err.cause.constraint` (error-handler.ts:34-47 documents this, and `investors/[id]` and `templates` already read `cause`). Adding an AMC that's already empanelled shows "internal" with a reference id rather than "Edit the existing row instead." _Fix:_ check `err.cause?.constraint` / `err.cause?.code === "23505"`.
- **[PF-26] [Low] Scheme sync duplicates the master when AMFI renames an AMC** — `src/app/api/schemes/sync/route.ts:106-129` — `scheme_master` is keyed `(tenant_id, amc_code, scheme_code)` with `amc_code` = AMFI's AMC *name*. After a rename (e.g. IDFC → Bandhan) every scheme under it is inserted again as a new active row with the same AMFI code; the old rows stay active, so pickers list each scheme twice and shortlist flags sit on the stale copies. _Fix:_ upsert on `(tenant_id, scheme_code)` and update `amc_code`, or deactivate rows whose code reappears under another AMC.

**Improvements**
- Toggling shortlist on an already-shortlisted scheme (bulk PATCH) re-stamps `shortlisted_at/by`, losing when it was first approved; only stamp on a false→true change.

### Equity (DMAT / equity team inputs)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/equity` (EquityDmatManager), `GET/POST /api/equity/dmat`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/equity/dmat/[id]`, `POST /api/equity/inputs`, `lib/equity/service.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-27] [Low] DMAT list is open to every user; deletes are hard and unaudited; history moves when an account closes** — `src/app/api/equity/dmat/route.ts:17-24`, `src/lib/equity/service.ts:86-92,186-188` — `GET` has no capability (client names + DMAT numbers to anyone); PATCH/DELETE write no `audit_log` and DELETE is a hard delete; `activeAsOf` also requires `status <> 'closed'`, so closing an account today (or closing it without a `closed_date`) removes it from last month's and every earlier "active clients" figure. _Fix:_ gate GET with `reports.business`, audit + soft-delete, and decide "active as of" from dates only (require `closed_date` when status is closed).

**Improvements**
- `currentFiscalYear()` and `prevPeriod()` use the server's local clock; use the India date helpers.

### Reconciliation
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/reconciliation`, `ReconActions`, `ReconTable` (status, bulk, sync), `POST /api/reconciliation/run`, `GET /api/reconciliation`, `PATCH /api/reconciliation/exceptions/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/sync`, `POST …/bulk`, `lib/reconciliation/engine.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-28] [High] "Sync to CRM" books a fake purchase as today's business** — `src/app/api/reconciliation/exceptions/[id]/sync/route.ts:53-75` — the fix posts a `type: "purchase"`, `status: "processed"` transaction dated **today** with `amount = units × NAV`. `purchase` is in `GROSS_TYPES` (`lib/metrics/business.ts:38`), so syncing a ₹40 L "missing in CRM" holding adds ₹40 L of gross purchases / net business to today on the Owner, Branch and RM dashboards and the RM's targets. It also becomes the lot's cost basis at today's NAV (gains/XIRR wrong). _Fix:_ post an opening-balance type excluded from flow metrics (or a holdings snapshot), dated at the feed's as-of date, with `amount` null.
- **[PF-29] [High] Reconciliation compares registrar product codes with AMFI codes, so most holdings show up as two false breaks** — `src/lib/reconciliation/engine.ts:44-71` — the CRM leg groups `transactions.scheme_code` (the feed's registrar code, e.g. `P8015`, written raw by `processTransactionFeed`), while the CAS leg groups `folios.schemes[].schemeCode`, which the rich master/holdings importer rewrites to the AMFI code (`lib/rta/import.ts:447`, `resolveSchemeIdentity`). The FULL OUTER JOIN on `scheme_code` matches neither, so one holding becomes a `missing_in_crm` (AMFI code) plus a `missing_in_feed` (RTA code). The `scheme_nav` join also misses RTA codes, so NAV is null and severity is always "low". Using PF-28's sync on the first break then double-counts the units. _Fix:_ resolve both legs to one identity (ISIN / AMFI code via `scheme-match.ts`) before joining.
- **[PF-30] [Medium] Redemption units with a negative sign are added, not subtracted** — `src/lib/reconciliation/engine.ts:48` — `CASE WHEN direction='inflow' THEN units ELSE -units END` assumes positive units, but the feed parser keeps the registrar's sign (`cams-feed-parser.ts:302`, no `Math.abs` on units, unlike amount). A −100-unit redemption adds 100 units to the CRM leg and creates a `unit_mismatch`. `lib/finance/valuation.ts` already uses `Math.abs(units)` (the FIFO variant of this is in the known list; this is a separate consumer). _Fix:_ `ABS(units)` with the sign taken from `direction`.
- **[PF-31] [Low] Re-syncing a re-opened break posts nothing but marks it resolved** — `src/app/api/reconciliation/exceptions/[id]/sync/route.ts:74,84-90` — the dedup key is `recon-sync:<exception id>`, and a break that re-appears keeps the same id (upsert on dedup_key). A second sync for a new shortfall hits `onConflictDoNothing`, posts no units, and still sets the break `resolved` "(already posted)", so it re-opens on the next run. _Fix:_ key on exception id + feed units (or the run id), or refuse to resolve when nothing was inserted.

**Improvements**
- Both legs include trashed / merged-away clients (no `investors` join); exclude them so dead records don't generate breaks.
- A manually "resolved" break re-opens on every run while the mismatch persists (only "ignored" sticks); say so in the UI, or add "accepted difference".

### RTA imports (upload, worker, feed processors, rich importer)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/rta-imports` (+ `[id]`, exceptions), `UploadDialog`, `RtaImporter`, `RtaImportsTable` (reimport, bulk trash/restore/purge), `POST /api/rta-imports/upload(-url)`, `GET/DELETE /api/rta-imports/[id]`, `POST …/[id]/reimport`, `POST …/bulk`, `POST /api/rta/import`, `/import-async`, `/import-status`, `workers/rta-import-worker.ts`, `lib/rta/process.ts` (transaction feed, rejections, brokerage, folio master, SIP book, mandates, AUM), `lib/rta/parsers.ts`, `lib/integrations/cams-feed-parser.ts`, `lib/rta/import.ts` (rich engine), re-import idempotency.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-32] [High] A feed with no reference column overwrites earlier transactions on re-use of line numbers** — `src/lib/integrations/cams-feed-parser.ts:292`, `src/lib/rta/process.ts:352-369` — when a row has no `externalRef`, the parser invents `cams_<lineIndex>` / `kfin_<lineIndex>`, and the processor upserts `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, order_id) DO UPDATE SET folio_id, status, nav, units …`. Line 17 of Monday's file and line 17 of Tuesday's file share `order_id = 'cams_17'`, so Tuesday's row is not inserted: it rewrites Monday's transaction's status/NAV/units/folio (keeping Monday's investor, amount and date) and is counted as "imported". The registrar's own TRXNNO is also stored un-namespaced, so the same number from CAMS and KFintech, or from two AMCs, collides the same way. _Fix:_ when there is no ref, use a natural-key hash (folio, scheme, date, type, amount, units) as `dedup_key`; namespace real refs by source + AMC.
- **[PF-33] [High] PAN-less clients are keyed on name alone, which merges different people (and splits one person)** — `src/lib/rta/process.ts:117-120,316-327,1040-1050`, `src/lib/rta/import.ts:93-100` — the feed and AUM processors key every PAN-less client as `NOPAN:N<NAME>`. Two unrelated "AARAV SHARMA" minors (the commonest PAN-less case) in different families become one client record holding both children's folios, transactions and SIPs, visible to whichever RM owns it and sent in one statement. Meanwhile the rich importer keys the same person on mobile first (`NOPAN:M…`), so a master import creates a second client for someone the feed already created. _Fix:_ key PAN-less clients on folio + name (or guardian PAN + name for minors), the same way in both engines, and route ambiguous names to the merge-review queue instead of auto-joining.
- **[PF-34] [Medium] Folio-number lookups ignore the AMC, attaching rows to another client's folio** — `src/lib/rta/process.ts:62-66` (`resolveInvestorId`), `:150-154` (`buildInvestorIndex`), `:828-831` (SIP book), `:1063-1066` (AUM statement) — folio numbers are only unique per AMC (the folios key is `(tenant, amc_code, folio_number)`), but these queries match `folio_number` alone `LIMIT 1`. When two AMCs issue the same number, a PAN-less transaction, SIP or valuation row lands on the other AMC's folio and its client: one client's money appears on another's statement. _Fix:_ include `amc_code` (or the AMC name) in the lookup, and skip with an error when the match is ambiguous.
- **[PF-35] [Medium] SIP register import ignores SIP status and never ceases SIPs** — `src/lib/rta/parsers.ts:192-209,421-433`, `src/lib/rta/process.ts:873-916` — the parser reads no status/cease column, so ceased, cancelled and paused registrations in a CAMS/KFintech SIP register are all upserted `status = 'active'`. The match is restricted to `status = 'active'`, so a SIP that was completed by `completeExpiredSips` (or stopped) and is still in the register is inserted again as a duplicate active SIP. SIPs that have dropped out of the latest register are never stopped. All of these inflate the SIP book. _Fix:_ parse the status/cease-date column, upsert on folio+scheme regardless of status, and mark SIPs absent from a full register as stopped.
- **[PF-36] [Medium] SIP frequency mapping mis-reads common registrar values** — `src/lib/rta/parsers.ts:561-568` — "Half Yearly" contains "year" → `yearly` (book understated 2×); single-letter codes ("Q", "W", "H", "Y") and "Daily" fall through to `monthly` (a quarterly ₹30,000 SIP counted as ₹30,000/month, 3× overstated); "Bi-Weekly" → `weekly`. `half_yearly` is never returned although the table and `SIP_PER_MONTH` support it. _Fix:_ an explicit map covering words and codes, with `half` checked before `year` and unknowns reported as row errors.
- **[PF-37] [Low] Re-import can double-queue a running import; purge orphans the stored file** — `src/app/api/rta-imports/[id]/reimport/route.ts:50-53`, `src/app/api/rta-imports/[id]/route.ts:50-63`, `bulk/route.ts:37-55` — reimport only refuses `completed`, so a row still `uploaded`/`parsing` can be queued again (double processing, and the rejection-event path in PF-14 runs twice for new rows). Purging a failed/partial import's history row deletes `file_key` but leaves the S3 object (PANs, folios) with nothing pointing at it. Trash/restore/purge write no audit. _Fix:_ refuse while `uploaded/parsing`; delete the object on purge; audit the actions.
- **[PF-38] [Low] Folio-master (feed engine) keeps a fully-redeemed scheme's old value** — `src/lib/rta/process.ts:695-703` — `value: current?.nav ? units×nav : r.value || current?.value`; a scheme whose units drop to 0 in a file without a value column keeps its previous rupee value (0 is falsy), so the folio AUM stays at the pre-redemption figure until a NAV-priced revalue happens to match it. _Fix:_ use `r.value ?? (units === 0 ? 0 : current?.value)`.

**Improvements**
- `processTransactionFeed` stores `units`/`nav` as 0 when the column is blank (`|| 0`), and its upsert never refreshes `amount`/`order_date` when the registrar restates a row; store null and include amount in the update.
- `processSipRejectionReport`, `processSipBook` and `processMandateStatus` still do 1–3 queries per row (no `buildInvestorIndex`); large registers are slow inside the single long tx.
- The rich import via `/api/rta/import-async` creates no `rta_file_imports` row and no `audit_log` entry, so a client-master import leaves no history.

### Product offerings & AMC empanelments
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/product-offerings` (+ `/new`, `/[id]`), `/dashboard/amc-empanelments` (+ `/new`, `/[id]`), `ProductOfferingForm`, `AmcEmpanelmentForm`, `GET/POST /api/product-offerings`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/product-offerings/[id]`, `GET/POST /api/amc-empanelments`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/amc-empanelments/[id]`.

**Bugs**
- **[PF-39] [Low] Product offering dates are unvalidated and reactivation keeps the discontinued date** — `src/app/api/product-offerings/route.ts:65-66`, `[id]/route.ts:63-64,123-133` — `launchedAt`/`discontinuedAt` are free strings (a non-date → DB cast error → 500), and PATCHing `status: "active"` on a discontinued offering leaves `discontinued_at` set, so it reads as both active and discontinued. `GET /api/product-offerings/[id]` needs `compliance.edit` while the list is open to everyone, which breaks the detail page for sales. _Fix:_ validate yyyy-mm-dd, clear `discontinuedAt` on reactivation, align the GET gate with the list.

**Improvements**
- The duplicate-AMC 409 path is broken (PF-25).
- `teamMemberIds` / `linkedRegistrationIds` aren't checked to belong to this firm.

---

## Planning, proposals & advice

### Proposals
_Checked:_ /dashboard/proposals (list, new, [id], [id]/edit, [id]/print, [id]/recommendation); API GET/POST /api/proposals, GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/proposals/[id], POST [id]/send, POST [id]/revise, GET [id]/pdf, POST bulk, GET current, GET snapshot, POST internal/proposals/mark-expired; public /p/[token] + POST /api/public/proposals/{accept,request-change,viewed}, GET pdf; lib/proposals (journey, public-link, projections, items, execution), lib/proposal/risk-alignment; ProposalBuilder / RecommendStep / ProposalStatusActions / ProposalsBulkTable.

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-01] [High] "Mark as Sent" / "Mark Accepted" skip every suitability gate** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:49-50,117-190` (UI `src/components/proposals/ProposalStatusActions.tsx:51-53,93-112`) — the hard riskometer block (owner override only) and the mandatory suitability note live only in `POST [id]/send`. PATCH accepts `status:"sent"` (the "Mark as Sent" button on any draft/ready proposal) and `status:"accepted"` straight from `draft`, then advances the lead/opportunity journey and starts onboarding. Scenario: a very-high-risk small-cap proposal for a conservative client is blocked by Send → RM clicks "Mark as Sent", then "Mark Accepted" → journey Won, opportunity accepted, no note, no override. Distinct from the send-route gap already known. _Fix:_ run the same alignment + note gate in PATCH for any transition to sent/viewed/accepted/partially_approved, and allow `accepted` only from sent/viewed/changes_requested.
- **[PLN-02] [High] An accepted or executed proposal can be reopened and rewritten** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:49-50,107-113` — PATCH allows any status → any status, and the content freeze checks only `before.status`. Sending `{status:"draft"}` on an accepted proposal succeeds, and a second PATCH with `products` is then allowed. That overwrites the exact lines the client signed (`proposal_approvals` still points at the row), which is what Revise exists to prevent. _Fix:_ use an explicit transition table; accepted/partially_approved/executed/superseded are terminal apart from executed.
- **[PLN-03] [Medium] Delete and bulk delete turn accepted or executed proposals into "rejected"** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:227-240`, `src/app/api/proposals/bulk/route.ts:44-53` — there is no status guard, so an accepted proposal's evidence is relabelled rejected with `rejection_reason='deleted'`. Open approval tokens are not revoked, and the opportunity is never told (unlike PATCH→rejected, which calls `notifyOpportunity("declined")`). _Fix:_ refuse delete for accepted/partially_approved/executed, revoke open tokens, and call notifyOpportunity.
- **[PLN-04] [Medium] A client can accept a version the RM has just replaced** — `src/app/api/public/proposals/accept/route.ts:70-144`, `src/lib/share-tokens/index.ts:170-178` — the status and revocation checks run before the claim. `claimShareToken` checks only `used_at IS NULL`, not `revoked_at`, and the final `UPDATE proposals SET status='accepted'` has no status predicate. If Revise commits between `loadProposalLink` and the claim, the superseded v1 becomes `accepted` and the journey moves to Won while v2 sits as a draft. request-change has the same race. _Fix:_ claim with `revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now()`, and update `WHERE status IN ('sent','viewed')`, aborting on 0 rows.
- **[PLN-05] [Medium] A family proposal ignores the family member the RM picked** — `src/app/api/proposals/route.ts:252-262` (builder sends `investorId=familyMemberId`, `ProposalBuilder.tsx:238-241`) — the family branch always overwrites `resolvedInvestorId` with `families.primaryInvestorId` and uses the head's email and phone. The send route's suitability gate therefore checks the head's risk profile, execution matching looks at the head's transactions, and the email goes to the head. _Fix:_ keep `body.investorId` when it is a member of the family; fall back to the head only when it is absent.
- **[PLN-06] [Medium] Saved totals ignore the SIP step-up shown on each line** — `src/lib/proposals/projections.ts:25-33,51-64` vs `src/components/proposals/builder/RecommendStep.tsx:242-251` — the builder projects each SIP line with annual step-up, but `proposalTotals` (stored as `expected_corpus` / `total_investment` and printed in the email, WhatsApp text and PDF headline) uses a flat `fvSip` and `monthly×12×years`. Scenario: ₹10k SIP with 10% step-up, 15 years at 12%: the line shows about ₹79L and the "Projected corpus" headline about ₹50L. Total investment is understated the same way. _Fix:_ use `fvStepUpSip` and the stepped contribution sum in `lineItemFutureValue` / `lineItemInvestment`.
- **[PLN-07] [Medium] Re-sending a proposal or goal plan by email fails for 24 hours** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/send/route.ts:361`, `src/app/api/goals/[id]/email/route.ts` (`idempotencyKey: goal:${id}`) — the key is fixed per record, but the payload changes on every send: a new token URL each time, and a PDF with a fresh "Generated" stamp. Resend rejects a reused key with a different body (409), so "send again" returns a 502 with the raw provider error. On the proposal path the new share token is still minted and committed. _Fix:_ add the token id (proposal) or a send nonce or timestamp (goal) to the key.
- **[PLN-08] [Medium] The suitability check knows four riskometer levels; the builder stores six** — `src/lib/proposal/risk-alignment.ts:18-26,118-131` vs `src/app/api/proposals/route.ts:43` and `RecommendStep.tsx:362-368` — `low_to_moderate` and `moderately_high` are never in `ALLOWED`, and `riskGap` returns NaN for them. Every such scheme is a "warn" regardless of the client: a conservative client gets "low_to_moderate-risk scheme is above a conservative profile", and a moderate client is warned on moderately-high schemes. The banner, send gate and summary count false mismatches, which teaches RMs to ignore the warning. _Fix:_ map all six AMFI levels (1–6) and grade the gap on that scale.
- **[PLN-09] [Medium] "Executed" credits purchases the proposal did not cause** — `src/lib/proposals/execution.ts:113-131` — any successful purchase or SIP instalment in a scheme with a matching name, from acceptance −1 day to +365 days, counts. That includes instalments of a SIP registered years earlier in the same fund. The same transaction can also be credited to two accepted proposals for the same client, since `used` is per proposal. Proposals are marked executed and the RM is told "your client has invested" when nothing new happened. _Fix:_ exclude SIP instalments whose `sips.start_date` is before acceptance, and dedupe transactions across proposals.
- **[PLN-10] [Medium] An RM can create a proposal for any client in the firm** — `src/app/api/proposals/route.ts:221-233` — POST loads the investor or lead with RLS only (tenant), while `GET /api/proposals/snapshot` correctly uses `loadViewableInvestor` / `leadScopeCondition`. A user with `proposal.write` can post a colleague's client id, get their email and phone copied onto a proposal they own, and send it. _Fix:_ authorise the subject with `loadViewableInvestor` / `leadScopeCondition` before snapshotting.
- **[PLN-11] [Low] Proposal and plan numbers can be issued twice** — `src/app/api/proposals/route.ts:174-187`, `src/app/api/financial-plans/route.ts:100-113` — numbers are `MAX()+1` with no lock and no unique index (`proposals_number_idx` is a plain index). Two concurrent creates get the same PROP-/FP- number. The year is also server UTC. _Fix:_ a per-tenant sequence or advisory lock, plus a unique index (tenant, number, version).
- **[PLN-12] [Low] A renewed proposal never expires again** — `src/app/api/internal/proposals/mark-expired/route.ts:31-34` — the job requires `is_expired = false` and sets it true. Renew (`ProposalsBulkTable.tsx:80-95` → PATCH `validUntil` + status) never resets it, so after one renewal the proposal stays "sent" past its new date. _Fix:_ clear `is_expired` / `expired_at` in PATCH when `validUntil` moves into the future.
- **[PLN-13] [Low] The "viewed" beacon can overwrite a newer status** — `src/app/api/public/proposals/viewed/route.ts:248-265` — `p.status` and `p.viewedAt` are read outside the transaction, then written unconditionally. An open landing just after an accept commits sets `status='viewed'` over `accepted`, and two quick opens send two "opened your proposal" notifications. _Fix:_ `UPDATE … SET status = CASE WHEN status='sent' THEN 'viewed' ELSE status END, viewed_at = COALESCE(viewed_at, now())` and notify only on `RETURNING` of the first set.
- **[PLN-14] [Low] PATCH stores product lines without validation, and the email HTML is unescaped** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:43` and `send/route.ts:83,126-130` — POST validates lines with the `LineItem` union and `cleanProductItem`; PATCH (every builder save after the first) takes `z.array(z.any())`. `buildHtmlEmail` interpolates `recipientName`, `title`, `coverLetter` and scheme names raw into HTML. _Fix:_ reuse `ProductsField` in PATCH, and escape the interpolations.
- **[PLN-15] [Low] A proposal stops accepting answers at 05:30 IST on its "valid till" day** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/route.ts:132`, `src/lib/proposals/public-link.ts:271` — `new Date("2026-11-02")` is UTC midnight, so the client is refused from 05:30 IST on the date the email promised. A PATCH with `validUntil: ""` reaches Drizzle as `""` and returns 500. _Fix:_ store end-of-day IST, and treat `""` as null.

**Improvements**
- Record a `proposal_approvals` row (channel `in_person` / `phone`, actor) when the RM marks a proposal accepted, so CRM-recorded acceptances carry evidence like the link path.
- The WhatsApp send flips the proposal to "sent" and mints a token even if the RM never opens the wa.me link; flip it on a confirm instead.
- The /p page shows the accept form for proposals marked rejected or accepted in the CRM (token unused) and only fails on submit; render the read-only state from `proposal.status` too.
- `suitability_matrix_entries` (and the MF-selection allocation hints) are never consulted by the builder or the send gate; surface the matching cell in RecommendStep.

### Risk profiles, risk-profile template and the public questionnaire (/r)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/risk-profiles (list, new wizard), /settings template editor; API GET/POST /api/risk-profiles, POST send-link, bulk-assign, bulk-notify, otp/send, otp/verify, GET [id]/pdf, [id]/document; GET/PUT /api/risk-profile-template, POST reset; public /r/[token], POST /api/public/risk-profile/{submit,otp/send}, GET pdf; lib/risk-profile (template, apply, bulk-age-assign, buckets), lib/comms/client-links risk/suitability fill.

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-16] [High] The client is shown the firm's first template version but scored against the latest** — `src/app/r/[token]/page.tsx:52-56` vs `src/app/api/public/risk-profile/submit/route.ts:200-205` — the page orders `risk_profile_templates` by `version_number` ascending and takes row 0 (v1, and ignores `is_active`). Submit loads `ORDER BY version_number DESC LIMIT 1`. After any edit or Reset (each publishes a new version), clients answer v1's questions. If keys changed, submit fails with "Missing answers for: …" and the link is unusable. If only options or scores changed, the client is scored on options they never saw. _Fix:_ load the template in /r with `loadActiveTemplate` (desc, `is_active`), and store the version id on the response.
- **[PLN-17] [High] A questionnaire taken with the RM leaves the client "provisional"** — `src/app/api/risk-profiles/route.ts:183-192` — the RM wizard save (OTP- or signature-verified) updates `risk_profile`, score and signed_at but never sets `risk_profile_provisional = false`; the public submit does (`submit/route.ts:270`). An age-suggested client who then completes the full questionnaire still reads "Awaiting confirmation":
  - the opportunity MF-proposal gate stays blocked (`lib/opportunities/journey.ts:162`);
  - bulk-notify (`bulk-notify/route.ts:68`) and send-link keep mailing "confirm your suggested profile";
  - dashboards count them as provisional;
  - `planAgeAssignment(refreshProvisional)` (`lib/risk-profile/bulk-age-assign.ts:85-90`) silently overwrites the signed answer with an age band.
  _Fix:_ set `riskProfileProvisional: false` (and clear the provisional source) in the POST update.
- **[PLN-18] [Medium] Custom band ids are saved as "moderate"** — `src/app/api/public/risk-profile/submit/route.ts:225-227`, `src/app/api/risk-profiles/route.ts:102-104` — the editor accepts any band id (`template.ts:51`, `z.string().max(40)`), but anything outside the four canonical ids is written to `investors.risk_profile` as `moderate`. Scenario: a firm defines "very_conservative" / "growth" / "high_growth" bands; every client is stored as moderate, and the suitability gate, equity band and client emails use the wrong category. _Fix:_ require band ids from the canonical enum in PUT (`validateBands`), or store an explicit category mapping per band.
- **[PLN-19] [Medium] A lead's questionnaire submission can move its journey backwards** — `src/app/api/public/risk-profile/submit/route.ts:276-298` — it sets `leads.stage='risk_profiled'` unconditionally and calls `transitionTo(…, 'risk_profiled', { systemBypass: true })`. A lead already at Proposal Sent, Won or onboarding (e.g. re-profiled, or a second link) is pulled back to Risk Profiled. The proposals flow is explicitly forward-only. _Fix:_ use `autoAdvance(..., { forwardOnly: true })` and skip the stage write when the lead is past it.
- **[PLN-20] [Medium] The RM-path "client_otp" evidence can be produced without the client** — `src/app/api/risk-profiles/otp/send/route.ts` (needEmail branch, ~L70-80), `src/app/api/risk-profiles/route.ts:133-145` — when the client has no email on file, the RM types any address (their own) and verifies the code themselves. The profile is then saved as `verification_method='client_otp'`, and the verified email is not recorded on the response (the public route stores `verifiedEmail`). A verified OTP row also never goes stale: it stays usable for a profile submitted weeks later. _Fix:_ persist `verifiedEmail` / `emailVerifiedAt` on the response, require `verifiedAt` within e.g. 30 min, and save a typed email to the client record only after verification (or label it "rm_supplied_email").
- **[PLN-21] [Medium] An age-suggested category is presented to the client as their risk profile** — `src/lib/comms/client-links.ts:165-175,261-262`, `src/lib/investors/suitability.ts:29-36` — `resolveCanonicalProfile` ignores `risk_profile_provisional`, so `{{risk.category}}`, the band text and `suitability.*` ("your equity is above your Aggressive profile") go out for a category the client never answered. `assessSuitability` likewise treats it as signed on the client page and in the proposal snapshot. This contradicts "risk category is never inferred from age". _Fix:_ return null / "provisional" from `resolveCanonicalProfile` when provisional, and give `assessSuitability` a provisional state.
- **[PLN-22] [Low] The "signature" sign-off accepts any string as the signed form** — `src/app/api/risk-profiles/route.ts:123-130` — `documentUrl` is not checked to be an uploaded key in this tenant's KYC prefix, so posting `documentUrl:"x"` records `client_physical` with no document behind it. _Fix:_ validate the key prefix (tenant / `risk-profile-signed`) and that the object exists.
- **[PLN-23] [Low] A client can hold several live questionnaire links** — `src/app/api/client-forms/send/route.ts:146-153` — this route always mints a new token instead of reusing a live unused one (the rule `client-links.ts` follows). Each link can be submitted once, so a client can file two different answers. _Fix:_ reuse the live token via `loadClientLinkTokens` / `liveLink`.

**Improvements**
- Template PUT is gated on `settings.users`; a dedicated `risk_profile.template` / compliance capability fits better.
- Record `template_version` on `risk_profile_responses` (the outbox meta has it only on the RM path) so an audit can show which questions were answered.

### Goals and goal planning
_Checked:_ /dashboard/goals (list, new, [id], [id]/print); API GET/POST /api/goals, GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/goals/[id], GET [id]/full, POST [id]/email, POST [id]/recompute, POST bulk; lib/goal-planning (calc, stored, recompute, glide-path, goal-pdf); GoalCreateForm, GoalDetailCalculator, LinkedInvestmentsPicker, CreateGoalFromCalc.

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-24] [Medium] The year-by-year projection ends short of the target it is supposed to reach** — `src/lib/goal-planning/calc.ts` `projectYearly` (`corpus = corpus*(1+m) + monthlySip`) — this is a month-end annuity. The required SIP it is fed (`requiredSip` / `fvSip`) is annuity-due. The table, bar chart, print page and client PDF (`goal-pdf.ts:77`, `goals/[id]/print/page.tsx:79`, `GoalDetailCalculator.tsx:90-98`) therefore show the "required" plan finishing about (1+m)⁻¹ of target. Example: the required SIP for ₹1 Cr in 10 years at 12% shows a final corpus of about ₹99.0L, about 99% of target. _Fix:_ `corpus = (corpus + monthlySip) * (1 + m)`.
- **[PLN-25] [Medium] The goal PDF and print page disagree with the goal screen** — `src/lib/goal-planning/goal-pdf.ts:63-77`, `src/app/dashboard/goals/[id]/print/page.tsx:66-76` — both call `computeGoal` with raw `expectedReturn`, ignoring the goal's glide path. They also default step-up to 10% when `stepUpPct` is null. The list, detail and nightly job use `computeStoredGoal` (glide-tapered). A goal 3 years out on a conservative glide therefore shows one required SIP and status in the CRM and a lower SIP and a rosier status in the PDF emailed to the client. _Fix:_ build PDF and print inputs through `computeStoredGoal`.
- **[PLN-26] [Low] Editing a goal resets a status set by hand** — `src/app/api/goals/[id]/route.ts:153` — when any calc input changes and no status is posted, `patch.status = calc.status`. An archived (`abandoned`, the "Archive"/delete action) or `on_hold` goal is flipped back to on_track or behind by an edit, while the nightly recompute deliberately preserves these. _Fix:_ skip the status overwrite when `before.status` is on_hold or abandoned.
- **[PLN-27] [Low] Linked folios and SIPs are not checked against the goal's client** — `src/app/api/goals/[id]/route.ts:44-62` — any folio or SIP uuid in the tenant is accepted. The nightly recompute (`recompute.ts:95-135`) then adds another client's AUM and SIP into this goal's progress. _Fix:_ verify the ids belong to `goal.investorId` before saving.
- **[PLN-28] [Low] The cached required SIP ignores step-up** — `src/app/api/goals/route.ts:167`, `src/lib/goal-planning/recompute.ts:155` — `required_monthly_sip` is always the flat figure even when `stepUpPct > 0`. The detail screen meanwhile projects with `requiredStepUpInitialSip`, so the list, alerts and the goal-drift recipe show a different "required SIP" from the plan. _Fix:_ store `requiredStepUpInitialSip` when step-up > 0, or store both.
- **[PLN-29] [Low] "Create goal" from a calculator carries nothing over, and its Inflation field does nothing** — `src/components/calculators/CalculatorsApp.tsx:540-545`, `src/components/calculators/CreateGoalFromCalc.tsx:146-158` — no `prefill` is passed, so the modal opens at ₹0 / 10 years whatever was calculated. It posts `targetMode:"future"`, so the Inflation % the user edits is ignored and the amount is taken as already in future rupees. _Fix:_ pass the calculator's target and years, and post `targetMode:"today"` with `presentTargetAmount` (or drop the inflation field).

**Improvements**
- Goals and proposals use a nominal r/12 monthly rate (₹10k for 10 years at 12% is ₹23.2L); every calculator uses an effective rate (₹22.4L). The same scenario differs about 3.5% between the Calculators page and the goal or proposal it becomes. Pick one convention, or label it.
- The glide path applies today's tapered return to the whole remaining horizon, so a goal 15 years out projects as if it is never de-risked. Simulate year by year instead.
- `GoalCreateForm` / `GoalDetailCalculator` send `new Date(year, 11, 31).toISOString().slice(0,10)`, which is 30 Dec in IST. Build the date string directly.

### Financial plans
_Checked:_ /dashboard/financial-plans (list, new / edit builder, [id], [id]/print); API GET/POST /api/financial-plans, GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/financial-plans/[id]; lib/financial-plans (compute, analysis, from-fact-find).

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-30] [Medium] A finalized plan can be rewritten or deleted without trace** — `src/app/api/financial-plans/[id]/route.ts` PATCH / DELETE — PATCH does not check `status='finalized'`, so goals, recommendations and the asset allocation of a plan already delivered to the client are overwritten in place. The audit row stores only `{status}`, so the earlier advice cannot be reconstructed. DELETE is a hard `tx.delete` of the advice record. Proposals got Revise for exactly this reason. _Fix:_ freeze finalized plans (edit means a new version with `parent_plan_id`), soft-delete, and audit the before/after content.
- **[PLN-31] [Low] A client whose EMIs exceed income cannot be saved** — `src/app/api/financial-plans/route.ts:92-93`, `[id]/route.ts` UpdateBody — the builder (`new/page.tsx:232-235`) computes `emiToIncomePct = totalEMI/income×100`, which can exceed 100 for a debt-stressed client. `z.number().max(100)` rejects it and the RM sees only "invalid body". _Fix:_ drop the max (or clamp), and surface the field issue.
- **[PLN-32] [Low] Unknown income becomes "0% savings (Low)"** — `src/app/dashboard/financial-plans/new/page.tsx:223-236` — with no income entered, `savingsRate` and `emiToIncomePct` are 0 and are saved and shown as real figures. The builder's savings rate also excludes EMIs while `cfpRatios` (`analysis.ts:147-151`) subtracts them, so the persisted and displayed savings rates differ for the same plan. _Fix:_ send null when income is missing, and use one definition (`cfpRatios`).

**Improvements**
- `emergencyStatus` adds the earmarked emergency fund to all "cash" assets; if the RM also lists that fund as a cash asset it is counted twice.
- Plan visibility is `prepared_by_user_id` only, so the client's own RM cannot open a plan a colleague prepared for their client; scope through `investorScopeSql`.
- Recommended allocation percentages are not validated to sum to 100.

### Calculators
_Checked:_ src/lib/calculators — sip360, stepup-reckoner, swp-comprehensive (verified the ₹50L / 9% / 7% reference → ₹29,167/mo, ₹75.19L), stp-comprehensive, emi-sip (verified ₹25L / 9% / 15y → EMI ₹25,357), xirr, real-return, fire, nps, child-education, hlv, tax-regime; goal-planning calc (fvSip ₹10k / 12% / 10y = ₹23.23L annuity-due ✓, retirementCorpus, swpDeplete, stpCalculate, costOfDelay, cagr, vacationPlan, opmHouseStrategy); CalculatorsApp / CreateGoalFromCalc.

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-33] [Medium] The new-regime tax ignores the 87A marginal relief** — `src/lib/calculators/tax-regime.ts:105-110` — the rebate is all-or-nothing at ₹12L taxable. Under FY 2025-26 rules, tax for income just above ₹12L cannot exceed the excess over ₹12L. Example: salaried, ₹12.85L gross → ₹12.10L taxable. The code shows ₹61,500 + cess = ₹63,960; the correct figure is about ₹10,400. The regime comparison and the "invest the saving" SIP are wrong in this band. _Fix:_ after the rebate, cap new-regime tax at `taxable − 12,00,000` when taxable is just above ₹12L (and add marginal relief on surcharge).
- **[PLN-34] [Medium] The NPS tax benefit assumes the full ₹50,000 whatever the client contributes** — `src/lib/calculators/nps.ts` (`annual80CCD1BBenefit = 50000 × slab`) — a client putting in ₹2,000/month (₹24k/year) is shown tax saved on ₹50k. 80CCD(1B) is also not available in the new regime, which most salaried clients now use. _Fix:_ `min(annual contribution, 50000) × slab × 1.04`, and 0 or a flag under the new regime.
- **[PLN-35] [Low] The old regime takes 80D and employer-NPS deductions uncapped** — `src/lib/calculators/tax-regime.ts:88-96` — `section80D` and `employerNps80CCD2` are added as typed (the 80D caps are ₹25k / ₹50k / ₹1L; 80CCD(2) is 10–14% of salary), so a typo inflates the old-regime saving. _Fix:_ cap them, or validate in the form.
- **[PLN-36] [Low] Edge-case results in the goal-planning helpers** — `src/lib/goal-planning/calc.ts` — `swpDeplete` leaves `monthsBeforeDepletion` null when the corpus runs out in the final month. `stpCalculate` clamps `gain` to ≥ 0, hiding a loss. `cagrCalculate` returns NaN for a final value ≤ 0. _Fix:_ set depletion inside the loop when balance hits 0, return a signed gain, and guard `finalValue <= 0`.

**Improvements**
- `computeChildEducation.netAfterSavings` grows savings only to the nearest milestone while the per-milestone offsets grow to each date, so the summary and the table disagree.
- The PDF sanitiser strips all non-ASCII even with DejaVu embedded, so a Devanagari client name prints blank on goal, proposal and calculator PDFs.

### Client forms (send a form link)
_Checked:_ POST /api/client-forms/send (risk_profile, document_collection, financial_discovery, portfolio_view); lib/client-forms/templates.

**Bugs**
- **[PLN-37] [Medium] The email bypasses the consent and compliance gate and will send any URL** — `src/app/api/client-forms/send/route.ts:142-197` — the header says it goes "through the same comms pipeline", but the email path calls `emailAdapter.send` directly. It skips `dispatch()`, consent (an opted-out client still gets it), the compliance gate and the `communications` audit row. The body's optional `url` (any `z.string().url()`) is emailed as the firm-branded CTA unchanged, so any user with `investor.edit.assigned` can send an arbitrary link from the firm's mailbox. _Fix:_ route the email through `dispatch()` (or `gateClientSend`), and accept `url` only if it resolves to a live share token for this subject.
- **[PLN-38] [Medium] The WhatsApp send can pick another form's template** — `src/app/api/client-forms/send/route.ts:59-91,222-228` — the first approved WA template whose key or name matches a loose regex is used. `financial_discovery`'s `/discovery|health|fact.?find|profile/` matches `risk_profile_request`, so a health-check link goes out under "please complete your risk profile". When no variable is named link or url, `varKeys[0]` (often the name slot) gets the URL. _Fix:_ match templates by an exact key per kind (from `ensureClientFormTemplate`), and fail closed when no link variable exists.
- **[PLN-39] [Medium] The wa.me fallback link is broken for ordinary 10-digit numbers** — `src/app/api/client-forms/send/route.ts:268` — it only strips non-digits, so a stored `9876543210` becomes `wa.me/9876543210`, which WhatsApp reads as country code 98. The proposal send and `SendRiskProfileLinkButton` prepend 91. This fallback is the main path whenever the 24-hour window is closed. _Fix:_ reuse the shared India normaliser (prepend 91 for 10-digit numbers starting 6–9).
- **[PLN-40] [Medium] There is no book check on who the form goes to** — `src/app/api/client-forms/send/route.ts:110-114` (also `src/app/api/risk-profiles/route.ts:110` and `risk-profiles/otp/send/route.ts`) — the subject is loaded by tenant only. Any user with `communication.send` or `investor.edit.assigned` can mint and send portfolio-view or risk links (the portfolio link is 180 days and exposes holdings) for a colleague's client, and an RM can file a risk profile for a client outside their book. _Fix:_ authorise with `loadViewableInvestor` / `leadScopeCondition` first.

**Improvements**
- Return which WhatsApp template was used (key) so the RM can see why a message said what it said.

### Suitability matrix and MF selection
_Checked:_ /dashboard/reports/suitability-matrix, /dashboard/reports/mf-selection-criteria; GET/PUT /api/suitability-matrix, POST /api/suitability-matrix/prefill; lib/mf-selection (criteria, suitability-defaults); lib/investors/suitability.

**Bugs**
- No bugs found (beyond PLN-08 and PLN-21, which affect how suitability is applied elsewhere).

**Improvements**
- The matrix's `low/moderate/high` vocabulary does not map to the client categories (`conservative/moderate/aggressive/very_aggressive`), and nothing in the proposal flow reads the matrix. Add a mapping and show the matching cell in the builder.
- `allocationHints` is not validated to sum to 100 on PUT.
- The equity bands in `lib/investors/suitability.ts` are hard-coded; make them the firm's matrix or settings so they agree with what the IC published.

---

## Insurance & claims

### Insurance — policy register, policy API, policy form

_Checked:_ `/dashboard/insurance` (register), `/dashboard/insurance/new` (create + `?edit=`), `/dashboard/insurance/[id]`, `/dashboard/insurance/protection-gap`, `/dashboard/insurance/overview` (redirect); `GET/POST /api/insurance`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/insurance/[id]`, `POST /api/insurance/[id]/renew`; RowActions "Cancel"; AI extract → applyDraft.

**Bugs**
- **[INS-01] [High] Editing any policy always fails with "invalid body"** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/new/page.tsx:489` + `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/route.ts:27-58` — `save()` builds one payload for create and edit that always carries `investorId`, and PATCH's schema is `.strict()` with no `investorId` key. Zod rejects the unknown key, so every "Edit Policy → Save" returns 400. Users cannot change status, the next due date, the premium or the nominee from the UI. That also means a lapse or surrender never reaches `audit_log` through the form, so the dashboard's lost-business history is never written. _Fix:_ omit `investorId` on edit, or accept it in `Patch` and ignore or validate it.
- **[INS-02] [Medium] Policy detail page has no capability gate and no book scope** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/[id]/page.tsx:55-66` — the page only calls `requireSession()` and loads the policy by id. The register and `GET /api/insurance/[id]` both require `insurance.view` and limit non-managers to their own book. Here any signed-in user (marketing, service team, an RM from another book) who has a policy id sees the nominee name, DOB and share, the premium, the sum assured and the client. Policy ids show up in links from claims, search and drawers. _Fix:_ add `requireCap("insurance.view")` and the same sold-by / client-RM / `seesWholeInsuranceBook` check the API uses.
- **[INS-03] [Medium] `insurance.view.all` (Insurance Head) is honoured only by the register** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/route.ts:65`, `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/renew/route.ts:41`, `src/app/api/insurance/route.ts:76`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/renewals/page.tsx:29`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/commission/page.tsx:23` — these use `investor.view.all || compliance.view` instead of `seesWholeInsuranceBook()`. An Insurance Head given the grant sees every policy in the register and on the dashboard. But Edit loads 404, "Mark renewed" returns 403 "isn't in your book", the Renewals and Commission pages show only their own book, and the claim form's policy picker (`GET /api/insurance`) cannot list the firm's policies. _Fix:_ use `seesWholeInsuranceBook(session)` everywhere the policy book is scoped.
- **[INS-04] [Medium] "Mark renewed" shifts the policy anniversary when the premium is paid in grace** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/renew/route.ts:62` — next due = `GREATEST(next_premium_due_date, current_date) + period`. A policy due 1 Sep and paid on 20 Sep (inside the 30-day grace) is moved to 20 Sep next year, and every later reminder, lapse check and retention cohort drifts. The insurer's due date stays 1 Sep. _Fix:_ advance from `next_premium_due_date` (loop until it is after today only for a policy that is truly past grace), not from today.
- **[INS-05] [Medium] Renew accepts any status and any due date** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/renew/route.ts:47-67` — there is no status guard. A cancelled, matured or surrendered policy is set back to `status='active'` with a fresh due date, and so are proposed and in-underwriting ones. A `claim_filed` policy loses its claim status too. Nothing stops a renewal when the due date is already a year or more ahead, or is past `maturity_date` or the premium-paying term. _Fix:_ allow renewal only from active / lapsed / claim_* statuses, and refuse when the due date is more than one period ahead or past maturity or PPT.
