- **[INS-06] [Low] Policy create accepts a soft-deleted client and an unvalidated seller** — `src/app/api/insurance/route.ts:100-108,147` — the client check ignores `deleted_at` and `merged_into_id`. A policy keyed on a trashed client disappears from the register and the dashboards, which exclude deleted clients. `soldByUserId` from the body is not checked against the tenant's users, and it decides the sales credit through `ownerSql`. _Fix:_ require a live client, and check the seller is an active user of this tenant.
- **[INS-07] [Low] Policy PATCH cannot clear a field, and date strings are unvalidated** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/route.ts:27-58` — every field is `.optional()` and none is `.nullable()`, so a wrong next-due or maturity date can never be removed. `z.string()` dates such as "31/02/2026" reach Postgres and return a 500. The same applies to the dates in POST. _Fix:_ use `.nullable()` plus an ISO-date regex.
- **[INS-08] [Low] New-policy form defaults Policy start to today, in UTC** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/new/page.tsx:171` — `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` gives yesterday between 00:00 and 05:30 IST. For a policy "the client already owns", the default makes it count as business booked today (booked-on = start date, nature = new when Business type is left blank). It inflates premium booked for the period. _Fix:_ leave the field blank, or default to the IST date and require it to be confirmed.
- **[INS-09] [Low] The detail page's type labels are missing every commercial line** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/[id]/page.tsx:20-33` — it keeps a local `TYPE_LABEL` with 12 types, so fire, marine, burglary, WC, GPA and GHI show as raw keys. _Fix:_ use `policyTypeLabel()` from `lib/insurance/book.ts`.

**Improvements**
- Show the commission, the claims on the policy and a "Mark renewed" action on `/dashboard/insurance/[id]`. Today the page has no way to renew, or to see the claims against the policy.
- Offer a "Lapse / surrender reason" field in the form. `lapse_reason` drives the dashboard's loss-reason split, but it can only be set through the API.
- Warn on a duplicate `policy_number` within the tenant on create, as the import already does.
- Suggest the next due date from start date + frequency when it is left blank. A blank one silently drops the policy out of every reminder.

### Insurance — renewals, premium reminders, lapse

_Checked:_ `/dashboard/insurance/renewals` (buckets, RenewAction), `daily-ops-scanner` (`insurance.premium_due`, `insurance.lapsed`, `insurance.protection_gap`), recipes `insurance_premium_reminder` and `insurance_lapse_recovery`, `/dashboard/insurance/protection-gap`.

**Bugs**
- **[INS-10] [High] A policy with a claim drops out of every reminder and lapse check, permanently when the claim is rejected** — `src/lib/insurance/claim-status.ts:5-14`, `src/app/api/insurance-claims/route.ts:121-133`, `src/app/api/insurance-claims/[id]/route.ts:107-119`, consumers `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:846,884,754`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/renewals/page.tsx:52,58`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/page.tsx:177,242`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/protection-gap/page.tsx:27`.
  - What happens: any claim moves the policy to `claim_filed`, and a settled claim to `claim_settled`. A rejected or withdrawn claim leaves the policy on `claim_filed` forever, because `policyStatusForClaim` returns null.
  - Who misses it: the premium-due reminders, the lapse alert, the protection-gap scan, the Renewals page and the register's "Premium due (30d)" all select `status = 'active'` only. The dashboards do count `claim_*` as in force.
  - Example: a family-floater health policy with a settled hospital claim in month 3 never gets its renewal reminder. Its lapse is never alerted, and the protection-gap page flags the client as "no health cover".
  - The opposite error: a settled death claim leaves a life policy "in force", so it shows as an overdue renewal on the Insurance dashboard.
  - _Fix:_ use `STATUS_IN_FORCE` in every renewal and reminder consumer. Return the policy to `active` when a claim is rejected or withdrawn, and do not keep a settled death claim in force.
- **[INS-11] [Medium] Renewals page money is the annual premium, not the installment due** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/renewals/page.tsx:50,56,106` — "due premium" sums `annual_premium`, and the Premium column prints it, for monthly, quarterly and half-yearly policies. A ₹1.2 L/yr monthly policy shows ₹1,20,000 due this week when ₹10,000 is due, 12× the real figure. The dashboard's renewal buckets use `installmentSql`, so the two screens disagree. _Fix:_ use `installmentSql("p")` for the due amount and label the annual figure separately.
- **[INS-12] [Low] Only one premium reminder per client per day** — `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:837-849` — `DISTINCT ON (p.investor_id)` keeps the soonest-due policy. A client whose motor policy is 7 days out and whose health policy is 15 days out on the same day gets no 15-day nudge for the health policy. The event's `premium` is also the annual premium, not the installment. _Fix:_ emit one event per policy, keyed on policy id in the dedup, and pass the installment.
- **[INS-13] [Low] Renewal dates use the DB's `current_date` (UTC)** — `src/app/api/insurance/[id]/renew/route.ts:62-63`, `renewals/page.tsx:17-22`, scanner `:849,887` — from 00:00 to 05:30 IST, `last_premium_date` is stamped as yesterday and the buckets and milestones are a day off. _Fix:_ use `(now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata')::date`.
- **[INS-14] [Low] "Mark renewed" fails silently** — `src/components/insurance/RenewAction.tsx:24-25` — a 403 (other book), a 400 (single-pay) or a 500 just re-enables the button with no message. _Fix:_ show `toast.error(j.error)`.

**Improvements**
- The Renewals page excludes policies of soft-deleted clients nowhere (`LEFT JOIN investors` with no `deleted_at` filter), unlike the register. Align the two.
- Add a "Renewed — keyed as new policy" link between the old and the new row (the renewals loader admits the CRM cannot link them), so the old row stops showing as due.

### Insurance — import and AI extraction

_Checked:_ `/dashboard/insurance/import` (ImportWizard), `POST /api/insurance/import/parse`, `POST /api/insurance/import/commit`, `POST /api/insurance/extract`, `lib/insurance/extract/to-draft.ts`, `lib/insurance/parse-policy.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[INS-15] [High] Import silently drops every row after the 1,000th** — `src/app/api/insurance/import/parse/route.ts:198` + `src/components/imports/ImportWizard.tsx:68,191` — parse allows up to 5,000 rows but returns `rows.slice(0, 1000)`. The wizard commits `parsed.rows` while its button says "Import 5000 rows". Rows 1,001–5,000 are never sent, and nothing reports them as skipped or errored. _Fix:_ return every row (or stream them in chunks), or cap the file at 1,000 and say so.
- **[INS-16] [Medium] One bad row aborts the whole import** — `src/app/api/insurance/import/commit/route.ts:167-189` — each INSERT sits in a JS try/catch inside one Postgres transaction with no SAVEPOINT. The first failing row (for example a numeric overflow on `sum_assured` or `commission_paisa`, or an int overflow) puts the transaction in an aborted state. Every later row "errors" with "current transaction is aborted", and the final `recordAudit` throws. The whole batch rolls back with a 500 although the code reads as per-row error reporting. _Fix:_ wrap each row in `tx.transaction()` (a savepoint), or validate the values before inserting.
- **[INS-17] [Medium] Import maps half-yearly to annual and every commercial line to a personal type** — `src/app/api/insurance/import/commit/route.ts:25-50`.
  - Frequency: `normFreq` tests `/annual|yearly|year|annum/` before `/semi|half/`, so "Semi-Annual" and "Half Yearly" import as `annual`. The installment maths and "Mark renewed" then advance 12 months instead of 6.
  - Type: `normType`'s list stops at `general_other`, so "Group Health" becomes `health` (Personal Lines), "GPA" becomes `personal_accident`, "Fire" becomes `home`, and Marine and WC become `general_other`. The Insurance dashboard's Personal/Commercial split and its targets are wrong for every imported commercial policy.
  - _Fix:_ test semi/half first, and map to the full `POLICY_TYPES` (fire, marine, burglary, workmen_comp, group_*).
- **[INS-18] [Medium] AI extraction maps "Half Yearly" and "Semi-Annual" to annual, and can emit an invalid frequency** — `src/lib/insurance/extract/to-draft.ts:33-37` — `n.includes("semi_annual")` never matches the human text, and the `/annual|yearly/` fallback catches "semi-annual" and "half yearly". When neither word is present (e.g. "Half-Year"), it returns `"half_yearly"`, which is not in `PREMIUM_FREQUENCIES`, and the save fails Zod with "invalid body". _Fix:_ test half/semi first and return `semi_annual`.
- **[INS-19] [Medium] The regex fallback reads the installment as the annual premium** — `src/lib/insurance/parse-policy.ts:179-182` — `annualPremium` takes the first of "instalment premium", "modal premium" and "premium payable". A monthly policy's ₹2,000 modal premium is stored as `annual_premium = 2000`, and the dashboards then divide it by 12 (₹167 per installment). _Fix:_ when the matched label is modal or instalment, multiply by the frequency factor, or keep it in `details` and leave `annualPremium` blank.
- **[INS-20] [Low] The import links a policy to a deleted or merged client and does not dedupe within the file** — `src/app/api/insurance/import/commit/route.ts:114,117-121` — the PAN lookup has no `deleted_at IS NULL` or `merged_into_id IS NULL`. The policy-number dedupe only checks the DB, so the same number twice in one file inserts twice. Invalid day/month pairs (e.g. 31-02-2026) roll over to 3 March through `Date.UTC`. _Fix:_ filter to live clients, track numbers seen in the batch, and reject dates that roll over.

**Improvements**
- `POST /api/insurance/import/parse` only calls `requireSession()`. Gate it on `insurance.write` like the page and the commit route.
- The import maps `status` only to the seven non-claim statuses. Map `claim_filed` / `claim_settled` (or "in force") explicitly rather than defaulting to `active`.

### Insurance — commission

_Checked:_ `/dashboard/insurance/commission`, `GET/POST /api/insurance/commission-entries`, `DELETE /api/insurance/commission-entries/[id]`, AddCommissionEntry, CommissionLedger.

**Bugs**
- **[INS-21] [Medium] Any `insurance.write` user can delete anyone's commission receipt** — `src/app/api/insurance/commission-entries/[id]/route.ts:21-27` — the list and GET show non-managers only their own entries (`recorded_by_user_id`), but DELETE has no owner or manager check. An RM can hard-delete the firm's recorded insurer payouts by id, and the deletion is audited but cannot be restored. _Fix:_ allow delete only for the recorder or a manager (`seesWholeInsuranceBook`), or soft-delete.
- **[INS-22] [Low] "YTD net" is calendar-year, and the inputs are unvalidated** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance/commission/page.tsx:52` (`date_trunc('year', …)`), `src/app/api/insurance/commission-entries/route.ts:21,28-32`. Every other figure in the CRM is FY (April–March), so between January and March "YTD" holds only three months. `entryDate` is a bare `z.string()` (a bad value returns a 500), and an explicit `netAmount` above gross, or TDS above gross, is accepted. _Fix:_ use FY-to-date with IST, an ISO-date regex, and `tds <= gross`, `net <= gross`.

**Improvements**
- "Recorded on policies" sums `commission_paisa` across cancelled and never-issued policies. Restrict it to issued ones (`STATUS_ISSUED_EXCLUDED`).

### Insurance — claims (register, form, API, follow-up)

_Checked:_ `/dashboard/insurance-claims` (register), `/new` (create + `?edit=`), `/[id]` (+ ClaimDocuments), `/scorecard`, `/desk` (redirect); `GET/POST /api/insurance-claims`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/insurance-claims/[id]`, `POST /api/insurance-claims/[id]/follow-up`; drawer actions (status, assign, follow-up, settle).

**Bugs**
- **[INS-23] [Medium] Closing a claim leaves its follow-up tasks open** — `src/app/api/insurance-claims/[id]/route.ts:96-131` — a claim's next action is a task with `meta.claimId`. When the claim is settled, rejected or withdrawn (drawer "Record settlement", status change, claim form), nothing completes those tasks. The handler keeps overdue "Claim follow-up — X" tasks for a claim that is finished, and they inflate My Day and overdue counts. Deleting a claim orphans them too. Opportunities close their tasks this way (`closeOpportunity`). _Fix:_ when `claimStatus` moves into `CLAIM_CLOSED`, or on DELETE, mark tasks `WHERE meta->>'claimId' = id AND status IN (open, in_progress, blocked)` completed.
- **[INS-24] [Medium] A claim's change to the policy's status is never audited on the policy** — `src/app/api/insurance-claims/route.ts:121-133`, `src/app/api/insurance-claims/[id]/route.ts:107-119` — these routes update `insurance_policies.status` with no `insurance_policy` audit row. Policy history lives only in `audit_log` (CLAUDE.md), so the policy's provenance and timeline never show why it left `active`. A claim PATCH that changes `policyId` also reconciles the OLD policy (`before.policyId`) and never the new one. _Fix:_ write an `insurance.update` audit with before/after status, and use `parse.data.policyId ?? before.policyId`.
- **[INS-25] [Medium] The claims register shows every claim to every `insurance.view` holder, including a branch-locked head** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance-claims/page.tsx:56-95` — `GET /api/insurance-claims` scopes non-managers to their own claims and the Claims Dashboard scopes a branch-locked head to their branch. The register page, its status counts and the claim page (`/[id]`) apply no scope. An RM sees every claimant's phone, email and amounts across the firm. The status, mode and stats queries also have no explicit `tenant_id` (the comment at line 57 says superuser connections bypass RLS). _Fix:_ apply `claimsAccess(session).scope`, or at least the branch lock, plus explicit tenant filters.
- **[INS-26] [Low] Claim edit cannot clear a field** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance-claims/new/page.tsx:79-102` — the form sends `undefined` for an emptied field, so a wrong settlement date, claim type or mode keyed by mistake can never be removed (PATCH accepts null, but the form never sends it). The claim's TAT and closed-on then stay wrong. _Fix:_ send `null` for cleared fields in edit mode.
- **[INS-27] [Low] The claim form's policy picker ignores the chosen client and stops at 500 policies** — `src/app/dashboard/insurance-claims/new/page.tsx:48-50,151-156` — it lists the viewer's 500 newest policies of any client, so a claim can be linked to another client's policy, and older policies cannot be picked in a large book. _Fix:_ load `/api/insurance?investorId=` for the selected claimant.
- **[INS-28] [Low] The follow-up route ignores the claims scope and closed claims** — `src/app/api/insurance-claims/[id]/follow-up/route.ts:52-58,75` — a branch-locked head can schedule tasks on another branch's claims by id, and a follow-up can be added to a settled or rejected claim. `dueDate` passes the regex as "2026-13-45", which yields an Invalid Date and a 500. _Fix:_ load through `claimsAccess().scope`, refuse closed claims, and validate the date.
- **[INS-29] [Low] The Settlement Scorecard disagrees with the Claims Dashboard** — `src/lib/insurance/claims-analytics.ts:41-52,61-80` — ageing skips claims with no intimation date (the dashboard falls back to the keyed date) and uses UTC `current_date`. Insurers are grouped on the raw `insurer` text, so "HDFC Ergo" and "hdfc ergo " are separate rows, and the policy's insurer is ignored when the claim's is blank. The dashboard uses `lower(btrim(COALESCE(c.insurer, p.insurer)))`. The page is also unscoped. _Fix:_ derive it from `loadClaimSet`, or reuse the same keys.

**Improvements**
- Validate on PATCH/POST that `settledAmount <= claimedAmount` (warn) and that settled statuses carry a settlement date. The drawer enforces this, but the claim form does not.
- When `claimStatus` is omitted on create, record `"claim_intimated"` in the create audit's `after`. The status-history CTE (`set.ts` `a.after ? 'claimStatus'`) otherwise has no entry for the initial status.
- ClaimDocuments PATCHes the whole `documents` array, so two people adding documents at once overwrite each other. Use an append/remove endpoint.

### Insurance Team Dashboard (loaders + components)

_Checked:_ `lib/insurance/book.ts`, `lib/dashboard/insurance/{window,access,business,renewals,lost,attention,pipeline,targets,team,quality,detail,clients}.ts`, `InsuranceCommandCentre.tsx` drill-down links, `/api/insurance/command-centre/clients/[id]`, Owner Dashboard `insurance-compliance.tsx` + `lib/metrics/insurance.ts`, `lib/insurance/advisor-production.ts`. Checked every `${date} ± ${n}` for the untyped-parameter trap; none found (all literals, columns or `::int`).

**Bugs**
- **[INS-30] [Medium] Lost business counts policies that were later reinstated, re-counts repeat cancels, and loses imported lapses** — `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/window.ts:254-268`, `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/lost.ts:44-63`.
  - Reinstated: `lostEvents` joins on the audit event whatever the policy's status is now. A policy marked lapsed on 5 Sep and renewed or reinstated on 20 Sep (`/renew` sets it to active) still shows in September's lost premium and in "renewal lost", under its owner and branch.
  - Repeat cancels: "Cancel" (DELETE) on an already-cancelled or lapsed policy writes another `insurance.delete`, which dates the loss again into the current period.
  - Imported lapses: the "undated" query drops any lost policy that has ANY update audit, even an edit to remarks only. Such a policy appears neither dated nor undated.
  - _Fix:_ require `pol.status IN STATUS_LOST`, ignore deletes whose `before.status` is already lost, and test for a status-change audit specifically in the undated check.
- **[INS-31] [Medium] The renewal-premium drill-down does not show the tile's rows** — `src/components/dashboards/InsuranceCommandCentre.tsx:200-202` vs `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/business.ts:71-84` — the tiles' renewal premium and policy count include "Mark renewed" collections in the period (`renewEvents`, from the second year). The link opens the register with `booked_from/booked_to&nature=renewal`, which lists only policies BOOKED in the period with businessType renewal. A firm that renews life policies with "Mark renewed" sees ₹X of renewal premium, and the drill-down shows zero rows. _Fix:_ add a register param for "renewed between" (from the `insurance.renew` audit), or label the tile's two components separately.
- **[INS-32] [Medium] The Owner Dashboard and the Insurance Team Dashboard disagree on renewal premium** — `src/lib/metrics/insurance.ts:49-71` vs `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/business.ts:71-84` — the Owner card's "New · Renewal" premium counts only policy rows booked in the period. The Insurance dashboard also adds "Mark renewed" installments. The same firm, period and label show two renewal figures, against CLAUDE.md's "one definition per number". The Insurance Production report (`advisor-production.ts:82`) is a third version: `status <> 'cancelled'`, so it counts proposed and in-underwriting premium as production, unlike "booked = issued". _Fix:_ move the renewal-collected definition into `lib/metrics` and use it on all three.
- **[INS-33] [Medium] The insurance pipeline ignores client opportunities** — `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/pipeline.ts:68-79` (`leadBase`, `window.ts:313-340`) — the pipeline, stage funnel, ageing, insurance-team "pending" and corporate rows read `leads` only. Since 2026-10-02 an existing client who wants insurance is a `client_opportunities` row with `pipeline='insurance'`, never a lead (CLAUDE.md). All cross-sell insurance work, which is most of an MFD's insurance pipeline, is missing from the Insurance Head's pipeline, and there is no needs-analysis stage count for it. _Fix:_ union insurance opportunities (journey stage → LEAD_STAGES mapping) into the pipeline.
- **[INS-34] [Medium] An insurance proposal can be sent without the needs-analysis gate** — `src/app/api/proposals/[id]/send/route.ts:228-280` vs `src/lib/opportunities/journey.ts:200-206` — CLAUDE.md says an insurance quote "needs a recorded needs analysis — never bypassed". That gate runs only when an opportunity is advanced. Proposals/send checks the MF suitability note and alignment but never `needsAnalysis` for an insurance proposal (or for one linked through `opportunity_id`). The quote reaches the client, and `onProposalEvent` then leaves the opportunity at Suitability, apparently unaware. This is the insurance counterpart of the known MF bypass. _Fix:_ in send, for an insurance-type proposal, require the linked or open insurance opportunity's `needsAnalysis` (`hasNeedsAnalysis`).
- **[INS-35] [Low] Register drill-downs drop some dashboard filters, and the branch view's rows are cut to the viewer's own book** — `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/window.ts:364-377`, `src/app/dashboard/insurance/page.tsx:96,113`.
  - Dropped filters: `registerHref` carries line, group, branch, owner, insurer and category only. With the dashboard filtered by nature, MF/insurance-only, client type, band, source, executive or status, every tile link opens a wider set than the tile counted.
  - Branch view: a branch-locked head granted `dashboard.view.insurance` has a branch-scoped dashboard, but the register restricts them to policies they sold or whose client they manage, so branch tiles open onto fewer rows.
  - _Fix:_ carry every filter the register understands, and give the register the same `insuranceAccess` scope.
- **[INS-36] [Low] The policy-sold event changes no opportunity or Cross-sell state** — `src/app/api/insurance/route.ts:110-160` — FD writes call `onProductBooked` (opportunity won, Cross-sell "Held with us"). Recording an insurance policy does neither. The client's open health or term opportunity stays open until someone opens it and syncs, and the Owner Dashboard's Cross-sell card under-reports until then. _Fix:_ on create, or when the status becomes issued, call `onProductBooked` for the matching product, or `syncOpportunity` for the client's open insurance opportunities.

**Improvements**
- `loadRenewalAttention` judges "high premium" on the installment (`inst >= insHighPremium`), while issuance and proposals judge the annual premium against the same threshold. Pick one basis, or rename it.
- `lib/insurance/persistency.ts` is not imported anywhere outside tests. Wire it into the retention section or remove it.
- `polBase` runs a LATERAL lookup on `leads` for every policy on every loader call. Precompute `source` once in a CTE keyed by investor.

### Claims Dashboard (loaders + components)

_Checked:_ `lib/dashboard/claims/{window,access,set,detail,search,params,analysis,review}.ts`, `ClaimsControlCentre` components (drawer actions: status / assign / follow-up / settle), `GET /api/claims-control/[id]`, `GET /api/claims-control/search`, `lib/insurance/{claims-book,claim-checklist}.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[INS-37] [Low] "Recently rejected" claims are dated by the last edit, not by the rejection** — `src/lib/dashboard/insurance/claims.ts:131,136` — the Insurance dashboard's claim snapshot treats a claim as rejected in the last 30 days when `updated_at` is recent. Editing a comment on a claim rejected a year ago brings it back into "rejected recently". CLAUDE.md says the rejection date comes from `audit_log`, which `set.ts` already does. _Fix:_ use the audit transition into `rejected`, as `closed_on` in `set.ts` does.

**Improvements**
- The drawer's "Change status" offers every status, including reopening a settled claim. If a closed claim is reopened, its `closed_on` / TAT still read from the last closing audit. Confirm before reopening, and clear the stale settlement date and amount.
- `loadClaimSet` stops at `LIMIT 20000` with no signal. Surface "showing the first 20,000" if it is ever hit.

---

## Communications, marketing & automations

Not re-reported (already known): designer test-send relay, designer merge-value escaping and the unsandboxed preview, unsubscribe link, gateClientSend opt-out scope, dispatch-inside-tx durability, scheduled-comms batch, workflow loop / single-run worker, rule-scheduler lease, webhook SSRF.

### Campaigns
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/campaigns` (list, stats), `/new`, `/[id]` + `CampaignActions`, `CampaignAudienceBuilder`, `CampaignTestSend`, `CampaignRecipients`; API `GET/POST /api/campaigns`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/campaigns/[id]`, `POST launch`, `POST control` (pause/resume/cancel/retry_failed), `POST test-send`, `GET recipients`, `POST recompute-roi`, `GET preflight`, `POST /api/segments/adhoc`; `lib/campaigns/{audience,roi,broadcast}`; the dispatcher paths these actions drive.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-01] [High] "Retry failed" re-sends nothing, deletes the audit rows and creates a duplicate recurring campaign** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/control/route.ts:72-90`. The route deletes the failed `communications` rows and sets the campaign back to `sending`. It never resets `campaign_recipients`. The dispatcher counts every `campaign_recipients.subject_id` as processed (`campaign-dispatcher.ts:118-120`), and those rows are already `failed` (or `skipped`), so `unsent` is empty and no row is pending. The campaign goes straight to `markSent()`. Result: no message is retried, the failure history is gone (this breaks "a skip creates a row, full audit, every time"), the UI says "Retrying N failed recipient(s)…", and `markSent` runs `maybeCloneRecurring` again, so a recurring campaign gets a second future copy. _Fix:_ reset the matching `campaign_recipients` rows to `retry` (attempts=0) instead of deleting `communications`. Make `maybeCloneRecurring` idempotent per parent and occurrence.
- **[COM-02] [High] A sent or cancelled campaign can be re-launched and re-edited** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/launch/route.ts:72` and `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/route.ts:93`. Launch has no source-status guard. Any status, including `sent`, `cancelled` and `sending`, is flipped to `sending`. The PATCH lock checks `["sending","completed"]`, but the status enum has no `completed` (it is `sent`). So after a send you can swap `segmentId`/`templateId`, set `status:"draft"`, and launch again. Each relaunch messages the new members, revives a campaign that was cancelled on purpose, and runs `markSent`, which clones the recurrence again. _Fix:_ only allow launch from `draft`/`scheduled` with a conditional UPDATE (`WHERE status IN (...)`). Change the lock list to `sent`/`paused`.
- **[COM-03] [Medium] The campaign approval gate does not gate anything** — `src/app/dashboard/campaigns/new/page.tsx:122`, `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/route.ts:110-114`, `src/components/campaigns/CampaignActions.tsx:47`. "Save & send" posts `approvalStatus:"not_required"`, and the API accepts it from any `campaign.send` holder. The Approve button PATCHes `approved` with no maker-checker. It is the same capability, and the creator is allowed. Approval is also not bound to content: after approval, PATCH can still change `templateId`, `segmentId` or the variant on a `draft`/`scheduled` campaign, and the approval stays in place. _Fix:_ approval needs a separate capability and approver ≠ creator. Strip `not_required`/`approved` from client input. Reset approval when any send-mechanics field changes.
- **[COM-04] [Medium] "Save as draft" with a schedule time creates a live scheduled send** — `src/app/dashboard/campaigns/new/page.tsx:130` and `src/app/api/campaigns/route.ts:139`. The draft path still sends `scheduledAt`, and the server sets `status = scheduled` whenever channel + template + segment + scheduledAt are present. If the user picked "Not required" in the approval dropdown, or someone approves it later, `promoteDueScheduledCampaigns` sends a campaign the user saved as a draft. A `scheduledAt` in the past is also accepted and fires on the next tick. _Fix:_ the draft action must not send `scheduledAt`, or the server must keep it `draft` unless explicitly scheduled. Reject past times.
- **[COM-05] [High] Campaign audiences include deleted, merged and closed clients** — `src/lib/campaigns/audience.ts:79-82`, `src/lib/filter/compiler.ts:240-249`, `src/workers/campaign-dispatcher.ts:140`. "All clients" selects every `investors` row for the tenant. Filter mode compiles to `SELECT … FROM investors inv WHERE tenant_id = … AND <ast>`, and the catalog/compiler has no live-client predicate. The dispatcher loads each subject by id without checking `deleted_at`/`merged_into_id`. A broadcast to "All clients" therefore messages soft-deleted clients (possibly deleted at their own request) and both halves of merged duplicates (the same person twice), plus closed and exited clients. "All leads" also includes converted leads, who are already in the client list. _Fix:_ have `compileToSelect` / the "all" mode add `aliveClient` (`deleted_at IS NULL AND merged_into_id IS NULL AND status NOT IN ('closed','exited')`). For leads, exclude converted leads. Re-check in the dispatcher.
- **[COM-06] [Medium] Campaign spend and ROI totals are inflated** — `src/workers/campaign-dispatcher.ts:367` and `src/lib/campaigns/roi.ts:44-80`. Every recurring clone copies `campaignCost`, so a monthly campaign of ₹50k shows ₹6L "Spend (YTD)". `recomputeCampaignRoi` credits a meeting or conversion to every campaign that touched the person in the previous 30 days. The docstring calls this "last-touch", but it is all-touch, so the YTD Meetings and Conversions tiles double-count overlapping campaigns. _Fix:_ do not copy cost onto clones (or split it). Attribute each outcome only to the latest campaign before it.
- **[COM-07] [Medium] Recipient drill-down has no permission check** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/recipients/route.ts:18-22`. Only `requireSession()` is called. Any staff user, for example an RM without `campaign.send`, can list up to 500 recipient names, statuses and skip/fail reasons for any campaign id, including clients outside their book. _Fix:_ add `requireCap("campaign.send")`, like the sibling routes.
- **[COM-08] [Medium] The variant B template and template channel are never checked at launch** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/launch/route.ts:51-70`. Also the promoter at `campaign-dispatcher.ts:268-276`. Only the primary template's status is checked. If variant B is (or later becomes) unapproved, the B share of the audience is silently `template_not_approved`, and that skip is not in `RETRYABLE`. PATCH can also point `templateId` at a template of a different channel, and dispatch then fails to find it per recipient. _Fix:_ before launch, validate both templates: approved, and channel equals the campaign channel.
- **[COM-09] [Low] Cancelling a completed campaign rewrites its history** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/route.ts:146-150`. DELETE refuses only `sending`, so a `sent` campaign becomes `cancelled` and falls out of the "sent" report filters. _Fix:_ refuse `sent`, or archive it separately.
- **[COM-10] [Low] Recurrence end date drops its last day** — `src/app/dashboard/campaigns/new/page.tsx:137`. `new Date("2026-12-31").toISOString()` is midnight UTC, and `next > recurrenceUntil` then excludes a run later that same IST day. An out-of-range throttle (0 or >1000) returns a bare "invalid body". _Fix:_ store the end of the day in IST. Clamp the throttle in the UI.
- **[COM-11] [Low] Campaign test-send goes to any address, unaudited** — `src/app/api/campaigns/[id]/test-send/route.ts:33-90`. The `recipient` override is free text with no rate limit and no audit row. Any approved template can go to any number or address as a "test". _Fix:_ limit it to the user's own and team contacts, and audit it.

**Improvements**
- Each "Set audience" click creates a permanent ad-hoc segment, and filter mode emits one `segment_entry.<name>` outbox event per member. That can be tens of thousands of no-op events. Skip outbox emission for ad-hoc audiences and garbage-collect unused ones.
- After "Change", re-picking an audience leaves the earlier segment orphaned. Reuse or delete it.
- The "Existing segment" dropdown offers task segments, which the dispatcher cannot send to (every member becomes `skipped_no_contact`). Filter to investor and lead.
- The Cancel button has no `confirmDialog`, and it is the campaign kill switch.
- The "YTD" tiles use the calendar year (`date_trunc('year')`), not the Indian FY.

### Segments & Filter AST
_Checked:_ `GET/POST /api/segments`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/segments/[id]`, `POST /api/segments/preview`, `POST /api/segments/adhoc`, `components/filter/FilterBuilder.tsx`, `lib/filter/{catalog,compiler,ast,event-fields}`, the segment materializer → outbox → `segment_entry` trigger path. There is no `/dashboard/segments` page in the tree.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-12] [Medium] Filter-builder dropdowns are missing values that exist in the database** — `src/lib/filter/catalog.ts:173` and `:366`. Investor `status` offers only `active/dormant/exited`; the schema also has `inactive`, `redemption_risk` and `closed`. Lead `stage` offers only the legacy aliases (`qualified`, `proposal_sent`, `cold`), not the canonical `meeting_scheduled`, `won_lead`, `not_interested`, `follow_up` and so on. "Status is not exited" therefore still targets closed clients. Redemption-risk clients and most real lead stages cannot be selected. _Fix:_ derive `enumValues` from the Drizzle enums.
- **[COM-13] [Medium] Relation predicates compile against the wrong table** — `src/lib/filter/compiler.ts:51`. The sub-compiler is `new Compiler(this.catalog.subject)`, so fields inside a `rel` (transactions/sips/folios/goals) resolve against the investor catalog. "Has a transaction with amount > 1L" either throws "Unknown field" or produces `EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM transactions tx WHERE … AND inv.aum > $1)`, which is a filter on the client's AUM. _Fix:_ give relations their own catalogs and compile the child predicate against them.
- **[COM-14] [Medium] Date filters on datetime fields are wrong** — `src/lib/filter/compiler.ts:101-112,164-170`. The builder's DateField sends `"2026-10-01"`, and `inv.onboarded_at = '2026-10-01'` matches only the exact UTC-midnight instant, so "Onboarded date is 1 Oct" returns 0. `gt`/`lt` compare against UTC midnight, and `date_today`/`date_is_anniversary` use the server's `CURRENT_DATE` (UTC; the database timezone is not set), so between 00:00 and 05:30 IST "today" means yesterday. _Fix:_ for `date`-valued comparisons on datetime fields, compare `(field AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata')::date`, and use the IST date for the `date_*` operators.
- **[COM-15] [Medium] Segment entry/exit workflow triggers can never fire** — `src/lib/workflow/triggers.ts:94-96`, `src/workers/segment-materializer.ts:114-128`, `src/workers/outbox-poller.ts:143-147`. The materializer emits `kind = "segment_entry.<segment name>"`. The trigger row stores `eventKind:"segment_entry"`, with no segment id and no prefix, and the poller requires an exact match. Keying on the name would also break the trigger whenever a segment is renamed (PATCH allows that). _Fix:_ emit `segment_entry` with `segmentId` in `after`, store `segmentId` on the trigger, and match on it.
- **[COM-16] [Low] An RM/branch filter value can crash the preview** — `src/components/filter/FilterBuilder.tsx:205` and `src/app/api/segments/preview/route.ts:63-77`. `uuid` fields render as a free-text input, so a user types a name, Postgres raises an `invalid input syntax for type uuid` error, and the request returns 500. _Fix:_ use a user/branch picker for uuid fields and validate before executing.
- **[COM-17] [Low] A bad filter in the ad-hoc audience route leaves an orphan segment** — `src/app/api/segments/adhoc/route.ts:27` and `src/lib/campaigns/audience.ts:54-69`. `ast: z.any()`, and the segment row is committed before `materializeSegment` compiles it. A malformed AST leaves an orphan dynamic segment and a 500. _Fix:_ validate with `FilterASTSchema` and pre-compile before inserting.
- **[COM-18] [Low] Deleting or editing a segment silently affects live campaigns** — `src/app/api/segments/[id]/route.ts:113-125`. DELETE hard-deletes a segment that a scheduled, sending or recurring campaign uses, and the dispatcher then auto-cancels the campaign with "Linked segment or template not found". PATCH of `ast` on a static segment does not re-materialize, so its members silently disagree with its filter. _Fix:_ refuse deletion while a campaign or workflow references the segment. Re-materialize static segments on an AST change.

**Improvements**
- Static segments and the materializer stop silently at 100,000 rows. Surface "truncated".
- `GET /api/segments` has no capability check (it returns every saved AST to any user).
- The FilterBuilder used in campaigns has no live count (the preview needs `segments.manage`, which `campaign.send` users may lack). Show the count from `/api/segments/adhoc` or relax the cap.

### Templates (incl. WhatsApp sync / import)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/templates` (list), `/new`, `/[id]` + `TemplateActions` + `EmailPreview`, `/[id]/edit` + `EditTemplateForm`, `FetchWhatsappTemplatesButton`; API `GET/POST /api/templates`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/templates/[id]`, `POST submit|approve|reject|version`, `POST /api/integrations/whatsapp/fetch-templates`; `lib/comms/whatsapp-template-{import,submit}.ts`, `lib/templates/{ensure,starters}`; the vartify sync's promote step.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-19] [High] Editing a live template takes it offline immediately** — `src/app/api/templates/[id]/version/route.ts:60-78`. "Edit template (creates a new draft)" points `currentVersionId` at the new draft and sets `templates.status = "draft"`. The pipeline needs `status === "approved"` (`pipeline.ts:163`), so from the moment an author clicks Edit, every workflow, rule, compose-by-template and running campaign using that key is skipped `template_not_approved` until v2 is approved. Campaign recipients skipped this way are not retryable. The new version also does not copy `attachments`, so attachments silently vanish. _Fix:_ keep the approved version live (a separate `draft_version_id`) and swap only on approval. Copy attachments.
- **[COM-20] [High] Attachments on an approved template can be changed without re-approval** — `src/app/api/templates/[id]/route.ts:96-99,118-149`. The non-draft guard checks only `body`/`variables`. A PATCH with only `attachments` rewrites the attachments on the approved version and keeps it sendable. A `template.edit` user can attach an unreviewed PDF to an approved marketing template. _Fix:_ include `attachments` (and any content field) in the non-draft guard.
- **[COM-21] [Medium] A marketing template can skip the compliance officer by changing its category** — `src/app/api/templates/[id]/route.ts:103-111` and `submit/route.ts:132-147`. Category can change only while `templates.status === "draft"`, but creating a new version sets the status to draft. The author can then flip an approved marketing template to `transactional`/`utility`, and submit auto-approves it ("auto-approved for non-marketing category") with no checker. Because the pipeline takes consent from the template's category, it also stops requiring marketing consent. _Fix:_ require `template.approve` (or a compliance step) for any category change away from marketing, and for every version of a template that was previously marketing.
- **[COM-22] [Medium] WhatsApp marketing templates never get internal compliance review** — `src/app/api/templates/[id]/submit/route.ts:69-71,86-127`, plus the import at `src/lib/comms/whatsapp-template-import.ts:150-152`. For WhatsApp, a `block` finding is ignored and no `compliance_officer` step is created. The sync worker then flips `pending_approval → approved` as soon as Meta approves. Import marks every Meta-approved template `approved` directly. So SEBI/AMFI rule violations (guaranteed returns, missing disclaimer) reach clients on WhatsApp, even though the 11-rule gate is supposed to be the moat. _Fix:_ for WA marketing (and any `block` finding), require internal approval before Meta submission, or before the sync is allowed to promote.
- **[COM-23] [Medium] WhatsApp import overwrites local work and un-pauses templates** — `src/lib/comms/whatsapp-template-import.ts:170-180`. For an existing key it updates `templates.status`/`category` and rewrites the current version's body, variables and approval in place. If the current version is a local v2 draft, the draft is overwritten with Meta's content and marked `approved_external`. A template the firm paused or archived is set back to `approved`. An approved version is mutated in place. _Fix:_ write imports as a new version, and never touch `paused`/`archived` or local drafts.
- **[COM-24] [Medium] Editing a WhatsApp template drops its Meta metadata** — `src/app/dashboard/templates/[id]/edit/EditTemplateForm.tsx:231-235`. Save posts `body: { body: { text } }`, which discards `vartifyTemplateName`, `languageCode`, `components` and the header media/sample fields that imported templates carry. After re-submission the adapter falls back to `template_language:"en"` (`vartify.ts:209`) with no header media. Hindi and media-header templates then fail at Meta. _Fix:_ merge the edited text into the existing body object instead of replacing it.
- **[COM-25] [Medium] A rejected template is a dead end** — `src/app/dashboard/templates/[id]/TemplateActions.tsx:79-118`. When the version is `rejected` (and the template status is `rejected`), none of the action blocks render: no Edit, no "create new draft", no resubmit. The author has to recreate the template under a new key. _Fix:_ show "Revise (new draft)" (POST `/version`) for rejected versions.
- **[COM-26] [Medium] Retrying "Save & submit" creates duplicate templates** — `src/app/dashboard/templates/new/page.tsx:345-372` and `src/app/api/templates/route.ts:107-115`. When submit fails (WhatsApp not connected, Meta rejection), the template is already created but the user stays on the form. Clicking again POSTs again, and the key collision is silently resolved as `<key>_2`, `_3`… so workflows pointing at the original key and the copies drift apart. _Fix:_ after the first successful create, switch the form to edit (PATCH and submit the existing id).
- **[COM-27] [Low] Two versions of one template can wait for approval at once** — `src/app/api/templates/[id]/version/route.ts:28-41`. The comment says "already a draft or submitted version", but only `draft` is checked. A new draft can be created and submitted while another version is `submitted`. Approve and reject then pick an arbitrary `submitted` row (`limit(1)`). _Fix:_ refuse while a version is `submitted`, and approve or reject by version id.
- **[COM-28] [Low] Template pages are visible to every signed-in user** — `/dashboard/templates/page.tsx:63`, `/[id]/page.tsx:41` and `/[id]/edit/page.tsx:12` call only `requireSession()`. The APIs require `template.view|edit|communication.send|workflow.edit`, so any role can read every template body through the pages. _Fix:_ apply the same `requireAny` on the pages.

**Improvements**
- Archiving (DELETE) does not warn about scheduled campaigns or enabled rules and workflows that use the key. They then skip silently.
- The rule builder's template picker caches `/api/templates` in a module-level variable (`_ruleTplCache`), so templates created in the same session never appear.

### Communications (inbox / outbox / compose / threads / follow-through)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/communications` (filters, folders, counts) + `CommsList`, `/compose`, `/cadence`, `/responses` + `FollowThroughTable`; API `GET /api/communications`, `POST actions` (cancel/retry/flag), `POST compose`, `POST resend`, `GET/POST /api/comms/whatsapp-thread`, `GET/POST /api/comms/email-thread`, `/api/cadences` (+enroll, run-due); `lib/comms/compose.ts` retry/scheduled paths.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-29] [High] Any staff member can read or post in any client's conversation** — `src/app/api/comms/whatsapp-thread/route.ts:34-70` and `src/app/api/comms/email-thread/route.ts:35-72`. The two GET routes call only `requireSession()`. There is no capability and no RM scope, while the Communications page gates the same data and scopes RMs to their own clients. Any logged-in user can pull the full WhatsApp and email bodies, including inbound replies, for any `investor`/`lead` id. The POST handlers (lines 148 / 194) also skip the "not your client" check that `/api/communications/compose` enforces. _Fix:_ reuse the page's `canView` + `seesAll`/`rm_id`/`owner_id` scoping on GET and POST.
- **[COM-30] [Medium] Compose to a typed email address bypasses the client's opt-out** — `src/app/api/communications/compose/route.ts:66-104` and `src/lib/comms/compose.ts:141`. In "Email" mode, consent is looked up only by `investorId`, which is null. Typing an opted-out client's address with category `transactional` sends the mail and logs it against nobody. That violates "an opt-out always blocks". `email-thread` compose with a `to` override also logs the message against the client while sending elsewhere. _Fix:_ resolve the typed address to an investor or lead (by email) and apply its consent. Log the actual recipient.
- **[COM-31] [Medium] Compose reports "Sent ✓" for messages that were skipped** — `src/app/dashboard/communications/compose/page.tsx:67-77`. Only `skipped_consent` is handled. `skipped` results with `suppressed_*` (bounce/complaint suppression) or other reasons show "Sent ✓" and navigate away. During the 700 ms success timeout `busy` is already false, so a second click sends a duplicate. _Fix:_ treat any `status === "skipped"` as not sent and show the reason. Keep the button disabled until the navigation.
- **[COM-32] [Medium] Bulk "Retry" can send the same email twice** — `src/lib/comms/compose.ts:320-338`. `retryEmail` reads `status = 'failed'` without `FOR UPDATE` or a conditional update, so two concurrent `/api/communications/actions` retry calls (double-click, two tabs) both re-send. _Fix:_ `UPDATE … SET status='queued' WHERE id=$1 AND status='failed' RETURNING`, and send only if a row came back.
- **[COM-33] [Low] Some skipped messages are missing from the "Skipped" folder** — `src/app/dashboard/communications/page.tsx:18`. `SKIPPED` lists four statuses and omits `skipped_quota`, `skipped_frequency_cap` and `skipped_no_contact` (every campaign member with no contact). Those rows never appear under "Skipped". _Fix:_ match `status LIKE 'skipped%'`.
- **[COM-34] [Low] Resend reaches clients outside the user's book and misses some clients** — `src/app/api/communications/resend/route.ts:56-66`. There is no own-book scope: an RM with `investor.edit.assigned` can re-mail any firm client by communication id. The query also requires `i.status = 'active'`, so `redemption_risk`/`dormant` clients on the nudge list are silently dropped (`matched` < requested with no reason given). _Fix:_ add the RM scope and use the `aliveClient` definition.

**Improvements**
- The compose page breadcrumb reads "Settings › Communications". It should be Communications.
- `GET /api/communications` (mobile) counts `reports.business` differently from the page and actions route (it is not in `seesAll` there). Use one helper.
- `POST /api/cadences/[id]/enroll` does not check the cadence's `subject_type` against the enrolled subject, or the RM's scope. Duplicate enrollment is only checked by read (race).

### Announcements, tenant circulars, birthdays, content library, marketing assets
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/announcements` + `AnnouncementBoard`/`AnnouncementsStrip`; API `GET/POST /api/announcements`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/announcements/[id]`, `POST /[id]/read`, `lib/announcements/{validate,notify}`; `POST/DELETE /api/tenant-circulars`; `GET /api/birthdays` (BirthdayBell); `POST /api/content-library/upload-url`; `/dashboard/reports/marketing-assets` and `lib/marketing/assets-catalog` (all hrefs resolve). No landing-pages module exists beyond `/api/landing/packages`.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-35] [Medium] A branch head can edit or delete any announcement** — `src/app/api/announcements/[id]/route.ts:47-60,106-118`. `checkBranchTarget` runs only when `branchId` is being changed. A branch head can therefore PATCH (retitle, unpublish, expire) or DELETE the owner's firm-wide notices and other branches' notices. _Fix:_ for non-owners, require `before.branchId === session.branchId` (or `createdBy === me`) on PATCH and DELETE.
- **[COM-36] [Low] Editing an acknowledged announcement keeps the old acknowledgements** — `src/app/api/announcements/[id]/route.ts:62-70` and `/[id]/read/route.ts:24-31`. Changing the body or title of a `requiresAck` notice keeps everyone's earlier `acknowledged_at`, so "X of Y acknowledged" covers text they never saw. `/read` also accepts any id without checking audience or visibility. _Fix:_ clear acks when content changes on an ack-required notice. Validate the id against the viewer's visible set.
- **[COM-37] [Low] The birthday widget shows wrong days, ages and clients** — `src/app/api/birthdays/route.ts:47-70`. The doc says "last 7 days" but the code uses 30. `current_date` is the database's UTC date, so between 00:00 and 05:30 IST the buckets are a day early. `status <> 'closed'` still includes legacy `exited` clients. `turning()` subtracts birth year from the current year, so a 28-Dec birthday shown on 3-Jan reads one year too old, and a 1-Jan "tomorrow" on 31-Dec reads one year too young. Feb-29 birthdays never show in non-leap years. _Fix:_ use the IST date and the `aliveClient` filter. Compute age at the birthday date.
- **[COM-38] [Low] Circular deletion is not audited and can return 500** — `src/app/api/tenant-circulars/route.ts:77-92`. DELETE writes no audit row (create does), and a non-UUID `id` raises a 500 from the cast. _Fix:_ validate the UUID and record the audit.

**Improvements**
- `content-library/upload-url` accepts any `contentType` (HTML/SVG) for template attachments. Restrict it to document and image MIME types.

### Workflow rules (Automations › Rules / Actions / Run history)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard/automations` (+`/actions`, `/history`, layout), `/dashboard/workflows/rules/{library,new,[id],[id]/edit}`, `RuleBuilder`, `RuleToggle`, `LockButton`, `MoveRuleButton`, `CloneRuleButton`, `DeleteRuleButton`, `TestRulePanel`; API `GET/POST /api/workflow-rules`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /[id]`, `POST toggle|lock|move|clone|run-now`, `GET/POST test`, `POST library/activate`, scheduled-jobs `retry`/`retry-all`; `lib/workflow-rules/{criteria,actions,modules,guard,predefined}`.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-39] [Medium] Number and date conditions in rule criteria never match** — `src/lib/workflow-rules/criteria.ts:79-122,145-168`.
  - **Numbers:** numeric columns arrive as strings (`"500000.00"`, from Drizzle numeric), while the builder stores `Number(500000)`. `loose()` keeps the types apart, so "AUM is 500000" never matches.
  - **Dates, before/after:** `greater_than` and the other comparisons call `Number("2026-10-01")`, which is NaN, so every date comparison the builder offers is always false.
  - **Dates, today/yesterday/tomorrow:** `isSameDay` compares the year too, so "Date of birth is today" never fires. The day is computed in server time (UTC), not IST.
  - _Fix:_ coerce by field type (numeric → `Number`; date → IST `yyyy-mm-dd` compare). Use a month-and-day match for anniversary-type fields.
- **[COM-40] [Medium] Criteria on renamed fields always read as empty** — `src/lib/workflow-rules/criteria.ts:65-71` and `modules.ts:163,227-228`. `readField` tries the key and its camelCase form, but ignores `ModuleField.column`. `date_of_birth` (column `dob`), `kyc_expires_at` (`ckyc_expiry_at`) and `last_activity_at` (`updated_at`) are always `undefined`: "is empty" is always true and every other operator false. The time scanner does honour `column`, so a rule anchored on DOB fires and then fails its own criteria. _Fix:_ resolve `field.column ?? field.key` (and its camelCase form) when reading the record.
- **[COM-41] [Medium] Rules can write a client status the rest of the CRM does not recognise** — `src/lib/workflow-rules/modules.ts:218-222` and `actions.ts:470-484`. The investors module's writable `status` enum is `active/dormant/inactive/deceased/closed`. `deceased` is not a schema status, and `redemption_risk`/`exited` are missing. `update_field` writes the value without enum validation, so a rule can set `status='deceased'`, which `aliveClient` and every dashboard still count as a live client. `update_field` also bypasses the outbox and audit, so no downstream rule sees the change. _Fix:_ align enumValues with the schema, validate them in `runUpdateField`, and emit an audit/outbox event.
- **[COM-42] [Medium] Locks can be bypassed and enable/disable is not audited** — `src/app/api/workflow-rules/[id]/route.ts:116-138`, `toggle/route.ts:15-25`, `move/route.ts`. Only PATCH honours `locked`. DELETE, toggle and move do not, so any `workflow.edit` user can delete or disable a rule another admin locked (the UI hides the toggle, but the API does not check). Toggle uses `SET enabled = NOT enabled`, which is not idempotent (two requests cancel each other out), and it writes no audit row, so who switched off a compliance rule is untraceable. _Fix:_ apply the lock check to all mutating routes. Make toggle take an explicit `enabled` and record an audit row.
- **[COM-43] [Low] Boolean and list conditions save the wrong values** — `src/components/workflow-rules/RuleBuilder.tsx:1027-1033` and `criteria.ts:100-107`. The boolean `<select>` displays "true", but the row's value stays `""` until the user changes it, so the saved rule compares to `""` and never matches. The `in`/`not_in` comma split does not trim, so `"a, b"` never matches `b`. _Fix:_ default the value to `true` and trim the split.
- **[COM-44] [Low] Lock reason uses a banned native prompt** — `src/components/workflow-rules/LockButton.tsx:25` calls `prompt()`, which CLAUDE.md bans. _Fix:_ use `promptDialog()`.
- **[COM-45] [Low] Rule detail page bypasses the Run-history gate** — `src/app/dashboard/workflows/rules/[id]/page.tsx:41-65`. There is no capability check, so any user sees up to 50 executions and scheduled jobs (errors, subject ids) that `/automations/history` restricts to `canViewMonitor`. "Total runs" and "Success rate" are computed from those last 50 only. _Fix:_ gate the run sections with `canViewMonitor`. Label them "last 50".
- **[COM-46] [Low] "Run now" sends real messages without an audit trail** — `src/app/api/workflow-rules/[id]/run-now/route.ts:16-50`. It executes real sends and field updates on any record id in the firm with no audit row and no own-book scope. _Fix:_ record an audit row (actor, subject) and scope it to records the actor can view.

**Improvements**
- `POST /api/workflow-rules` creates rules `enabled: true` straight from the builder (library rules start disabled), and writes no audit row on create.
- `library/activate` skips `ruleNameProblem` and `unsupportedRuleFeature`, unlike create.
- The test-route record picker lists the 20 newest rows including soft-deleted ones.

### Graph workflows & recipes
_Checked:_ `GET/POST /api/workflows`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/workflows/[id]`, `POST /versions`, `POST /publish`, `POST /install-recipe`, `/dashboard/workflows/runs/[id]`, `components/workflow/WorkflowEditor` (no page mounts it), `lib/workflow/{schema,triggers}`, `lib/recipes/*` (52 files) + `install-defaults.ts`. Template keys were cross-checked against starters, ensure, seed and the recipes' own `defaultTemplates`.

**Bugs**
- **[COM-47] [Medium] Recipes send templates that are never created** — `src/lib/recipes/*.ts` and `src/lib/recipes/install-defaults.ts:73-92`. 28 template keys referenced by recipe send nodes exist nowhere in the codebase. Among them, five of the nine recipes auto-installed at signup point at missing keys:

  | Installed recipe | Missing template keys |
  |---|---|
  | `birthday_touchpoint` | `happy_birthday` |
  | `kyc_expiry_renew` | `kyc_renew`, `kyc_renew_email` |
  | `nominee_missing` | `nomination_update` |
  | `daily_mis_digest` | `daily_digest_wa`, `daily_digest_email` |
  | `nism_expiry_alert` | `nism_expiry_alert_user`, `nism_expiry_alert_owner` |

  The recipes that do declare `defaultTemplates` never have them installed (nothing reads that field). Once enabled, each send step throws "Template '…' not found", the run fails, and later steps never run. _Fix:_ provision `defaultTemplates` (through `ensureStarterTemplate`) on install and add the missing ones. Add a test that every recipe `templateKey` resolves.
- **[COM-48] [Medium] Workflow graphs are saved and published without validation** — `src/app/api/workflows/route.ts:50`, `[id]/versions/route.ts:31`, `[id]/install-recipe/route.ts:44-52`. Routes accept `graph: z.any()` and pass it straight to `validateGraph()`, which assumes an already-parsed graph. `WorkflowGraphSchema` is never applied. Unknown node types and missing configs (for example a `send_email` without `templateKey`) are stored and publishable, and then every run throws at `WorkflowGraphSchema.parse` in `runner.ts:146`. A body without `nodes` returns 500. install-recipe runs no validation at all. _Fix:_ `WorkflowGraphSchema.safeParse` first, then `validateGraph(parsed.data)`. Re-validate in publish.
- **[COM-49] [Medium] Republishing re-enables paused and deleted workflows** — `src/app/api/workflows/[id]/publish/route.ts:80-90`. Publish always sets `enabled: true, pausedReason: null` and materialises enabled triggers. Republishing a workflow that was paused (or auto-paused) or soft-deleted (`pausedReason:"deleted"`) silently turns it back on. _Fix:_ keep the current enabled flag (or require an explicit "publish & enable"), and refuse on deleted workflows.
- **[COM-50] [Low] Run detail's "Back to workflow" link is a 404, and recipe workflows cannot be managed from the UI** — `src/app/dashboard/workflows/runs/[id]/page.tsx:64`. The link goes to `/dashboard/workflows/<id>`, which does not exist. No page mounts `WorkflowEditor`, and the documented `/dashboard/workflows/recipes/<key>` is gone. Signup-installed recipe workflows therefore cannot be reviewed, enabled or published from the product. _Fix:_ link to automations history, and mount or retire the graph editor.
- **[COM-51] [Low] Workflow PATCH accepts any category and nothing is audited** — `src/app/api/workflows/[id]/route.ts:22-28,72-90`. PATCH accepts any string for `category`/`recurrencePolicy` (enum column: invalid values give a 500 or bad data). PATCH and DELETE write no audit. GET lists soft-deleted workflows. _Fix:_ validate with enums, audit, and filter `pausedReason='deleted'`.

**Improvements**
- `GET /api/workflows` and `/[id]` have no capability check (workflow graphs, including webhook URLs and headers, are readable by any user).
- Concurrent publishes are not serialised (no `FOR UPDATE` on the draft), and `materializeTriggers`' delete-then-insert can race. Lock the workflow row.

---

## Firm, team, HR & practice finance

### Employees, invite, offboarding, org chart
_Checked:_ /dashboard/employees (list, [id], new, import, compliance, org); GET /api/employees; GET/PATCH/DELETE(?trash/?purge) /api/employees/[id]; POST restore, invite, import, accept-invite; lib/offboarding.ts; EmployeeRowActions / EmployeeEditForm (deactivate, offboard, trash, edit, exit-handover panel).

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-01] [Critical] Password hash and TOTP secret are written to the audit log, and the employee page sends them to the browser** ✔ _verified_ — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:243-252` (also `:349-357` and `:375-384`); `src/app/dashboard/employees/[id]/page.tsx:84-92,291`. `employee.update`, `employee.trash` and `employee.offboard` audit `before: existing, after: next`, where `existing` comes from `tx.select().from(users)`. That is the full row: `passwordHash`, `twoFaSecret` and `twoFaBackupCodes`. `capPayload` keeps it whole under 4 KB. Over 4 KB it still keeps the first ~3.8 KB as `preview`, and `passwordHash` is the 6th column. The employee detail page loads the last 50 `audit_log` rows for the user with `a.before, a.after` and passes `logs={data.logs}` to the client `EmployeeEditForm`. The whole JSON therefore ends up in the RSC payload. That page has no capability check (ORG-02). Scenario: an RM opens `/dashboard/employees/<owner-id>` and reads the owner's bcrypt hash and TOTP secret from the page source, which defeats the owner's 2FA. _Fix:_ write an explicit allow-list of fields (or a redacted diff) to the audit row; never select `before`/`after` into a client prop; scrub the audit rows already stored.
- **[ORG-02] [Medium] Employee pages have no access check, even though the API restricts the same data** — `src/app/dashboard/employees/[id]/page.tsx:18-25`, `src/app/dashboard/employees/page.tsx:29`. `GET /api/employees/[id]` requires `settings.users` ("HR/PII … owner/admin only"). The `[id]` page has no such check. Any signed-in user gets a colleague's custom permissions, targets, leave balance, compliance documents, task list, audit trail and the full edit form. The PATCH is rejected, but everything is readable. The list page shows every RM's AUM and annual target, plus offboard/trash buttons to everyone. _Fix:_ `requireCap(..., "settings.users")` on `[id]`, `new` and `import`; on the list, keep a directory-only view for everyone else.
- **[ORG-03] [Medium] Offboarding says it redistributes clients, but it only creates a nameless task and leaves leads and reports with the leaver** — `src/lib/offboarding.ts:107-138`, `src/components/employees/EmployeeRowActions.tsx:176`. The confirm text says "their clients are redistributed". In fact `investors.rm_id` is untouched and one task is created. Its title reads "Reassign N clients of departing employee" without naming who left. Clients are counted only with `status = 'active'`, so `redemption_risk` and other live clients are left out of the count. Nothing re-points `leads.owner_id`, service requests (`assigned_to_user_id`), claims or direct reports (`users.reporting_manager_id`). Scenario: an RM with 40 open leads leaves. The leads keep an offboarded owner and drop off every follow-up queue. _Fix:_ name the leaver in the task; count live clients (`aliveClient`); reassign leads, SRs and direct reports to the same target (or list them in the task); correct the dialog text.
- **[ORG-04] [Medium] Reporting manager is not checked for self, cycles, tenant or active status** — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:56,186`; `src/app/dashboard/employees/org/page.tsx:46-52`. `reportingManagerId` is saved as sent. With a cycle (A→B, B→A) neither person is a root, so both silently disappear from the org chart along with everyone under them. Each of them can also approve the other's leave and regularizations (delegated approval, ORG-12), and each gets the other's book as "team" on the dashboards (`reportsOf`). Setting yourself as your own manager is accepted by the API (the UI only filters it out). _Fix:_ reject self, cross-tenant, non-active and cycle-forming managers with a recursive CTE check in PATCH.
- **[ORG-05] [Medium] The last-owner guard counts trashed owners** — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:28-35`. `countOtherActiveOwners` checks `status = 'active'` but not `deleted_at IS NULL`. A trashed owner keeps `status='active'` and cannot log in. Scenario: owners A and B. B is moved to Trash. Someone with `settings.users` then trashes or offboards A; the guard counts B and allows it. The firm is left with no usable owner. _Fix:_ add `AND deleted_at IS NULL` to the guard.
- **[ORG-06] [Medium] Offboarded and trashed staff and expired invites use up seats permanently** — `src/app/api/employees/invite/route.ts:70`, `src/app/api/employees/import/route.ts:62-63`. Seats used = users with `status <> 'suspended'`, which includes `offboarded` and trashed users, plus invites with `accepted_at IS NULL AND revoked_at IS NULL`, ignoring `expires_at`. Scenario: a 10-seat firm offboards 2 RMs. It still cannot invite their replacements, and every expired invite holds a seat forever. _Fix:_ count `status IN ('active','invited') AND deleted_at IS NULL` and only invites with `expires_at > now()`.
- **[ORG-07] [Low] The exit-handover panel shows things that did not happen** — `src/components/employees/EmployeeEditForm.tsx:1079-1105`. For status `offboarding` it says "Sessions terminated… Revoked", but the PATCH only revokes on `suspended`/`offboarded` (`route.ts:226-235`), so that user can still sign in. The client-handover progress bar is a hard-coded `width: "30%"`. _Fix:_ derive both from real state, or revoke sessions on `offboarding` too.
- **[ORG-08] [Low] Moving someone to Trash leaves their work with nobody** — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:334-360`. A trashed user cannot log in, but their open tasks, upcoming meetings and leads stay assigned to them. No handover task is created and their manager is not notified. _Fix:_ create the same "reassign" task as offboarding, or block Trash while open work exists.

**Improvements**
- Reactivating an offboarded user (status → active) does not clear `offboarded_at`, and nothing undoes the cascade. Say so in the UI.
- Expose `reportingManagerId` and branch in the invite flow so new hires don't land as "Incomplete" roots in the org chart.
- Clear the session revocation cache on access changes too (only done on deactivation now).

### Branches
_Checked:_ /dashboard/branches (BranchesManager); GET/POST /api/branches; PATCH/DELETE /api/branches/[id].

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-09] [Low] Branch delete checks only users, and the HQ guard can be bypassed** — `src/app/api/branches/[id]/route.ts:135-157,77`. Investors, leads, families, policies, claims and proposals all reference `branches.id` with a plain FK. Deleting a branch that still has clients fails with a raw FK error (500 / "internal") instead of "reassign N clients first". `isHq` can be set on several branches, or unset on the only HQ and that branch then deleted. `headUserId` is not checked for tenant or active status. _Fix:_ count dependent rows the way users are counted; enforce exactly one HQ; validate the head user.

**Improvements**
- Setting a branch head on the branch does not set that user's `branch_id` or role, so the branch dashboard lock doesn't follow. Sync them or warn.

### Attendance (clock, regularisation, bulk, holidays, settings, export)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/attendance (+grid, holidays, regularizations, settings); GET/POST /api/attendance; POST clock, bulk; GET export; GET/POST/DELETE holidays; GET/PUT settings; GET/POST regularizations; PATCH regularizations/[id]; daily-ops-scanner attendance seed.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-10] [Medium] Approving or cancelling leave never updates attendance, so the payroll export is wrong** — `src/app/api/leave-applications/[id]/route.ts:236-252`, `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:1085-1120`. Approved leave reaches attendance only through the once-a-day seed, which uses `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` and covers today only. Scenarios: sick leave approved at noon for today, after the seed ran, stays "Absent". A leave filed and approved after the employee returns leaves those past days "Absent". A cancelled leave stays "On Leave". The payroll CSV (`/api/attendance/export`) pays or docks the wrong days. _Fix:_ in the approve/cancel transaction, upsert `on_leave` (or revert auto rows) for every date in the range, skipping manual and clock rows.
- **[ORG-11] [Medium] A branch head can correct their own attendance and approve their own regularization** — `src/app/api/attendance/route.ts:59-64`, `src/app/api/attendance/bulk/route.ts:228`, `src/app/api/attendance/regularizations/[id]/route.ts:193-205`. The manual upsert and bulk mark accept any `employeeUserId`/ids, including the caller's own. Regularization approval has no segregation-of-duties check (leave has one at `leave-applications/[id]/route.ts:220`). A branch head can turn their own "Absent" into "Present" with times, or file a correction and approve it. None of these routes is limited to the branch head's own branch. _Fix:_ reject self-edits and self-approval except by an owner; scope `branch_head` to `users.branch_id = session.branchId`.
- **[ORG-12] [Medium] The reporting manager can approve by API but not in the UI** — `src/app/dashboard/attendance/regularizations/page.tsx:15-35`, `src/app/dashboard/leave-applications/page.tsx:34`, `src/components/leave/LeaveTable.tsx:143`. Both PATCH routes allow "the applicant's own reporting manager", and the POST notifies that manager. But the regularizations page shows non-managers only their own rows, and the leave table shows Approve/Reject only to owner/branch_head. The manager follows the notification link and finds nothing to act on. _Fix:_ include direct reports' rows and pass a per-row `canDecide`.
- **[ORG-13] [Medium] Any user can read everyone's punch location and IP** — `src/app/api/attendance/route.ts:35-50`. `GET /api/attendance` returns `SELECT a.*` for the whole firm to any session. That includes `clock_in_lat/lng`, `clock_out_lat/lng` and `clock_in_ip`. _Fix:_ return own rows (plus reports for managers) and drop the geo/IP columns except for managers.
- **[ORG-14] [Medium] "Require geolocation on clock-in" has no effect** — `src/app/api/attendance/clock/route.ts:144-199`, `src/components/attendance/ClockWidget.tsx:27-46`. `requireGeo` is saved and shown in settings, but neither the clock route nor the widget reads it. A denied or missing location still clocks in. The coordinates are also self-reported, with no geofence. _Fix:_ load the setting and return 400 when `lat`/`lng` are missing; optionally check against the branch location.
- **[ORG-15] [Low] Export summary merges employees with the same name** — `src/app/api/attendance/export/route.ts:111-119`. The per-employee summary is keyed by `employee_name`, so two "Rahul Sharma"s get one combined payroll line. _Fix:_ key by `employee_user_id`.
- **[ORG-16] [Low] Clock-in date comes from the database clock, not IST** — `src/app/api/attendance/clock/route.ts:163,186`. The clock route and the page's `myToday` use `current_date`. The page's stats use `ist.date`. On a UTC database, a punch between 00:00 and 05:30 IST lands on the previous day, and a clock-out after IST midnight on an IST database fails with "clock in first". _Fix:_ pass the IST date from the app (`indiaNow()`).

**Improvements**
- A manager cannot clear a wrong clock time (the upsert uses `COALESCE`). Allow explicit null.
- `POST /api/attendance` doesn't check that `employeeUserId` belongs to the tenant or that the date is valid; a bad date returns 500.
- Regularizations can be filed for future dates, and cancelling an approved one leaves the corrected attendance row in place.

### Leave applications
_Checked:_ /dashboard/leave-applications (list, timeline, new, settings); GET/POST /api/leave-applications; PATCH [id] (approve/reject/cancel); GET conflicts, preview-days; GET/PUT settings; lib/leave/*.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-17] [High] Every leave request and its reason is visible to every employee** — `src/app/dashboard/leave-applications/page.tsx:42-97,64-72`, `src/components/leave/LeaveTimeline.tsx:304`. The API GET is limited to own + direct reports because reasons are "HR-sensitive (medical / bereavement / personal)". The page runs its own SQL with only `la.tenant_id`, for both the list and the Team Timeline (reason shown in the cell tooltip). Any RM can read every colleague's medical reasons. `new/page.tsx:18-28` also sends every employee's custom entitlements and usage to the browser. _Fix:_ apply the API's scope (`employee_user_id = me OR reports`; owners see all) in the page queries; hide reasons outside that scope.
- **[ORG-18] [Medium] A branch head can decide any leave in the firm, including one they filed themselves** — `src/app/api/leave-applications/[id]/route.ts:208-235`, `src/app/api/leave-applications/route.ts:78-83`. `isManager` includes every `branch_head`, with no branch check, so a branch head can approve or reject leave for staff in other branches and for other branch heads. A branch head can file leave "on behalf of" a friend (allowed for managers) and then approve it: the maker-checker check only compares against the employee, not the filer. _Fix:_ limit branch heads to their own branch; record `filed_by` and block filer = approver.
- **[ORG-19] [Medium] Leave balance is never enforced, and cancelling taken leave restores it** — `src/app/api/leave-applications/route.ts:110-125`, `src/app/api/leave-applications/[id]/route.ts:253-265`. The balance is only a warning in the form. POST and approve accept any number of days beyond the entitlement, including `comp_off` with 0 entitled. The applicant can also cancel an already-approved leave whose dates have passed: "cancel" is allowed on `approved` at any time. Scenario: take 5 days, cancel them a month later, get 5 days of balance back. _Fix:_ check the balance on POST and approve (respecting per-employee and firm entitlements); allow only managers to cancel approved leave once it has started.
- **[ORG-20] [Low] FY usage window is wrong, and the employee page ignores firm policy** — `src/app/dashboard/leave-applications/new/page.tsx:26`, `src/app/dashboard/employees/[id]/page.tsx:56-61,162`. Usage is `from_date >= fyStart` with no upper bound. Next-FY leave approved in March counts against the current FY, and a Mar 30–Apr 2 leave counts entirely in the old FY. The employee page calls `getEntitlement(t, custom)` without the tenant entitlements, so it shows built-in defaults (12/6/15) even when the firm has set 10/5/12. _Fix:_ bound the window to `[fyStart, fyEnd]` and pro-rate leaves that span it; pass `loadLeaveEntitlements`.
- **[ORG-21] [Low] "Carry forward earned leave" is saved but never applied** — `src/app/api/leave-applications/settings/route.ts:79-82`. `carryForwardEarned` is stored and shown, but nothing in balances reads it. _Fix:_ apply it in the balance calculation, or remove the toggle.

**Improvements**
- A half-day on a weekly-off or holiday still charges 0.5 (`working-days.ts:128`). Reject it or charge 0.
- Leave approver notifications go to offboarded owners and branch heads too (no `status='active'` filter, `route.ts:128-133`).
- `/api/leave-applications/conflicts` lets anyone list another department's leave by passing any `employeeId`.

### Targets / branch targets / KRA / business targets
_Checked:_ PUT /api/targets; GET/POST /api/branch-targets; GET/POST /api/team/targets; PUT /api/business-targets; /dashboard/team, /team/scorecard, /team/scorecard/targets (TargetGrid).

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-22] [Medium] Read-only roles can write KRA and branch targets** — `src/app/api/team/targets/route.ts:41-45`, `src/app/api/branch-targets/route.ts:55-58`. Both POSTs are gated on `dashboard.view.owner`, a *view* capability. `rbac.ts` grants every pure-view capability to `viewer` automatically and also gives it to `compliance`. A Viewer (an auditor or partner) can therefore rewrite every RM's FY targets and every branch's targets. Neither route writes an audit row. Branch heads (who hold the cap) can also set targets for other branches and for the owner. _Fix:_ gate the writes on `settings.dashboard` (or a new `targets.manage`), scope branch heads to their branch, and audit before/after.
- **[ORG-23] [Medium] Any user can set their own sales targets** — `src/app/api/targets/route.ts:39-42`. Non-managers may PUT their own `annualTarget`, `sipMonthlyTarget` and meeting targets. Scenario: an RM sets the annual target to ₹1 and every achievement tile shows 100%+. _Fix:_ only managers set targets; at most let staff *propose* one.
- **[ORG-24] [Low] Branch targets can be duplicated** — `src/app/api/branch-targets/route.ts:164-203`. Select-then-insert with no unique key, so a double-submit or two tabs create two rows for (branch, metric, FY). Readers that sum or pick an arbitrary row then misreport. `branchId` is not checked as an existing branch. _Fix:_ add a unique index and use `ON CONFLICT`; validate the branch.

**Improvements**
- Two target systems live side by side (`users.annual_target` / `sip_monthly_target` vs `kra_targets` vs `annual_targets`). Pick one source per metric and drop the editor for the other.
- `/dashboard/team?from=garbage` → `new Date()` → `toISOString()` throws, giving a 500.

### Business entries (closing entries)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/business-entries (list, new, [id], [id]/edit, dashboard, liquid); GET/POST /api/business-entries; GET/PATCH/DELETE [id]; POST [id]/email; POST bulk; lib/business/create-entry.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-25] [High] Cancelling or editing an entry leaves its SIPs in the SIP book** _(Same issue as PF-07.)_ — `src/lib/business/create-entry.ts:101,184-188`; `src/app/api/business-entries/[id]/route.ts:108-138,215-218`; `bulk/route.ts:59-61`. The form calls `insertBusinessEntry(..., { registerSips: true })`, which inserts active `sips` rows at once, even for a `draft`. Cancelling (DELETE or bulk delete), or PATCHing the lines (amount, scheme, removing the SIP line), never touches those rows. Scenario: an RM books a ₹50k/month SIP as a draft and cancels it. The SIP book, `investors.active_sip_amount`, SIP reminders and every dashboard SIP figure keep a SIP that does not exist. _Fix:_ link sips to the entry (`source_entry_id`); on cancel, mark them cancelled and `refreshActiveSipAmount`; on line edits, sync them; register only once the entry is confirmed.
- **[ORG-26] [Medium] Cancelled entries can be emailed, which brings them back into the book** — `src/app/api/business-entries/[id]/email/route.ts:36-43,163-166`. There is no status check. The client gets a "transaction confirmation" for a cancelled booking, and the stamp sets `status='emailed'`. Reports exclude only `cancelled` (`report.ts:34`), so the cancelled business counts again. _Fix:_ refuse `cancelled` (and probably `draft`); never move a cancelled entry to `emailed`.
- **[ORG-27] [Medium] An RM can see, and email, business outside their own book** — `src/app/dashboard/business-entries/dashboard/page.tsx:60-77`, `liquid/page.tsx:15-18`, `[id]/page.tsx:34`; `src/app/api/business-entries/route.ts:83-90`. The list API limits RMs to their own entries, but the dashboard (whole-firm summary plus an RM filter for any colleague), the Liquid report (client names and parked amounts firm-wide) and the entry detail page do not. POST and PATCH check only that `investorId` is in the tenant, not in the RM's book. An RM can link another RM's client and use "Email" to send that client mail. PATCH `rmId` is not checked against the tenant. _Fix:_ apply `seesAllEntries` / `investorScopeCondition` on these pages and on investor linking; validate `rmId`.

**Improvements**
- Entry edits are not limited by status: an RM can rewrite the amounts of a confirmed or emailed entry. Consider locking it after confirmation.
- Bulk `assign` accepts an offboarded user as RM.

### Practice finances (P&L, expenses, recurring, budgets)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/finances (hub, pnl, expenses, progress); GET/POST /api/business-expenses; DELETE [id]; GET/PUT budgets; GET/POST recurring (create, post-month); PATCH/DELETE recurring/[id]; lib/finance/practice-summary.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-28] [Medium] The P&L leaves out insurance commission** — `src/lib/finance/practice-summary.ts:55-86`. Income is only `amc_payouts`. The `insurance_commission_entries` ledger ("actual commission the firm RECEIVED") is never added.
