For a firm with insurance business, the P&L, margin, YoY and monthly trend understate income, and the firm can show a loss it doesn't have. _Fix:_ add an insurance income line from `insurance_commission_entries` (gross / TDS / net by `entry_date`).
- **[ORG-29] [Low] Deletes are one click, with no confirmation and no error handling** — `src/components/finances/ExpenseLedger.tsx:124-132`, `RecurringExpenses.tsx:83-96`, `src/components/tech-stack/TechStackRegister.tsx:119-127`. A single click hard-deletes an expense (salaries included) or a recurring template. The row is removed from the UI before the request, and a 403/500 is ignored (`fetch` doesn't throw), so the row disappears on screen but stays in the database. _Fix:_ `confirmDialog()`, check `res.ok`, restore the row on failure.
- **[ORG-30] [Low] Malformed periods and dates return 500** — `src/app/api/business-expenses/recurring/route.ts:36,86-99`; `route.ts:23`. `period` `2026-13` passes `^\d{4}-\d{2}$` and builds `2026-13-01::date`, a 500. `expenseDate` is only `min(8)`, so any 8-character string reaches Postgres. Posting recurring entries writes ledger rows with no audit (manual ones are audited), and the UI can only post the current month. _Fix:_ validate month 01–12 and ISO dates; audit postings; add a month picker.

**Improvements**
- Expenses cannot be edited (only deleted and re-entered), which loses the audit continuity.
- Show TDS as a receivable instead of netting it out of "Net income".

### Commission & brokerage reconciliation
_Checked:_ /dashboard/commission-reconciliation (PayoutImport, ReconcileButton, PeriodPicker); GET /api/commission-reconciliation; POST /api/commission/payouts; POST /api/commission/reconcile; lib/commission/reconciler, sub_distributor_payouts.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-31] [High] Pasting a payout statement twice doubles the income, with no way to undo** — `src/app/api/commission/payouts/route.ts:53-69`. Every POST blindly inserts rows. There is no dedup key, no replace-period option, and no delete endpoint or UI anywhere. Scenario: back office pastes the HDFC statement, gets a network hiccup and pastes again. `received` doubles in the reconciliation, the P&L (`practice-summary.ts` sums `amc_payouts`) and the YoY figures, permanently. _Fix:_ add an idempotency key per statement (amc, scheme, period, type, amount) or "replace this period's manual rows"; add a delete/void path.
- **[ORG-32] [Medium] The reconciliation page is open to every user, including partner bank details** — `src/app/dashboard/commission-reconciliation/page.tsx:29-35`; `src/app/api/commission-reconciliation/route.ts:25`. The page only calls `requireSession()`. Any RM sees firm brokerage, shortfalls and sub-distributor payouts, which `computeSubDistributorPayouts` returns with `bankDetails`. `rbac.ts` deliberately keeps RMs out of `reports.commission`. The JSON route also accepts `dashboard.view.owner`, so Viewers get partner bank accounts. _Fix:_ `requireCap("reports.commission")` on the page and the API; drop `bankDetails` from the payload.
- **[ORG-33] [Medium] YTD is empty from January to March** — `src/app/dashboard/commission-reconciliation/page.tsx:75`, `src/app/api/commission-reconciliation/route.ts:63`. YTD is `period >= '<calendar year>-04'` with no upper bound. In Feb 2027 that means `>= '2027-04'`, so the YTD tiles read ₹0. _Fix:_ use the FY start (`currentFyLabel`) and bound the window to the FY.
- **[ORG-34] [Medium] Expected brokerage uses today's AUM for every period** — `src/lib/commission/reconciler.ts:204-220`. Expected = current `folios.schemes[].value` × trail / 12, whatever period is being reconciled. Re-running August after a 10% market move changes August's expected amount and the anomaly flags. It also counts folios of exited and merged clients. _Fix:_ use `rm_snapshots` / `firm_snapshots` or the period's month-end valuation; skip dead clients; at least label the figure "estimated on today's AUM".
- **[ORG-35] [Medium] A sub-distributor is paid twice on a client reached through more than one lead** — `src/lib/commission/sub_distributor_payouts.ts:89-96`. `SUM(i.aum)` runs over the leads × investors join. Two leads from the same partner converted into one investor (a re-enquiry, a duplicate lead) add that investor's AUM twice, while `COUNT(DISTINCT i.id)` shows 1. The partner share is overstated. Only `status='active'` clients count, so `redemption_risk` clients are dropped. _Fix:_ aggregate distinct investors first (`SELECT DISTINCT sd.id, i.id …`), then sum; use the live-client definition.

**Improvements**
- Payout `amc_code` is free text; normalise it to the folio's AMC code, or "HDFC" vs "H" never matches and every line shows as an anomaly.
- Commission records for (amc, scheme) keys that disappear on re-reconcile are never cleaned up.

### Business impacts
_Checked:_ GET/POST /api/business-impacts; /dashboard/reports/impact-attribution; RecordBusinessImpact.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-36] [Low] Impact entries cannot be corrected and are not reviewed** — `src/app/api/business-impacts/route.ts:41-83`. Amounts are self-reported, with no edit, delete, dedup or review. A slip (₹10 Cr typed for ₹10 L), or recording the same save twice, inflates "AUM impact" on the attribution report forever. _Fix:_ add void/edit with audit, and dedup on (investor, outcome, sourceId).

**Improvements**
- GET ignores the client scope (`investor.view.all` only); fine today, but scope it if the cap is widened.

### Induction
_Checked:_ /dashboard/induction (+[lessonId], exam, result); POST /api/induction/progress, /exam (start/submit); GET/POST/DELETE /api/induction/tracks; lib/induction/engine.

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-37] [Low] A trainee who passed can retake and end up "failed"** — `src/app/api/induction/exam/route.ts:47-87,148-151`. `start` doesn't check `enrolment.status === 'passed'`. A failed retake sets the enrolment to `failed` while the letter token and `validUntil` from the pass stay live. The max-attempts message says "Your manager has been notified", but no notification is sent. With several enrolments, `.limit(1)` picks one at random. _Fix:_ block or ignore retakes after a pass; send the notification; select the enrolment explicitly.

**Improvements**
- Watch progress is self-reported (one POST with `watchedSeconds=43200` satisfies it). That is acceptable as documented, but cap each beat's increment by the time since the last beat.

### Sub-distributors, firm directors, legal registrations, tech stack
_Checked:_ /dashboard/sub-distributors (list/new/[id]); GET/POST /api/sub-distributors, GET/PATCH/DELETE [id], GET lookup; GET/POST /api/firm-directors, GET/PATCH/DELETE [id]; /dashboard/legal-registrations (list/new/[id]); GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/legal-registrations; scanner 12/12b; /dashboard/tech-stack; GET/POST /api/tech-stack, DELETE [id].

**Bugs**
- **[ORG-38] [Low] The licence-expiry event fires every day, including for perpetual licences** — `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:535-571`. Dedup is per `scanDate`, so `legal_registration.expiring` is emitted once a day for each of the 90 days before expiry. A workflow subscribed to it sends about 90 reminders. Unlike the lapse step (`:584`), the query doesn't exclude `renewal_frequency = 'perpetual'`, though the UI promises perpetual licences "never alarm". _Fix:_ emit at fixed milestones (90/60/30/7/1) and exclude perpetual.
- **[ORG-39] [Low] Tech-stack annual spend includes discontinued tools** — `src/components/tech-stack/TechStackRegister.tsx:62-65`. "Total annual spend" sums every row, discontinued and evaluating included. There is no edit path (POST/DELETE only), so changing a renewal date means deleting and re-entering. _Fix:_ sum `status = 'active'` only; add PATCH.

**Improvements**
- Employee EUIN, personal ARN and DSC expiries (`users.euin_expiry_at`, `arn_expiry_at`, `dsc_expiry_at`) have no scanner event or recipe, only the NISM one. An RM can keep selling on a lapsed EUIN unnoticed. Add them to the daily scan.
- Firm directors' shareholding is not checked to total ≤ 100%.
- Sub-distributor `commissionSplitPct` accepts 100% with no warning, which means the firm keeps nothing.

---

## Compliance, audit & reports

### Reports — catalog, hub and the generic viewer (`/dashboard/reports/[key]`)
_Checked:_ `src/lib/reports/catalog.ts` (every catalog key resolved to a page, so no broken hub links), `reports/page.tsx`, `reports/layout.tsx`, all 52 SQL reports in `reports/[key]/page.tsx`, `ReportExportButtons`, the hub cards for manager-only boards.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-01] [High] The generic report viewer has no capability gate and no viewer scope** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/[key]/page.tsx:1357-1366` (gate: `reports/layout.tsx:20-27`). The layout admits anyone with `investor.view.assigned` or `investor.view.all` (RM, insurance_team, service_team, back_office, ops). The layout's own comment says each manager page must "gate itself", but `[key]` only calls `requireSession()` and then runs firm-wide SQL. As a result, any RM can open `/dashboard/reports/rm_business`, `employee_tasks`, `team_productivity`, `open_complaints` (client names and complaint subjects), `high_aum` (the firm's top 100 clients), `unmapped_family_members` (full unmasked PAN) or `campaign_*` (spend), and export each one as CSV. None of these queries goes through `investorScopeSql`, so the per-tenant data wall and the branch lock are both bypassed. _Fix:_ give each registry entry a required capability and a scope fragment (`investorScopeSql` or the `MetricScope` helpers), and refuse the page when the capability is missing.
- **[RPT-02] [High] Money-flow reports leave out every RTA-imported transaction and use the wrong type rules** — `reports/[key]/page.tsx:39-86` (daily_lumpsum, redemption, net_sales) and `:449-471, :609-633, :1237-1257` (amc_business, scheme_category, aum_movement). The RTA importer stores `status='processed'` (`src/lib/rta/process.ts:597-600`). Daily Purchase, Daily Redemption and Net Business, which is in `DEFAULT_MOST_USED`, filter `status = 'successful'` only, so they show only manual rows, or nothing. Daily Purchase also filters `type='lumpsum'` and misses the canonical `purchase` type. Redemption leaves out `swp`. Net, AMC, category and AUM-movement figures add or subtract by `direction`, so switch-in/out, STP and dividend-reinvest rows count as gross sales or redemption. That contradicts `lib/metrics/business.ts` (`GROSS_TYPES`, `REDEMPTION_TYPES`, `PROCESSED_TXN_STATUSES`), so these reports never agree with the Owner Dashboard. _Fix:_ build these reports on `lib/metrics/business.ts`.
- **[RPT-03] [Medium] SIP figures are raw amounts and include dead clients** — `[key]/page.tsx:25-37, 105-117, 582-607, 234-253`; `src/lib/finance/sip-lifecycle.ts:252-262`.
  - `active_sip_book` and `rm_sip_book` sum `sips.amount` whatever the frequency, so a ₹30k quarterly SIP reads as ₹30k a month. They also LEFT JOIN investors with no deleted, merged or closed filter.
  - `daily_sip_added` counts every SIP row by `created_at`, including rejected ones.
  - `rm_sip_book.stopped_mtd` uses `updated_at`, so any later edit re-dates a stop.
  - On the SIP Expiry page, the "₹/mo at risk" KPI sums raw amounts, and its summary does not join investors while the table does. The KPI count can therefore exceed the rows shown.

  The SIP book definition is `sipMonthlySql` over live clients. _Fix:_ use `sipMonthlySql` + `aliveClient`.
- **[RPT-04] [Medium] Client-book reports use `status='active'` instead of the live-client definition** — `[key]/page.tsx:119-180, 958-990, 1283-1355`.
  - rm_business, rm_clients, high_aum, review_pending, branch_business, segmentation and sip_topup leave out live clients that are `dormant`, `inactive` or `redemption_risk`, and most do not exclude merged rows.
  - **Dormant Clients (`:151-164`) requires `status='active'`, so clients actually marked `dormant` never appear.**
  - rm_business and rm_clients list only users with the `rm` role and have no "unassigned" row. Clients held by branch heads or team members, and unassigned clients, drop out, so the column totals do not equal the Owner Dashboard's clients or AUM.

  _Fix:_ use `aliveClient` and the RM ledger pattern with synthetic rows.
- **[RPT-05] [Medium] Soft-deleted tasks and leads are counted** — `[key]/page.tsx:916-956, 1181-1235, 1305-1329, 196-211, 1032-1180`. `tasks.deleted_at` is never filtered in employee_tasks, rm_pending or team_productivity, so a deleted task still reads as overdue against the person. Deleted leads (`lost_reason='__deleted__'`, `lib/metrics/leads.ts:16`) are counted in lead_conversion, `leads_owned` and all four blueprint reports. _Fix:_ add `deleted_at IS NULL` / `leadWhere()`.
- **[RPT-06] [Medium] Day and month buckets are computed in UTC** — `[key]/page.tsx:33, 47, 62, 80, 524, 593-596, 928-930, 1269` (e.g. `created_at::date`, `current_date`, `date_trunc('month', now())`). The pool sets no session TimeZone (`src/db/client.ts:45-62`), while `lib/metrics` explicitly converts with `AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'`.
  - A SIP registered on the 1st between 00:00 and 05:30 IST lands in the previous month's "SIP Added This Month".
  - Daily reports file early-morning IST activity under the previous day.
  - `employee_tasks.due_today` is wrong for 5½ hours a day.

  This is separate from the already-reported FY-boundary issue. _Fix:_ bucket on `(ts AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata')::date`.
- **[RPT-07] [Low] Lead drop-off counts can multiply** — `[key]/page.tsx:1053-1060`. `lead_stage_history` is joined to `blueprint_stages` on `stage_key` only, with no `lbi.blueprint_id = bs.blueprint_id`. With more than one active `leads` blueprint, or a stage key that appears in two blueprints, every history row is counted once per blueprint. _Fix:_ also join on blueprint id.
- **[RPT-08] [Low] Silent truncation** — most `[key]` reports end in `LIMIT 100/200/500`, and the header shows "N rows" with no "more" indicator; CSV export carries only the truncated set. Example: `review_pending` (`:166-180`) returns every client with any `next_review_at`, sorted ascending and cut at 100. A firm with 3,000 clients sees an arbitrary first 100, not "overdue or upcoming" as the description says. _Fix:_ filter to a window and show a truncation notice.

**Improvements**
- "AUM Movement" (`:1237`) shows only flows and no AUM; rename it or add opening and closing AUM from `firm_snapshots`.
- `daily_*` reports are fixed at a 30-day or 90-day window with no period picker; reuse the dashboard `period` filter.
- Hide hub cards the viewer cannot open (see RPT-18) instead of listing all 87 to everyone.

### Reports — dedicated report pages and `/api/reports/*` boards
_Checked:_ business-mis, growth-mis, net-sales, efforts, sip-health, sif-status, branch-rm/[kind], metric/[metric], redemption, sip-rejection, back-office-work, other-services, insurance-*, equity-team, lead-funnel, proposal-conversion, churn-risk, cross-sell, tax-harvesting, risk-drift, referral-radar, impact-attribution, revenue-intelligence, trail-forecast, sip-expiry, kyc-tracker, nomination-tracker, arn-euin-tracker, IC pages, the checklists, data-hygiene API, and the GET/POST routes behind each board.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-09] [Medium] Report pages ignore the branch lock** — the `seesAll` pattern (`reports/churn-risk/page.tsx:26-30`, `cross-sell:45-49`, `tax-harvesting:44-47`, `risk-drift:33-36`, `referral-radar:58-62`, all five `tax/*` pages, e.g. `tax/realised-capital-gains/page.tsx:25-28`) treats `reports.business` / `reports.team` (branch_head) as firm-wide. Other places are similarly unscoped:
  - nomination-tracker, revenue-intelligence and trail-forecast use role literals and query the whole tenant.
  - The `/api/reports/{sip-rejection,back-office-work,branch-rm,redemption,insurance-claims}` routes accept any `?branch=`.

  So a branch head locked to Pune (no `dashboard.view.all_branches`) reads every branch's clients, including PAN on the tax reports. CLAUDE.md says that lock may only be lifted by explicit permission. _Fix:_ derive scope with `isBranchScoped` / `investorScopeSql` and clamp `branch=` to the session branch.
- **[RPT-10] [Medium] SIP Expiry has no capability check and no scope** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/sip-expiry/page.tsx:36-47`; `src/lib/finance/sip-lifecycle.ts:70-112`. Every staff user who passes the reports layout gets the firm's expiring SIPs with client name and PAN, which bypasses the data wall for RMs. _Fix:_ pass the viewer's `investorScopeSql` into `loadSipExpiry` and the summary.
- **[RPT-11] [Medium] Report CSV exports bypass `investor.bulk_export` and are not audited** — `src/components/reports/ReportExportButtons.tsx:14-31`, `src/components/reports/KycTrackerClient.tsx:119-171`, `src/app/api/reports/saved/[id]/export/route.ts:22-102`. The Clients export requires `investor.bulk_export` (compliance only) and writes `investor.bulk_export` to the audit log. The report pages offer the same data with neither:
  - RMs can export their book with PAN from any tax report.
  - KYC tracker exports PAN, email, phone and Aadhaar last-4 for every client.
  - Saved-report export pulls 5,000 rows of any catalog field.

  The two client-side CSV writers also do not neutralise `=+-@` (lead and client names arrive from public forms), so they have the same formula-injection gap as the already-reported exports. _Fix:_ route exports through a server endpoint that checks `investor.bulk_export`, writes an audit row and escapes formulas.
- **[RPT-12] [Medium] "Degrade if the table is missing" breaks the page instead** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/revenue-intelligence/page.tsx:104-111` → `:158`; also `src/lib/finance/sip-health.ts:105-120` and `src/lib/finance/efforts.ts:41`. A failed statement inside `withTenant` aborts the transaction. The `catch` swallows the error, but the next `tx.execute` (the folio query) fails with "current transaction is aborted", so Revenue Intelligence returns a 500 on exactly the environments the comment describes (no `commission_rows`). _Fix:_ wrap the optional query in a savepoint (`tx.transaction`, as `api/search` does) or test `to_regclass()` first.
- **[RPT-13] [Medium] Business MIS AUM disagrees with the AUM metric** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/business-mis/page.tsx:72-74`. It sums `folios.aum` over every folio of the tenant: closed folios and folios of deleted, merged or closed clients are included. For any past FY the headline "AUM (RTA)" is today's AUM printed under that FY. The query also loads every folio with its `schemes` jsonb into Node on each render. _Fix:_ use the metrics AUM over live clients, and for a past FY the month-end `firm_snapshots` value or "—".
- **[RPT-14] [Medium] Lead Funnel conversion does not match the Leads board** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/lead-funnel/page.tsx:39-43, 74-90`.
  - It counts deleted leads (no `lost_reason <> '__deleted__'`).
  - The `DEAD` list leaves out `insurance_lost`.
  - It treats only `converted_at` as won, whereas `lib/metrics/leads.ts` counts any lead in an onboarding stage as Won.

  The conversion rate and won count therefore differ from the dashboard's lead pipeline for the same leads. _Fix:_ use `leadWhere` + `leadStageKeySql`.
- **[RPT-15] [Low] KYC tracker population is wrong** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/kyc-tracker/page.tsx:52-58, 81-97`. It filters `status='active'` only, so soft-deleted and merged rows are included, while dormant, inactive and redemption-risk live clients (whose KYC still matters for SEBI) are excluded. It also uses three separate transactions and an unbounded row load. _Fix:_ use the `aliveClient` predicate in one transaction.
- **[RPT-16] [Low] Nomination tracker overstates the gap** — `src/app/dashboard/reports/nomination-tracker/page.tsx:39-45`. Closed, exited and merged clients and clients with no folios or zero AUM are all counted in coverage. Only the investor-level `nominee_name` is checked, although the catalog says "Folios still missing a nominee", and a recorded opt-out is never recognised. _Fix:_ restrict to live clients with AUM and honour an opt-out flag.
- **[RPT-17] [Low] Data-hygiene "KYC unverified" misses NULLs** — `src/app/api/reports/data-hygiene/route.ts:47, 69`. `kyc_status != 'verified'` is NULL for clients with no status, so the clients most in need of KYC are not counted. `bank_missing` ignores the `bank_accounts` jsonb array. _Fix:_ use `kyc_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'verified'`.
- **[RPT-18] [Low] Manager boards render as empty for non-managers** — e.g. `src/components/reports/GrowthMisBoard.tsx:52-58`. The hub lists every report to everyone. A 403 from `/api/reports/*` is mapped to `null` (`r.ok ? json : null`), so an RM opening Growth MIS, Net Sales or SIP Health sees an empty "no data" board rather than "not available for your role". Compliance-gated pages (KYC tracker, ARN tracker, suitability matrix) throw a bare 403 error page. _Fix:_ filter the catalog by capability and show a clear no-access state.
- **[RPT-19] [Medium] Report-side target and figure writes are unscoped and unaudited** — `src/app/api/reports/sif-status/route.ts:45-60`, `metric-status/route.ts:50-71`, `efforts/route.ts:45-55`. `reports.manage` (back_office) and branch-locked branch heads can overwrite any branch's or desk's SIF targets, branch metric figures and effort plans. No `audit_log` row records who changed a target (`target.update` exists in `AuditAction` but is not used). _Fix:_ clamp to the viewer's branch and call `recordAudit` with before/after.

**Improvements**
- Tax pages call `loadFirmTxGroups` (`src/lib/tax/firm-gains.ts:57-75`), which loads every settled transaction of the firm into Node on each render, with no FY bound, and the pool's `statement_timeout` is 30s. Pre-filter to investors with a sell in the chosen FY.
- Revenue Intelligence, Trail Forecast and Nomination gate on literal role names (`ALLOWED = [...]`), so custom permissions (e.g. `reports.commission`) do nothing; use `can()`.
- Three different "redemption" sources: `[key]` redemption (transactions), Redemption by Client (manual business entries) and dashboards (RTA ledger). Label the source on each page.
- The finance and marketing checklists store ticks in `localStorage` (`ChecklistBox`), so the DDQ binder's state is per-browser, never shared and never reset per period.

### Custom reports (saved reports, runner, scheduled delivery)
_Checked:_ `api/reports/saved` (GET/POST), `[id]` (GET/PATCH/DELETE), `[id]/run|export|send|clone`, `src/lib/reports/run.ts`, `deliver.ts`, `src/workers/report-scheduler.ts`, the custom/new and custom/[id] pages.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-20] [Medium] The runner applies no base filter and no viewer scope** — `src/lib/reports/run.ts:186-201, 214-229, 296-301`. The WHERE is only `tenant_id = $n AND <AST>`. Investor reports include soft-deleted and merged clients, lead reports include `__deleted__` leads, and task reports include deleted tasks, so a "count of clients by RM" never matches the dashboards. A branch-locked branch head (who holds `reports.manage`) gets every branch's PII. _Fix:_ add a per-subject base predicate (`aliveClient`, `leadWhere`, `deleted_at IS NULL`) plus `investorScopeSql`.
- **[RPT-21] [Medium] Private saved reports are readable and runnable by id** — `api/reports/saved/[id]/route.ts:76-84`, `[id]/run/route.ts:26`, `[id]/export/route.ts:30`, `[id]/send/route.ts:34-36`, `[id]/clone/route.ts:28`. The list hides other users' `private` reports, but every by-id route only checks the tenant, so anyone with `reports.manage` who has the id can view, run, export, email or clone someone else's private report. _Fix:_ apply the same `createdById = me OR sharedScope IN (...)` predicate on every by-id route.
- **[RPT-22] [Medium] Report e-mail goes to any external address** — `src/app/api/reports/saved/[id]/send/route.ts:22, 38-49`; schedule schema at `api/reports/saved/route.ts:55-63`. `emails: z.string().email()` accepts any domain, despite the "recipients are INTERNAL staff" comment. A back-office user can email a 200-row client report with PAN and phone columns to a Gmail address now, or daily via a schedule. The audit row records only `recipients: <count>`, and scheduled sends write no audit row. _Fix:_ restrict recipients to active tenant users' emails, or require approval, and log the addresses.
- **[RPT-23] [Low] Matrix totals and schedule edge cases** — `run.ts:251-276`. For `avg`/`min`/`max` the "Total" column and grand total sum the cells (a sum of averages), and this is exported as "Total" (`export/route.ts:85-90`). A monthly schedule with `day` 29–31 never fires in shorter months (`report-scheduler.ts:31`). PATCH validates the merged definition without the `ast` (`[id]/route.ts:111-121`). _Fix:_ show no total for non-additive aggregates and clamp the day to the month's last day.

**Improvements**
- `deliver.ts:94` stamps the email with `new Date().toLocaleString("en-IN")` without a `timeZone`. This is correct only while the container's `TZ=Asia/Kolkata` holds; pass the time zone explicitly.
- The custom report viewer runs `limit=500` with no paging control, although `run` supports `offset`.

### Compliance (DDQ score, audit checklist, registers, expiry watch, vault, inspection pack)
_Checked:_ `dashboard/compliance` (+ command-center, registers, audit-checklist, amfi-circulars, tagline-check), `api/compliance` (GET), `recompute`, `registers` (POST/PATCH), `documents` (GET/POST/DELETE), `documents/[id]/supersede`, `documents/download`, `download-all`, `upload`, `upload-url`, `inspection-pack`, `api/ddq-checklist` (+ autoverify), `lib/compliance/*`.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-24] [High] Any uploader can supersede any document in the vault** — `src/app/api/compliance/documents/[id]/supersede/route.ts:38-56`. Only `document.upload` is required; RM, team_member, insurance_team and service_team all hold it. The lookup is by tenant and id only, with no `vaultScopeCondition` and no firm-document restriction. An RM can therefore supersede the firm's ARN certificate, a DDQ document, or another RM's client's KYC with their own file. The original disappears from the vault (`superseded_by_id` set) and the forged version becomes current. _Fix:_ apply `vaultScopeCondition`, require `compliance.edit` for non-investor documents, and add `WHERE superseded_by_id IS NULL` to the update to close the double-supersede race.
- **[RPT-25] [Medium] Document registration trusts a caller-supplied `storageKey` and client id** — `src/app/api/compliance/documents/route.ts:72-95`. The key is not bound to the tenant prefix (`vaultKey`, `src/lib/storage/s3.ts:164-186`) or to an upload the caller actually made. `relatedEntityId` is not scope-checked, and the GET list returns `storageKey` for every visible document. Two consequences:
  - An RM who once saw a key can register a new row pointing at it under one of their current clients and keep downloading the file after the original client is reassigned. `/download` presigns whatever key the row holds (`documents/download/route.ts:46-77`).
  - If a key from another tenant is known, it can be read.

  Registration is also not audited. _Fix:_ require the key to start with this tenant's prefix and come from a recent upload by the caller, scope `relatedEntityId` through `loadViewableInvestor`, and audit `document.upload`.
- **[RPT-26] [Medium] Deleting a vault document destroys evidence and can hide its whole version chain** — `documents/route.ts:113-135`.
  - It hard-deletes the row with no retention or soft delete for regulatory documents.
  - It deletes the S3 object before the transaction commits, so a later failure leaves a row pointing at a missing file.
  - "not found" is thrown as a plain Error, so it returns 500.
  - Deleting the latest version leaves the earlier version's `superseded_by_id` pointing at a non-existent row (there is no FK; migration 0150). The vault filters `superseded_by_id IS NULL`, so the whole ARN or KYC history disappears from the UI.

  _Fix:_ soft-delete, delete the object after commit, and on delete re-point or clear the predecessor's `superseded_by_id`.
- **[RPT-27] [Medium] DDQ consent score can exceed 100%, and the consent numbers are inconsistent** — `src/lib/compliance/ddq-calculator.ts:154-163`. The numerator is distinct investors with any granted consent among all investors, including deleted, closed and merged. The denominator is `status='active'` investors only, and the score is not capped. Example: 100 active clients and 30 closed clients, all consented, scores 130, which lifts the overall DDQ score. The same mismatch drives the Command Center and the SEBI inspection pack (`api/compliance/inspection-pack/route.ts:34-35`). There, a client with transactional consent granted and marketing consent revoked counts as both granted and revoked, and the "active clients" count includes soft-deleted rows. _Fix:_ count live clients only on both sides, by scope, and cap at 100.
- **[RPT-28] [Medium] DDQ complaint score and freshness are wrong** — `ddq-calculator.ts:78-88`; `src/app/dashboard/compliance/page.tsx:50-70`.
  - An open complaint that is not yet due (`resolved_at IS NULL`) counts as a 21-day failure, so logging a complaint today lowers the score.
  - The web page shows the last saved `ddq_scores` row indefinitely, with no scheduled recompute, while the mobile `GET /api/compliance` computes live. Web and mobile show different scores.
  - branch_head can see the page, but Recalculate needs `compliance.edit`, so it returns 403.

  _Fix:_ score only complaints that are resolved or past their SLA, recompute on read or nightly, and align the gates.
- **[RPT-29] [Medium] Auto-verify overwrites manual DDQ decisions using trivially weak checks** — `src/app/api/ddq-checklist/autoverify/route.ts:40-56`; `src/lib/compliance/ddq-autoverifiers.ts:45-66`.
  - The upsert sets `status='in_place'` and replaces `note` regardless of the existing row, so an item the compliance officer marked `gap` or `not_applicable` with an explanation loses both.
  - The checks are existence tests. `amfi_certs_team` passes if any single active user has a valid NISM, and `euin_team` if any single user has an EUIN; 1 certified RM out of 10 reads as "In place".
  - Items are never downgraded when a check later fails.

  _Fix:_ only fill items that have no manual status, use ratio checks (all distributing staff), and downgrade on failure.
- **[RPT-30] [Low] Compliance registers are not audited and PATCH is loosely validated** — `src/app/api/compliance/registers/route.ts:70-140, 160-185`. Mis-selling, third-party-payment, regulatory-action and unsuitability records, which are exactly what an inspector asks about, write no `audit_log` row. PATCH accepts `z.any()` values (status `"foo"`, a non-boolean `reportedToAmc`, unparseable dates cause a 500), returns `ok` for an id that does not exist, and is writable by ops/back_office through `settings.masters`. _Fix:_ validate per register with zod, return 404 on no row, and audit before/after.
- **[RPT-31] [Low] Third-party-payment digit extraction is broken by template-literal escaping** — `src/lib/compliance/third-party-payments.ts:44, 53, 57`. `'\D'` inside a JS tagged template is cooked to `'D'`, so `regexp_replace(..., 'D', '', 'g')` strips the letter D rather than non-digits. Masks such as `1234 ` or `XX-12-34` produce a last-4 containing non-digits, which gives false "account mismatch" flags and missed matches. _Fix:_ write `'\\D'` or `'[^0-9]'`.
- **[RPT-32] [Low] Expiry watchlist is misleading** — `src/app/dashboard/compliance/page.tsx:113-121` and `api/compliance/route.ts:66-71`. The CKYC rows include deleted and merged investors, and only 3 rows are taken, sorted ascending, so long-expired ones crowd out upcoming expiries. Every NISM/EUIN holder is labelled "(RM)". _Fix:_ use live clients, a window such as expired in the last 90 days or due in the next 90, and the actual role label.

**Improvements**
- `download-all` (`documents/download-all/route.ts:51-66`) does not validate that `ids` are UUIDs (one bad id causes a 500), and the audit row lists ids that were skipped or exceeded the size cap; record only what was zipped.
- The audit-trail integrity score is 100 if any audit row exists; check for gaps instead (e.g. days with mutations but no audit rows).
- `audit_log` is "append-only" in comments only: no trigger or REVOKE stops UPDATE/DELETE under `tenant_isolation`.
- The inspection-pack and audit-log CSV downloads themselves are not audited.

### Audit log (viewer, export, record history API, audit writes)
_Checked:_ `dashboard/audit-log/page.tsx`, `api/audit` (record ChangeLog), `api/audit/export`, `src/lib/audit.ts`, audit coverage of the sensitive actions in these modules.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-33] [High] Record history API leaks full PII diffs to any signed-in user** — `src/app/api/audit/route.ts:31-61`. For any entity type not in `SENSITIVE_ENTITY_TYPES`, including `investor`, `lead`, insurance and proposals, it returns full `before`/`after` with no capability or record-scope check. `investor.update` stores the whole row (PAN, phone, email, address, bank, nominee, DOB; `api/investors/[id]/route.ts:516-520`). A marketing user (no investor view at all), or an RM behind the data wall, can call `/api/audit?entityType=investor&entityId=<another RM's client>` and read that client's PII. _Fix:_ authorise through the parent record (`loadViewableInvestor` / `leadScopeSql`) per entity type, and gate unknown types to compliance.
- **[RPT-34] [Medium] Audit-log pagination silently skips events** — `src/app/dashboard/audit-log/page.tsx:46-52, 64-66`. The cursor is `at.getTime()`, which truncates Postgres microseconds to JS milliseconds, and is compared as `(at, id) < (ms_timestamp, id)`. Every row sharing the boundary row's millisecond, including all rows written in one transaction (`defaultNow()` is the transaction start: autoverify, bulk routes), compares greater and is dropped from the next page. An inspector paging through the trail never sees those events. _Fix:_ put the full-precision `at::text` in the cursor (or keyset on `id` within the same `at`).
- **[RPT-35] [Low] Unvalidated filters and view/export mismatch** — `audit-log/page.tsx:29-30, 44`; `api/audit/export/route.ts:31-41`. `?days=abc` gives NaN, then `toISOString()` throws a RangeError, so a 500. A non-UUID `?user=` gives a 500. The page accepts any `days`, while the export caps at 366, so the exported file can differ from what is on screen. _Fix:_ use one parser for both, clamped to the same choices.
- **[RPT-36] [Low] `recordAudit`'s "never break the business flow" does not hold inside a transaction** — `src/lib/audit.ts:121-145`. A failed insert (e.g. a non-UUID `entityId`) aborts the surrounding Postgres transaction. The catch swallows the error, but every later statement fails. If the audit write is last, `COMMIT` on the aborted transaction silently rolls back the business mutation while the route returns 200. _Fix:_ run the insert in a savepoint (`tx.transaction`) so a failure is really isolated.

**Improvements**
- Sensitive actions in these modules with no audit row: report CSV/XLSX exports and the KYC-tracker export (RPT-11), compliance document registration (RPT-25), the compliance registers (RPT-30), the SIF/metric/effort target writes (RPT-19), scheduled report emails, the audit-log export and the inspection pack.
- `api/audit` gates sensitive types on literal roles that include branch_head, while the audit-log viewer allows owner and compliance only; use one capability.

### Global search (`/api/search`) and Help
_Checked:_ `src/app/api/search/route.ts` (10 groups, scopes, savepoints, trigram indexes in migrations 0032/0135-0137), `dashboard/help/page.tsx` links.

**Bugs**
- **[RPT-37] [Low] Search: LIKE wildcards not escaped, and some scopes are inconsistent** — `src/app/api/search/route.ts:37-41, 115-120, 167-185`.
  - `q="%%"` or `"__"` matches every row in each group, and `COUNT(*) OVER ()` then counts the whole in-scope book on every keystroke.
  - The documents group returns superseded versions (no `superseded_by_id IS NULL`) and shows firm documents only to `compliance.view`, while the vault shows them to `kyc.manage` / `investor.view.all` holders.
  - Meetings ignore attendees (CLAUDE.md: use `attendeeIs`), so a team member attending a meeting cannot find it.

  _Fix:_ escape `%_\`, filter out superseded documents, reuse `canOpenVault` / `seesWholeVault` and `attendeeIs`.

**Improvements**
- `investorScopeSql` is computed twice per request (`invScope`, `listScope`); build it once and alias it.
- Help's quick links (e.g. `/dashboard/workflows/rules`) are shown to roles that cannot open them; filter by capability.

---

## Settings, onboarding, billing & platform admin

### Settings: firm profile, branding, theme, domains
_Checked:_ /dashboard/settings (index + SettingsDirectory links), /settings/organisation, /settings/appearance, /settings/firm-profile (FirmProfileEditor, DomainSettings), PATCH/GET /api/tenant, GET/PATCH /api/tenant/domain, lib/branding/theme.ts, lib/tenant/host.ts, login/forgot/reset branding injection.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-01] [Medium] Appearance and Organisation pages send the whole `tenants` row to any signed-in user** — `src/app/dashboard/settings/appearance/page.tsx:13-30`, `src/app/dashboard/settings/organisation/page.tsx:13-55` — Neither page checks a capability. Both do `tx.select().from(tenants)` and pass `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tenant))` into a client component, so the full row is in the RSC payload. That row holds bank account number/IFSC, authorised-signatory PAN/DIN, GST/TAN, auditor, `onboarding_notes` and `complaint_form_token`; Organisation also sends the subscription and package. `GET /api/tenant` deliberately removes these fields for non-admins. An RM who types `/dashboard/settings/appearance` gets them all (the sidebar hides Settings, but nothing blocks the URL). _Fix:_ gate both pages on `settings.users`, or pass only the branding fields.
- **[SET-02] [Medium] Saving the firm profile returns a 500 for any firm without an incorporation date** — `src/components/settings/FirmProfileEditor.tsx:62` + `src/app/api/tenant/route.ts:94` — The editor sets `incorporationDate: tenant.incorporationDate ?? ""` and posts it on every save. The schema is `z.string().nullable()`, so `""` passes validation and reaches the `date` column. Postgres rejects it ("invalid input syntax for type date") and the whole Firm Profile save fails with a generic error until someone types a date. _Fix:_ preprocess `""` → null (as `legalStructure` already does) and validate as an ISO date.
- **[SET-03] [Low] Every firm that never set a theme still gets a sky-blue accent override** — `src/lib/branding/theme.ts:93-105`, `drizzle/migrations/0000_base_schema.sql:379` — `secondary_color` defaults to `#0ea5e9`. `resolveThemeColors` treats only the legacy *primary* default as "unbranded", so the accent resolves to `#0ea5e9`, which is not `DEFAULT_THEME.accent` (`#2563eb`). `buildTenantThemeCss` then injects a full `:root` override for every stock tenant, and the designed stock palette is never shown. `OrganisationProfileForm.tsx:49-50` also re-saves these legacy defaults on every save. _Fix:_ treat `#0ea5e9` as the legacy accent default (or null the column default), and stop the Organisation form from posting colours it does not own.
- **[SET-04] [Medium] A custom domain can be claimed without proving ownership, and soft-deleted tenants keep their domains reserved** — `src/app/api/tenant/domain/route.ts:129-147`, `src/lib/tenant/host.ts:55-59`, `drizzle/migrations/0162_tenant_custom_domains.sql:14-15` — PATCH stores any well-formed domain, verified or not, and the unique index then blocks everyone else. Firm A can set `crm.firmb.in`, and Firm B gets "already taken" for its own domain. `resolveTenantByHost` and `/api/tls/allowed` also resolve and serve unverified domains. The unique indexes do not exclude `deleted_at`, so a deleted firm's subdomain/domain stays locked until purge, even though delete frees the ARN. _Fix:_ hold the claim as pending (unique only once verified, e.g. TXT challenge), resolve only verified domains, and release subdomain/domain on soft delete.

**Improvements**
- `SUBDOMAIN_RE` accepts a 1-character label although the error message says 3–40; 2-character labels are rejected.
- `LocalOrAbsoluteUrl` (`/api/tenant`) accepts protocol-relative `//host/x.png` and any `new URL()` scheme for logo URLs. Restrict to `/api/upload-local/…` or `https:`.
- The theme colours can be edited in two places (Organisation and Appearance). Keep only the Appearance editor.
- The `/api/tenant` PATCH header comment says GST/PAN are "locked to AINA", but they are editable. Fix the comment or the behaviour.

### Settings: my profile, photo and email signature
_Checked:_ /dashboard/settings/profile (ProfileForm), PATCH/GET /api/me/profile, POST /api/compliance/upload-url (used for the photo and signature), /dashboard/settings/security.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-05] [Low] Marketing and viewer users cannot upload their own photo or email signature** — `src/app/dashboard/settings/profile/ProfileForm.tsx:117,252` → `src/app/api/compliance/upload-url/route.ts:31` — The self-service profile uploads presign through `/api/compliance/upload-url`, which requires `document.upload`. `marketing` and `viewer` do not have it, so they get "Could not start upload" on a page described as self-service for every role. _Fix:_ add a self-scoped avatar/signature presign route that does not need `document.upload`.

**Improvements**
- Validate the uploaded signature/avatar MIME type on the server, not only `file.type.startsWith("image/")` in the browser (see the known SVG upload issue).

### Settings: users and permissions (Employees)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/employees, /dashboard/employees/[id] (EmployeeEditForm), GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/employees/[id], /api/employees/[id]/restore, the audit-trail readers (/api/audit, employee page log).

**Bugs**
- **[SET-06] [Critical] Password hashes and 2FA secrets leak into the audit log and onto an unguarded page** ✔ _verified_ _(Same issue as ORG-01.)_ — `src/app/api/employees/[id]/route.ts:96-104,249-250,355-356,381-382`, `src/app/api/employees/[id]/restore/route.ts:47-48`, `src/app/dashboard/employees/[id]/page.tsx:17-25,80-90,292` — The employee PATCH, DELETE and restore handlers call `recordAudit({ before: existing, after: next })` with the full `users` row. That includes `password_hash`, `two_fa_secret` and `two_fa_backup_codes`. `capPayload` keeps the first ~3.8 KB, and `password_hash` sits near the top. `GET /api/employees/[id]` also returns the full row. The employee detail page has **no capability check** and passes `logs` (with `before`/`after`) to the client `EmployeeEditForm`. So any signed-in staff member who opens `/dashboard/employees/<owner id>` gets the owner's bcrypt hash, and the TOTP secret when it fits under the cap. `/api/audit` exposes the same to `branch_head` and `compliance`. _Fix:_ select an explicit safe column list everywhere (no secrets), redact audit payloads, gate the page on `settings.users`, and scrub the existing audit rows.
- **[SET-07] [Medium] Any staff member can see colleagues' HR data and targets** — `src/app/dashboard/employees/page.tsx:29-70`, `src/app/dashboard/employees/[id]/page.tsx:17-150` — Neither page checks `settings.users`. The API GET comment says "HR/PII … owner/admin only". Any RM can list every colleague's annual/SIP targets, AUM, EUIN, NISM expiry and last login. The detail page adds notes, leave, attendance, compliance documents, tasks and meetings. Settings only hides the link (`GATED roles: owner, branch_head`). _Fix:_ server-side role gate, with branch heads limited to their own branch.

**Improvements**
- When an owner saves an access change, the edited user is logged out. Say so in the form ("they will be signed out").

### Settings: targets, thresholds, dashboard defaults, desks, masters
_Checked:_ /settings/targets (TargetsManager, BranchTargetsManager, InsuranceTargetsManager), PUT /api/targets, GET/POST /api/branch-targets, PUT /api/business-targets, /settings/dashboard-thresholds + GET/PUT/DELETE /api/settings/dashboard-thresholds (and every `loadDashboardThresholds` reader), /settings/dashboards + PUT /api/dashboards/defaults, /settings/desks + /api/desks(/assign), /settings/masters + /api/masters/[type].

**Bugs**
- **[SET-08] [Medium] Branch heads and compliance can set branch targets, including their own branch's** — `src/app/api/branch-targets/route.ts:53-58` — The POST is gated on `dashboard.view.owner`, which `rbac.ts:319` grants to `compliance` and `branch_head`. The route says "Owners only", and `settings/targets/page.tsx:60-62` explains why ("a branch head setting their own branch's target is marking their own homework"). The page hides the editor, but the API does not enforce it. The route also writes no audit row, never checks that `branchId` is a real branch, and has no try/catch (raw 500s). _Fix:_ require `owner` (or `settings.dashboard`), validate the branch, and audit the write.
- **[SET-09] [Medium] Anyone can rewrite their own sales targets; branch heads can rewrite anyone's in the firm** — `src/app/api/targets/route.ts:20,38-41`, `src/app/dashboard/settings/targets/page.tsx:20-45` — Non-managers may PUT their own `annual_target` / `sip_monthly_target`, so an RM can lower the target their achievement and scorecards are measured against. That contradicts `/api/me/profile`, which says targets are owner-controlled. `isManager` includes `branch_head` with no branch check, so a branch head can change the owner's or another branch's RMs' targets, and the page lists every firm member to them. _Fix:_ only owner or `settings.users` edits targets; branch heads only for users in their branch.
- **[SET-10] [Low] A rejected threshold silently wipes the previously saved value** — `src/app/api/settings/dashboard-thresholds/route.ts:66-80` — PUT replaces the whole `settings` jsonb with `sanitizeThresholdOverrides(body)`. If one field is out of range it is dropped from `clean`, so an earlier valid override for that key is deleted, not kept. The toast says "the default still applies", but the firm also lost its old value. _Fix:_ merge `clean` over the stored overrides, and drop a key only when it is sent blank.
- **[SET-11] [Medium] Desk admin is not branch-scoped, and desks can span branches** — `src/app/api/desks/route.ts:33-46`, `src/app/api/desks/assign/route.ts:24-30`, `src/lib/org/desks.ts:124-126` — `settings.desks` is granted to `branch_head`, `back_office` and `ops`. They can create desks in any branch and move any user into any desk. `assignUserDesk` never checks that the desk's branch matches the user's branch. That breaks the Branch → Desk → RM MIS roll-up and lets someone join another branch's desk to see dashboards shared with that team. _Fix:_ check the desk branch against the user branch, and limit branch heads to their own branch.
- **[SET-12] [Low] Masters PATCH writes an unvalidated body; an unknown master type returns 500** — `src/app/api/masters/[type]/route.ts:73-100,47-66` — `label: null` violates NOT NULL (500), `meta` can be any JSON type, and `type` is cast `as any` into the enum, so a typo throws a Postgres error, not a 400. A hard-deleted custom value also orphans the key on the records that use it. _Fix:_ Zod-validate PATCH, check `type` against the enum, and soft-deactivate values that are in use.

**Improvements**
- `PUT /api/dashboards/defaults` checks only that the *admin* can see the dashboard, not the target role/user (the UI filters, the API does not).
- Thresholds of kind `days`/`hours` accept fractions (2.5 days). Round them or reject them.

### Settings: integrations (email sender, mailbox, Vartify, Zoho, WhatsApp, RTA mailback)
_Checked:_ /settings/integrations (VartifyConnectCard, ZohoIntegrationCard, RtaMailbackCard), /settings/email (EmailSenderForm, MailboxForm), /settings/whatsapp, /api/settings/email-sender, /api/settings/integrations/mailbox (+test, +oauth start/callback), /api/settings/integrations/vartify (+verify), /api/integrations/zoho/{connect,callback,credentials,status,disconnect}, /api/integrations/rta-mailback (+regenerate), /api/settings/whatsapp-status, lib/comms/sender.ts + resend adapter From handling.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-13] [High] Any staff member can repoint the firm's Zoho sync to their own Zoho account** — `src/app/api/integrations/zoho/connect/route.ts:17-34`, `src/app/api/integrations/zoho/callback/route.ts:17-60` — Only `credentials` checks for owner. `connect` and `callback` need just a session and use no OAuth `state`. An RM can open `/api/integrations/zoho/connect`, which builds the auth URL with the firm's client_id, approve with a personal Zoho login, and land on `callback`. That calls `saveZohoRefreshToken` for the firm. From then on, `runZohoSyncOnce` pushes every client, lead and record to the RM's Zoho. With no `state`, a third party can also do this through CSRF on an owner's browser. _Fix:_ require owner/`settings.users` on both routes and use a signed, session-bound `state` (as the mailbox OAuth routes do).
- **[SET-14] [Medium] Saving a sender name in Settings → Email can break every outgoing email** — `src/lib/comms/adapters/resend.ts:73-79`, `src/app/api/settings/email-sender/route.ts:59-87` — The form requires `fromName`, and `fromEmail` is optional. With no `fromEmail`, `baseFrom` is `EMAIL_FROM_TRANSACTIONAL`, which the built-in fallback and `.env.example` set as `"Aina CRM <connect@ainatech.in>"`. The adapter then sends `From: Firm <Aina CRM <connect@ainatech.in>>`, which is malformed, and Resend rejects every message for that firm. Separately, a `fromEmail` on a domain that is not verified is saved without any check and then fails every send on the platform key. _Fix:_ parse the address part of the env value before wrapping it with the firm name; when no own key is set, refuse (or warn on) a `fromEmail` whose domain is not verified.
- **[SET-15] [Medium] The mailbox SSRF guard covers only "Test"; saving skips it, and Test can send the saved password to a new host** — `src/app/api/settings/integrations/mailbox/route.ts:84-140`, `src/app/api/settings/integrations/mailbox/test/route.ts:47-66` — `isUnsafeAssetUrl` runs only in `/test`. POST `?scope=user` (any user) stores `127.0.0.1` / `169.254.169.254` / internal hosts on any port, and the IMAP sync worker and SMTP send then connect there. In `/test`, a blank password reuses the stored one *for whatever host the body names*. So "Test" with your own host and a blank password sends the firm mailbox password to that host. _Fix:_ run the host guard on save too, and reuse the stored password only when host and user match the saved ones.
- **[SET-16] [Low] The Vartify "webhook URL" is used as the API base with no host guard** — `src/app/api/settings/integrations/vartify/route.ts:49-70`, `src/lib/comms/adapters/vartify.ts:430-445` — The owner-supplied `webhookUrl` becomes the base for every API call, including `/verify`, which returns `httpStatus` and parsed fields. Owners can point the server at internal URLs (blind SSRF, with the bearer token attached). _Fix:_ allow-list Vartify hosts, or apply the asset-URL SSRF guard.
- **[SET-17] [Low] The WhatsApp and Integrations pages report connections that belong to the platform** — `src/app/dashboard/settings/whatsapp/page.tsx:26-36`, `src/app/dashboard/settings/integrations/page.tsx:12-16` — For a firm with no Vartify key, the WhatsApp page falls back to `VARTIFY_API_TOKEN`. It shows "Vartify WhatsApp is connected — workspace '<AINA's>' (<AINA email>)" and makes a live Vartify call on every page load. The Integrations page shows "BSE Star MF: Connected" when `BSE_STAR_MEMBER_CODE` is set, although BSE execution is not built. _Fix:_ show only per-tenant state and label platform fallbacks as such.

**Improvements**
- `GET /api/integrations/zoho/status` and `/api/settings/whatsapp-status` have no capability check. Gate them like the write routes.
- `/api/integrations/zoho/disconnect` uses `settings.masters` (compliance / back office) while connect is meant to be owner-only. Use one capability for the whole lifecycle.

### Signup and self-serve onboarding (plan selection)
_Checked:_ /signup (AuthScreen), POST /api/auth/signup, /onboarding/choose-plan (ChoosePlanClient), POST /api/onboarding/select-plan, license gate (`loadTenantLicense`, dashboard layout `needsOnboarding`).

**Bugs**
- **[SET-18] [High] Re-entering checkout creates a new Razorpay subscription each time and orphans the earlier ones** — `src/app/api/onboarding/select-plan/route.ts:93-118`, `src/app/api/billing/subscribe/route.ts:47-87`, `src/app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:76-79` — Every click creates a new Razorpay plan and subscription and overwrites `razorpay_subscription_id`; nothing cancels the previous one. This is easy to trigger: the handler logs the owner out, and a delayed webhook leaves them on "pending" choose-plan, so they click again. Billing → "A setup was started but not finished. Click below…" does the same. If an older checkout tab is completed, its webhook looks up `subscriptions` by the *old* id, finds nothing, and does not fall back to tenant because `rzpSubId` is set. The firm is charged and never activated, or holds two live mandates. _Fix:_ reuse the pending subscription (`fetchRazorpaySubscription`) or cancel it before creating a new one, add a per-tenant lock, and fall back to `notes.tenant_id` in the webhook.
- **[SET-19] [Medium] select-plan accepts packages that are not offered, and never charges the setup fee** — `src/app/api/onboarding/select-plan/route.ts:36-41,71-78` — The page lists only `is_active AND is_public` packages, but the API checks only `isActive`. The seeded `custom` package (`is_public=false`, ₹0, 100 users / 100k clients, all modules) activates for free for anyone who has its id. The free check uses only the yearly price, so a package priced monthly only (yearly 0) also activates free. `setupCostPaise` (₹5,000 on every seeded paid plan, shown on the card and on Billing) is never charged anywhere. _Fix:_ require `isPublic` (or an explicit invite), compute the price for the chosen cycle, and add the setup fee to the first charge.
- **[SET-20] [Medium] What checkout charges does not match the plan card** — `src/components/onboarding/ChoosePlanClient.tsx:137-153` vs `src/app/api/onboarding/select-plan/route.ts:80-110`, `src/lib/billing/razorpay.ts:296-304` — The card leads with "₹1,499/mo", "+ ₹X/extra user/mo" and "14-day trial window". The API creates a yearly subscription for `yearly + perUserYearly × maxUsers` (every seat in the plan, not extra users), with no `start_at`, so the card is charged at once and the advertised trial is skipped. _Fix:_ show the exact amount and cycle before checkout, charge per extra seat, and honour `trialDays` through `startAtUnix`.
- **[SET-21] [Medium] The "additive" recipe and template installs can fail the whole signup** — `src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts:161-187` — `installDefaultRecipes` / `installClientEmailTemplates` are wrapped in try/catch *inside* the `withSystem` transaction, with no savepoint. Any SQL error leaves the Postgres transaction aborted, so the next statement and the COMMIT fail and the user gets "signup failed". This is the opposite of the comment's "if it fails we still want the tenant create to succeed". _Fix:_ wrap each in a SAVEPOINT (nested `tx.transaction`) or run them after commit.
- **[SET-22] [Medium] ARNs are unverified and unnormalised, so the first claimant locks the real firm out** — `src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts:37-44,80-83`, `drizzle/migrations/0000_base_schema.sql:2184` — Any string is accepted as an ARN. Anyone can sign up with a competitor's ARN, and the real firm then gets "ARN already registered" with no recourse in-product. The unique index is case-sensitive and the input is not normalised, so `ARN-1234` and `arn-1234` or `1234` become separate tenants. Super-admin compares `lower(arn)`, so the two paths disagree. _Fix:_ normalise to `ARN-<digits>` and validate the format; mark self-declared ARNs unverified and do not let them block a claim.
- **[SET-23] [Low] Choose-plan shows the owner's personal name as the firm name** — `src/app/onboarding/choose-plan/page.tsx:56` — `firmName={session.name}` is the user's name, so the page reads "Pick the package that fits Ramesh Kumar" and the Razorpay checkout title shows the person, not the firm. _Fix:_ pass `tenants.display_name`.

**Improvements**
- After a successful free or paid selection the owner is logged out and sent to /login. Refresh the session and go to /dashboard instead.
- `select-plan` activation (free path) sets no `licenseEndAt`. Record an explicit period.

### Billing (tenant side)
_Checked:_ /dashboard/billing (BillingCycleChooser, AutoPaySetup, usage), /dashboard/billing/receipt/[eventId], GET /api/billing, POST /api/billing/cycle, POST /api/billing/subscribe, Razorpay webhook state transitions, lib/billing/razorpay.ts.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-24] [High] Razorpay mandates are never cancelled, and a later charge revives cancelled or deleted tenants** — `src/lib/billing/razorpay.ts:285` (no callers), `src/app/api/super-admin/tenants/[id]/route.ts:150-175`, `src/app/api/super-admin/tenants/[id]/status/route.ts:100-105`, `src/app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:98-111` — Deleting a tenant, the admin "Cancel" action and a package change only update our row. `cancelRazorpaySubscription` is never called, so the customer's auto-debit keeps running yearly. When `subscription.charged` arrives, the webhook sets `status='active'` and extends `licenseEndAt` on the cancelled or deleted firm's subscription. The firm keeps paying, and the admin's cancel is undone. _Fix:_ cancel at Razorpay on delete/cancel/plan change, and have the webhook ignore or refund charges for cancelled or deleted subscriptions.
- **[SET-25] [Medium] The "Monthly" billing cycle is cosmetic** — `src/app/api/billing/cycle/route.ts:26-37`, `src/components/billing/BillingCycleChooser.tsx:43-95`, `src/app/api/billing/subscribe/route.ts:56-63,82` — The chooser stores `billing_cycle='monthly'` and shows the monthly price. Auto-pay always creates a *yearly* plan, overwrites the cycle back to yearly and quotes the yearly amount. Switching cycle while a mandate is active changes nothing at Razorpay. The owner chooses monthly, sees monthly, and is charged yearly. _Fix:_ drive the plan period from the chosen cycle and re-plan the mandate on change, or remove the monthly option.
- **[SET-26] [Low] Billing data is visible to every user, and owner-only checks ignore `settings.billing`** — `src/app/api/billing/route.ts:21-23`, `src/app/dashboard/billing/page.tsx:27,136,150`, `src/app/dashboard/billing/receipt/[eventId]/page.tsx:25` — Any RM can read payment amounts and events (no `settings.billing` check). The UI decides `canManage` and receipt access with `roles.includes("owner")`, while the APIs use `settings.billing`. A user granted billing admin sees "Only the firm owner can…" and gets a 404 on receipts. _Fix:_ use `can(…, "settings.billing")` on both the page and the API.

**Improvements**
- The receipt route does not validate that `eventId` is a UUID (malformed ids give a 500 instead of a 404), and it issues a "Tax Invoice" for any event with an amount. Restrict invoices to `payment_received`.
- Billing has no page or banner for `past_due` (the gate leaves it unrestricted). Show the dunning state and a retry link.

### Super-admin console
_Checked:_ /super-admin (layout guard, tenants, tenant detail + TenantActions, packages, subscriptions, users, support, settings), /api/super-admin/tenants (POST), /tenants/[id] (PATCH/DELETE), /status, /access, /reset-password, /purge, /recover, /packages(/[id]), /users, ai-config/razorpay-config/email-config/retention guards.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-27] [Medium] Pressing Cancel on the reason prompt still suspends or cancels the tenant** — `src/components/super-admin/TenantActions.tsx:21-24` — It uses the banned `prompt()`. `prompt()` returns `null` on Cancel, `?? undefined` turns that into "no reason", and the request is still sent. An admin who backs out of "Reason for cancel?" cancels the firm's licence. A non-numeric extend value becomes `NaN` → null, which gives a generic "invalid body". _Fix:_ use `promptDialog()`, abort on null, and confirm destructive actions.
- **[SET-28] [Medium] Tenant edits (package, cycle, owner login email) leave no record and do not reprice billing** — `src/app/api/super-admin/tenants/[id]/route.ts:79-113` — Changing `packageKey` / `billingCycle` writes no `subscription_events` row, and changing the owner's login email (an account-takeover lever) writes no audit row. Every other admin action is logged. A package change also leaves the Razorpay plan amount unchanged. _Fix:_ write a subscription event and a platform audit row, and re-plan the mandate.
- **[SET-29] [Medium] AINA staff accounts cannot be deactivated or changed** — `src/app/api/super-admin/users/route.ts:26` (POST only), `src/app/super-admin/users/page.tsx` — There is no PATCH/DELETE, password reset, role change or suspend for platform admins or support. A departing AINA employee keeps super-admin access until someone edits the database. _Fix:_ add deactivate (with session revocation), role change and reset.
- **[SET-30] [Low] Admin-provisioned tenants are set up differently from self-serve ones** — `src/app/api/super-admin/tenants/route.ts:66-137` — Unlike signup, they get no default recipes and no client email templates (so Templates is empty and the risk/feedback emails cannot send). They also get two `created` subscription events, and `tenants.plan` is hard-coded to `'starter'` whatever the package. _Fix:_ share one provisioning function with signup.
- **[SET-31] [Low] Recovering a deleted tenant marks it paid** — `src/app/api/super-admin/tenants/[id]/recover/route.ts:52-66` — Recovery forces `status='active'` with a 30-day grace whatever the previous state. A deleted *trial* or *pending* (never-paid) firm comes back as a paid active subscription. _Fix:_ restore the pre-delete status (store it in the delete event).
- **[SET-32] [Low] The support ticket filter is built with string concatenation through `sql.raw`** — `src/app/super-admin/support/page.tsx:45-48,64` — Quotes are stripped, so injection is not obvious, but it breaks the "no raw SQL with user input" rule. An unknown enum value (`?status=foo`) throws a Postgres enum error and the page fails. _Fix:_ use parameterised `sql` fragments with values checked against the enum.
- **[SET-33] [Low] ARR on the Subscriptions page is understated** — `src/app/super-admin/subscriptions/page.tsx:24-26` — It counts only `billing_cycle='yearly'` subscriptions at the base `yearly_price_paise`. Per-user pricing, seat overrides and monthly-cycle subscriptions are ignored. _Fix:_ reuse `computeAnnualAmountPaise`, plus monthly × 12.

**Improvements**
- Platform release notes (What's new) have no authoring UI or API; add one under /super-admin.
- `extend` on a `pending` (unpaid onboarding) subscription changes dates but leaves the firm stuck on choose-plan. Say so, or offer "activate".

### Support tickets and NPS feedback
_Checked:_ /dashboard/support, GET/POST /api/support/tickets, /super-admin/support, /dashboard/support/feedback (SendNpsSurvey), POST /api/support/feedback/send, /api/support/feedback/pdf.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-34] [Medium] Support tickets can never be worked or resolved** — `src/app/api/support/tickets/route.ts` (GET/POST only), `src/app/super-admin/support/page.tsx` — No route updates `support_tickets` (status, assignee, reply, `resolved_at`). The AINA support page is read-only, so every ticket stays `open` forever, "avg resolution hrs" is always empty, and the MFD never sees a response. _Fix:_ add a PATCH and comment thread for platform staff, with a reply view for the tenant.
- **[SET-35] [Low] "My tickets" shows every ticket in the firm, and failed submits are silent** — `src/app/api/support/tickets/route.ts:28-36`, `src/app/dashboard/support/page.tsx:20-35` — The list is firm-wide (every user sees the owner's billing tickets) although the page calls it "My tickets". A non-OK POST shows nothing to the user. The page is also hand-rolled (no Button/Field/EmptyState primitives). _Fix:_ filter to `raised_by_user_id` unless admin, and show the error.
- **[SET-36] [Medium] Clients after the first 300 never receive the NPS survey** — `src/app/api/support/feedback/send/route.ts:80,107` — Each call sorts the audience `ORDER BY name` and takes `slice(0, 300)`. The UI says "N more are waiting; send again to reach them", but the next call picks the same first 300. Their `nps:<id>:<month>` idempotency keys dedupe, and the rest of the book is never surveyed. _Fix:_ exclude clients already sent this month (or paginate with a cursor) before slicing.

### Announcements and What's new
_Checked:_ /dashboard/announcements, GET/POST /api/announcements, PATCH/DELETE /api/announcements/[id], /api/announcements/[id]/read, /dashboard/whats-new, the layout's unread badge.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-37] [Medium] A branch head can edit, re-publish or delete any announcement, including the owner's firm-wide ones** — `src/app/api/announcements/[id]/route.ts:38-60,106-115` — `isManager` lets any branch head through, and `checkBranchTarget` runs only when the body changes `branchId`. A branch head can rewrite the title/body of a firm-wide notice (and re-notify with `publishNow`) or delete it. `GET ?manage=1` also lists every branch's drafts and read/ack counts to them. _Fix:_ for a non-owner, allow PATCH/DELETE only on announcements they created or that target their branch, and scope the manage list the same way.

**Improvements**
- `publishNow` on an already-published notice resets `published_at`, bumping it to the top. Ignore it when already live.
- What's new marks *every* published note read, including ones beyond the page's `LIMIT 200`.

### Trash
_Checked:_ /dashboard/trash (TrashTable, TaskTrashActions), GET /api/trash, the restore/purge endpoints it calls, the purge pass in workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-38] [Medium] Trash shows the wrong number of days before permanent deletion** — `src/app/api/trash/route.ts:30,62`, `src/app/dashboard/trash/page.tsx:37`, `src/workers/daily-ops-scanner.ts:1300-1311` — The bin computes "days left" from the hard-coded `DEFAULT_SOFT_DELETE_RETENTION_DAYS` (30). The nightly purge hard-deletes clients using `readSoftDeleteRetentionDays()`, which the platform admin sets in Settings → retention. If retention is 7 days, Trash says "23 days left" for a client who is erased tomorrow (cascading folios, transactions and SIPs). _Fix:_ read the configured retention in both the page and the API.

### Client onboarding pipelines (api/onboarding)
_Checked:_ GET/POST /api/onboarding, /api/onboarding/[id] (GET/PATCH/DELETE), /api/onboarding/[id]/stage.

**Bugs**
- **[SET-39] [Medium] The onboarding pipeline list shows every client's name and PAN to every user** — `src/app/api/onboarding/route.ts:33-49,72-77` — GET has no capability check and no book scope. Any signed-in user gets up to 500 pipelines with `investor_name`, `investor_pan` and RM. POST checks `onboarding.manage` but not that the investor is in the caller's book (`investorScopeSql` / `loadViewableInvestor`). _Fix:_ apply `investorScopeSql` to the list and `loadViewableInvestor` on create, and require a view capability.

---

## Dashboards & app shell

### Dashboard platform (named dashboards, sharing, defaults, filters, CSV export)
_Checked:_ `/dashboard`, `/dashboard/[dashboardId]` (+ `/edit`), `/dashboard/new`, `/dashboard/manage`, Settings → Dashboards; `GET/POST /api/dashboards`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/dashboards/[id]`, `/clone`, `/restore`, `/prefs`, `/shares`, `/export`, `PUT /api/dashboards/defaults`; `lib/dashboards/{platform,team,open-system,load-options}.ts`, `system/{access,filters,period,catalog,definitions}.ts`, `components/dashboards/system/{prepare,DashboardGrid,WidgetCard,WidgetMenu,DashboardFilterBar,ManageRowActions,EmbeddedWidgets,links}.ts(x)`.

**Bugs**
- **[DSH-01] [High] Brokerage CSV export skips the "firm-wide only" gate, so any user can download the firm's brokerage income** — `src/components/dashboards/system/widgets/practice.tsx:307-313` (with `src/app/api/dashboards/[id]/export/route.ts:36-44`). The card's `render` returns nothing unless `ctx.access.kind === "firm"` and no RM, branch or client filter is set (`practice.tsx:214`). Its `csv` calls `getBrokerage(tx, ctx.tenantId, …, 24)` with no check at all. Anyone may create a dashboard with any widget (`POST /api/dashboards`). So an RM can build a private dashboard holding `brokerage` and call `GET /api/dashboards/<id>/export?w=brokerage-1`, which returns 24 months of the ARN's firm-wide brokerage, even though the card itself renders blank for them. _Fix:_ apply the render gate in `csv` too, or have the export route refuse when `render` returns `body: null`.
- **[DSH-02] [Medium] The custom-widget access grant set by the platform admin does nothing on the new dashboards** — `src/config/dashboard-widgets.ts:22-66`, `src/components/dashboards/system/widgets/practice.tsx:104`. `subscriptions.allowed_dashboard_widgets` (edited in `TenantAccessControl`) still uses the deleted OwnerCockpit's keys (`kpi.net_business`, `section.rm_leaderboard`, `section.lead_funnel`, …). Only `sip_health` checks it. A plan that turns off, say, the RM leaderboard or net business still shows Team, KPI row, Lead funnel, etc. on the Owner Dashboard and on every custom dashboard. _Fix:_ re-key the catalog to `SYSTEM_WIDGETS` keys and filter `p.layout` in `prepareDashboard` (and the builder picker / export) by the grant.
- **[DSH-03] [Medium] Marketing gets an almost empty Owner Dashboard: lead cards show only leads they own** — `src/lib/dashboards/system/access.ts:40-50`. Owner Dashboard is marketing's role default, and CLAUDE.md says the "lead cards" live there for them. `dataAccessFor` gives firm access only through owner, `all_branches` or `investor.view.all`. Marketing holds `lead.view.all` but no investor capability, so it falls to `self`. `scopeFor` then sets `rmIds=[me]`, so Lead pipeline, Funnel, Sources and Leads-by-RM count only leads the marketing user owns (usually none), and client cards are empty. _Fix:_ derive lead (staff) access from `lead.view.all` separately from client access, or give marketing firm access for lead widgets.
- **[DSH-04] [Medium] The "Product type" filter does nothing** — `src/lib/dashboards/system/filters.ts:53`, `src/lib/dashboards/system/access.ts:76-104`. `product` (`?prod=`) is in `EXTRA_FILTERS`, so the Owner Dashboard and every custom dashboard (`platform.ts filterConfigOf` default) offer it. No widget lists `product` in `supports`, and `scopeFor` never maps it into `MetricScope`. Picking "MF" marks every card "Filter not applied" and changes no number. _Fix:_ implement it in `MetricScope` and the widgets that can honour it, or remove it from `EXTRA_FILTERS` and the default config.
- **[DSH-05] [Low] The Family filter lists every household in the firm, whatever the viewer may see** — `src/lib/dashboards/load-options.ts:74-79`. The `families` query has no access predicate, unlike RM and Branch. An RM (`self` access) on any dashboard they create sees the name and head name of every family in the firm, capped at 500, so big firms also get a truncated list. Tags (`:66-72`) are firm-wide too. _Fix:_ restrict to families containing a client inside `access` (as `/api/search` does) and drop the silent cap.
- **[DSH-06] [Low] "Filter this card" is offered on cards that ignore the period** — `src/components/dashboards/system/WidgetMenu.tsx:100-125`, `DashboardGrid.tsx:93-103`. Needs attention, Tasks due, Renewals due, Top clients, Expiries, Complaints, Business trend and Compliance gaps have no `"period"` in `supports`. Setting "This month" on them shows "Card filter: This month" in the subtitle while the numbers stay the same. _Fix:_ show the menu item only when `meta.supports.includes("period")`.
- **[DSH-07] [Low] A saved filter cannot be removed from the UI** — `src/components/dashboards/system/DashboardFilterBar.tsx:137-150, 347-356`. "Clear all" only rewrites the URL. The next plain visit (sidebar → Dashboard, no params) applies the saved filter again (`prepare.ts:153-155`). The prefs API accepts `savedFilters: null`, but nothing sends it. _Fix:_ add "Remove saved filter" that POSTs `savedFilters: null`.
- **[DSH-08] [Low] Bad ids in the defaults and shares APIs return 500 instead of 400** — `src/app/api/dashboards/defaults/route.ts:23,41-43` and `src/lib/dashboards/platform.ts:179-187`. `targetRef` for a user and `shareRef` are only `z.string()`. `eq(users.id, "abc")` and `${r.shareRef}::uuid` (a branch head sharing with a team) throw a Postgres cast error. A manager's `user`/`branch`/`team` shares are stored without checking the ids exist. _Fix:_ validate uuids with zod and check the referenced row exists.

**Improvements**
- `PUT /api/dashboards/defaults` accepts a dashboard the target user cannot see. `resolveDefaultDashboard` then skips it silently. Validate with `canView` for the target, as the Settings page already does for its options.
- Settings → Dashboards (`settings/dashboards/page.tsx:39`) and Manage (`manage/page.tsx:43`) run `Promise.all` over one `tx`, which `in-order.ts` says pg@9 rejects. Use `inOrder`.
- `touchOpened` runs on every centre `AutoRefresh` (every 2 min per open tab), so each refresh is a write to `dashboard_user_prefs`. Skip it on refresh renders.
- The Team table's row link to the same dashboard (`business.tsx:428`) keeps only period and RM, dropping segment, category and the other client filters in force.
- `branch_table` restates the overdue-task and SIP-book SQL inline (`business.tsx:490-498`). CLAUDE.md requires `lib/metrics` (`getOverdueTasksByAssignee` / `getSipBookByBranch`).
- `effectiveSizes` ignores `S` cards, so a lone S card next to a hidden partner leaves a hole on md/xl.

### Drill-down links vs list pages
_Checked:_ `components/dashboards/system/links.ts` against `/dashboard/investors` (`lib/investors/list-filters.ts`), `/dashboard/leads`, `/dashboard/tasks`, `/dashboard/sips`, `/dashboard/transactions`, `/dashboard/insurance`, `/dashboard/risk-profiles`, `/dashboard/service-requests`; widget hrefs in `widgets/{business,leads,sheet,work,practice}.tsx`; RM/Branch link builders `lib/dashboard/my/window.ts`, `components/dashboards/my/voice.ts`.

**Bugs**
- **[DSH-09] [Medium] For a team lead (`team` access), card counts include the team but the lists show only the lead's own rows** — `src/lib/dashboards/team.ts:46-51` vs the list pages. Cards count the whole reporting line (`scope.rmIds = memberIds`), but:
  - `/dashboard/tasks` limits anyone without `task.view.all` to "assigned to / created by me" (`tasks/page.tsx:112-114`), so `assignee=<team>` is cut back to the lead's own tasks.
  - `/dashboard/leads` applies `leadScopeSql` (`owner_id = me`).
  - `/dashboard/risk-profiles` forces `rmIds = [session.userId]` (`risk-profiles/page.tsx:83`).
  - `/dashboard/investors` does the same when `restrict_user_data_scope` is on.

  Example: "Tasks overdue 14" opens a list of 3. _Fix:_ teach the list scopes about the reporting line (one shared `teamScope` helper), or link with `rm=me` only when access is `team`.
- **[DSH-10] [Medium] The Owner Dashboard counts firm-wide for a branch head with `dashboard.view.all_branches`, but drill-down lists keep them in their branch** — `src/lib/leads/access.ts:32-39` vs `src/lib/dashboards/system/access.ts:42`. `dataAccessFor` gives such a head `firm`. `isBranchScoped` ignores `all_branches`, so the Leads list (unconditionally) and the Clients list (switch on) still show only their branch. `risk-profiles/page.tsx:83` shows only clients where they are the RM (`investor.view.assigned`). Every Owner-Dashboard drill-down undercounts for them. _Fix:_ have `isBranchScoped` (or the list scopes) honour `dashboard.view.all_branches` consistently, or keep the dashboard on branch access.
- **[DSH-11] [Medium] The Relationship Manager Dashboard's "Team member" view drills into the viewer's OWN book** — `src/lib/dashboard/my/window.ts:408,419`, `components/dashboards/my/voice.ts:38-39`, `my/MyPipeline.tsx:184,194,206`, `my/MyReviewDesk.tsx:45`, `lib/dashboard/my/desk.ts:267,431`, `lib/dashboard/my/actions.ts:358,402`. With `?member=<id>`, `MyBusinessDashboard` builds `scope = { kind: "rm", userId: member }`. Every list link is still `mine=1` (or a hard-coded `/dashboard/leads?mine=1`), which the list pages read as the signed-in user. Example: "Pooja's clients · 42" opens the team lead's own 17 clients, and "Reviews overdue" opens the lead's overdue reviews. _Fix:_ for a person book, emit `rm=<userId>` / `owner=<userId>` / `assignee=<userId>` instead of `mine=1` when `userId !== session.userId`.
- **[DSH-12] [Medium] The RM desk's client filters are not carried into its drill-down links** — `src/lib/dashboard/my/window.ts:407-415`. `clientListQs(scope)` ignores `scope.filter`. Filter the desk to Category A, city "Pune" or "new this FY" and the KPI tile says "My clients 12", but the link opens all 80 (`/dashboard/investors?mine=1`). The investors list already reads `category` and `ctype`. City, cohort and source need list params. _Fix:_ serialise `scope.filter` into the list query, adding the missing params to `list-filters.ts`.
- **[DSH-13] [Low] Lead sources splits Client Referral into two rows, and each row's link opens a different count** — `src/components/dashboards/system/widgets/leads.tsx:157-167, 191-200`. `sourceRows` groups by the raw `l.source`, so `existing_client` and `referral` are separate rows, labelled `humanise()` ("Existing client" / "Referral") instead of `leadSourceLabel` ("Client Referral"). The `referral` row links `source=referral`, which `leadWhere` widens through `expandLeadSources` to include `existing_client`. The list therefore shows both rows' leads under one row's count. _Fix:_ group by `SOURCE_ALIASES`-normalised source and label with `leadSourceLabel`.
- **[DSH-14] [Low] The Clients list's `src=` filter does not expand lead-source aliases, but the card does** — `src/lib/investors/list-filters.ts:183-188` vs `lib/metrics/scope.ts:94-98`. A card filtered to Lead source = Client Referral counts clients converted from `existing_client` leads. Its `clientsHref` drill-down (`src=referral`) matches `l.source IN ('referral')` only, so the list is shorter than the count. _Fix:_ use `expandLeadSources(src)` in the list builder.
- **[DSH-15] [Low] RM lead funnel tiles link to lists they don't match** — `src/components/dashboards/my/MyPipeline.tsx:194-206`. "Leads owned" counts converted and onboarded leads, but `/dashboard/leads?mine=1` hides them (no `include_won=1`). "Stale (14d+)" links to the same unfiltered list instead of the stale leads. _Fix:_ add `include_won=1` and a stale filter (`stuck=1`, or a param that honours `staleAfterDays`).

**Improvements**
- `links.ts` comments promise "count = row count". A small test that renders each widget's hrefs and runs the list builder on the same fixture would catch DSH-09…15-style drift.
- `MyFunnel`, `MyReviewDesk`, `MyWork` and `actions.ts` hard-code list URLs. Route them all through `voice.clients` / `voice.leads` so the book decides.

### Role dashboards (Branch Head, RM, Operations centres)
_Checked:_ `components/dashboards/system/centres.tsx`, `BranchCommandCentre.tsx`, `MyBusinessDashboard.tsx`, `OperationsControlTower.tsx`, `lib/dashboard/my/{window,desk,actions}.ts`, `lib/dashboard/ops/{access,params}.ts`, `/api/ops/items/[id]`, `/api/ops/export`, `lib/dashboard/period.ts`, `lib/dashboard/run-limit.ts`, `src/app/dashboard/error.tsx`.

**Bugs**
