- **[DSH-16] [Medium] One failing band takes down a whole centre dashboard** — `src/components/dashboards/MyBusinessDashboard.tsx:402-640` (and the band functions in `BranchCommandCentre.tsx` and `OperationsControlTower.tsx`). Widget dashboards catch per card (`DashboardGrid.tsx:39-45`). The centres' ~20 async bands have no try/catch or error boundary: `dayP.catch(() => {})` only stops an unhandled-rejection warning, and the awaiting band still throws. Any loader error, including the pool-connect timeouts `run-limit.ts` describes, bubbles to `app/dashboard/error.tsx` and replaces the whole RM, Branch or Ops dashboard. `AutoRefresh` every 2 minutes turns a transient failure into a blank dashboard. _Fix:_ wrap each band in a small error boundary, or a `safe(run)` helper that renders a "This section could not be loaded" Cell.
- **[DSH-17] [Low] Today's meetings on the RM desk ignore meetings the user attends; Upcoming 7 days includes them** — `src/lib/dashboard/my/desk.ts:105-116, 531-537, 927-932`. My Day's meeting count, the worklist's meetings and meeting prep filter by `staffMatch("rm_id")` only. "Upcoming 7 days" (`desk.ts:808`) adds `attendeeIsAnyUser(w.staff)`. CLAUDE.md: "never read only rm_id … for 'my meetings'". A colleague's client meeting the RM attends today shows under "next 7 days" but not in today's count or the worklist. _Fix:_ OR `attendeeIsAnyUser(w.staff)` into those three queries.
- **[DSH-18] [Low] The Ops Compliance tab maps task scope onto client access, so sharing the dashboard can widen what is counted** — `src/components/dashboards/OperationsControlTower.tsx:471, 497-500`. `dataAccessOf(access.scope)` turns `task.view.all` into `{ kind: "firm" }` for client facts. Marketing holds `task.view.all` but no investor capability. If an admin shares the Ops Dashboard with a marketing user (`mayUseRoleDashboard`), they see firm-wide compliance gaps, complaints and expiries, against "sharing never widens what it counts". _Fix:_ use `resolveDataAccess(tx, session)` for the embedded cards.

**Improvements**
- `BranchHead` / `RmDesk` cast `sp.period` with `as Period` (`centres.tsx:211, 239`). The bar's own keys (`period=fy` from a hand-shared Owner link) silently fall back to month-to-date while the header shows "This FY" elsewhere. Map with the same `LEGACY_PERIOD` table the Ops centre uses.
- For the Ops centre, a custom range with `to < from` becomes "to today" (`centres.tsx:296-300`), while `resolvePeriod` and `tidyRange` swap the dates. Make one rule.
- The Branch Head centre links Operations only by capability (`BranchCommandCentre.tsx:341`, `/dashboard/operations`). A head the Ops dashboard was shared with gets no link. Use `systemDashboardId(…,"operations")`.

### App shell (sidebar, badges, bells, search, removed pages, mobile home APIs)
_Checked:_ `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx` (NAV, PALETTE_ONLY, `canSee`, badges, `branchList`), `config/nav-access.ts`, `components/layout/{GlobalSearch,CommandPalette,ProfileMenu,BirthdayBell,NotificationBell}.tsx`, `/api/search`, `/api/birthdays`, `/api/notifications`, `/api/dashboard/{home,my-day,my-performance}`, `/api/command-center`, redirects `pipeline-board`, `operations`, `command-center`, `my-day`, `boards`, `complaints/disclosure`, `redesign-preview`.

**Bugs**
- **[DSH-19] [Medium] The header bells and the mobile and home APIs show a branch-locked branch head the whole firm's clients** — `src/app/api/birthdays/route.ts:43`, `src/app/api/notifications/route.ts:27`, `src/app/api/command-center/route.ts:32-38`, `src/app/api/dashboard/my-performance/route.ts:39`, `src/app/api/dashboard/home/route.ts:24-29`, `src/app/api/dashboard/my-day/route.ts:30-36`. Each treats `dashboard.view.owner` (or a `FIRM_WIDE` list containing `branch_head`) as firm-wide. Branch heads hold that capability by default (`rbac.ts:319`), although the dashboards lock them to their branch. On every page, the cake bell lists every branch's client birthdays with phone/WhatsApp numbers, and the notification panel lists their names. On a web session, `/api/command-center?user=<any id>` returns any colleague's queue, `/api/dashboard/my-day` returns firm-wide meetings, follow-ups and tasks with phones, and `my-performance` the firm leaderboard. (`rbac.ts:333-336` defers narrowing `dashboard.view.owner`; these shell surfaces are where it leaks.) _Fix:_ scope these by `investorScopeSql` / `isBranchScoped` (branch → `i.branch_id = session.branchId`) instead of `dashboard.view.owner`.
- **[DSH-20] [Medium] `/dashboard/automations` bypasses the server-side module and nav-grant gate** — `src/config/nav-access.ts:104-106`, `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx:519-524`. Grant keys are still `/dashboard/workflows/{rules,actions,runs}`, and `gatedNavKeyForPath("/dashboard/automations")` matches none, so `navBlocked` is false. A firm without `automation_workflows`, or a team member not granted it, opens Automations, Actions and Run history by typing the new URL. The old URLs are blocked before their redirect even runs. The sidebar hides the item (it uses `accessKey`), so this is a URL-only bypass. _Fix:_ map `/dashboard/automations*` to the existing keys in `gatedNavKeyForPath`, as the sidebar's `accessKey` does.
- **[DSH-21] [Medium] The insurance team has no Insurance item in the sidebar** — `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx:176-177`. The Insurance item's `roles` are `owner, rm, branch_head, back_office, ops, compliance`, without `insurance_team`. Their default is the Insurance Team Dashboard, which drills into `/dashboard/insurance`, but they cannot navigate to the policy register (renewals, claims tabs) from the menu or Ctrl+K. The page itself allows them (`insurance.view`). _Fix:_ add `insurance_team` to the Insurance item (and review Clients and Meetings for the same role).
- **[DSH-22] [Low] Mobile/home APIs say each block "fails soft", but one failure zeroes everything after it** — `src/app/api/dashboard/home/route.ts:35-55`, `src/app/api/dashboard/my-day/route.ts:47-150`. Every statement runs on one transaction via `Promise.all` and catches its own error. In Postgres, a failed statement aborts the transaction, so every later query also errors and returns 0 or `[]`. The response looks like a quiet day, not an error. It also overlaps `client.query` calls on one client (deprecated; rejected by pg@9 per `in-order.ts`). _Fix:_ use a savepoint per block (`tx.transaction`, as the layout's `countOf` and `/api/search` do) and `inOrder`.
- **[DSH-23] [Low] Profile → "Switch branch" is offered to branch-locked heads and silently does nothing** — `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx:436-443`, `src/components/layout/ProfileMenu.tsx:229`. The branch list is gated on `dashboard.view.owner` (branch heads have it) and includes placeholder "Unassigned" branches. It links `/dashboard?branch=<id>`, which opens the viewer's default. For a locked head that is the Branch Head centre, which ignores `?branch=`, and any default that is a centre ignores it too. _Fix:_ gate on `dashboard.view.all_branches` and `realBranches()`, and link to the Owner/Branch Head dashboard id directly.
- **[DSH-24] [Low] `/dashboard/redesign-preview` shows every user of every firm a fake dashboard** — `src/app/dashboard/redesign-preview/page.tsx`, `src/components/dashboards/modern/*`. It renders hard-coded figures for a fake firm ("Aina Wealth Advisory", "ARN-184920", fabricated RM leaderboard), and its `requireSession()` is wrapped in a try/catch that would swallow the auth redirect. It is reachable by URL in production and listed nowhere. _Fix:_ delete the route and the `modern/` components (or gate them to platform admins in dev).
- **[DSH-25] [Low] The Branches table leaves out clients in the placeholder branch, so its columns don't add up** — `src/components/dashboards/system/widgets/business.tsx:504, 531-537`. Rows come from `ctx.branchNames` (`realBranches`, which drops "Unassigned"/"None"). The extra row covers only `branch_id IS NULL`. Clients whose `branch_id` is the placeholder branch are counted in `getBookByBranch` but shown in no row. _Fix:_ fold placeholder-branch ids into the "No branch" row.
- **[DSH-26] [Low] The Lead pipeline's Lost bar is drawn in the brand colour** — `src/components/dashboards/system/widgets/leads.tsx:71`. The fill test is `col.key === "lost"`, but the Lost column's key is `not_qualified` (`lib/leads/stages.ts:65`), as the comment at `:32` notes. The leak is painted like a progress stage. _Fix:_ compare with `"not_qualified"`.

**Improvements**
- Dead code that CLAUDE.md says is "no longer rendered" has zero importers: `AttentionCentre`, `RmScoreboard`, `RmTargetMatrix`, `BranchSipPerformance`, `BranchActivity`, `BranchQuickActions` (`components/dashboards/branch/`); the 10 insurance and claims panels; and, despite CLAUDE.md saying they "stay in the tree for the Branch Head Dashboard", `Worklist`, `MyOpportunities`, `SourceAnalysis`, `ReviewModePanel` and `SipDebitCheck`. Delete them, along with their loaders where nothing else uses them, and correct CLAUDE.md.
- `/api/command-center` and `buildCommandQueue` survive the removed My Day / Command Center page only for mobile, and `config/dashboard-widgets.ts` and `lib/dashboard/period.ts` still describe the deleted `OwnerCockpit` / `RmDashboard`. Mark them mobile-only or retire them.
- `/api/birthdays` excludes `closed` but not `exited` (use `aliveClient`), and its header says "Past (last 7 days)" while the query takes 30.
- `/api/search` meetings use `m.rm_id = me` only, so attendee meetings are not found (use `attendeeIs`). `%` and `_` typed into search are not escaped in the ILIKE patterns.
- Nav items `/dashboard/support/feedback` (module `service_requests`), `/dashboard/expected-inflows` and `/dashboard/induction` are not in `nav-access` keys, so their `module` field is never enforced (`canSee` does not read `module`).

---

## Client portal, public pages, mobile & workers

### Investor portal & client share links
_Checked:_ /portal/[token] page, POST /api/portal/[token]/service-requests, lib/portal/access.ts, GET/POST/DELETE /api/investors/[id]/portal-access + PortalAccessButton, /portfolio/[token] + /portfolio/[token]/pdf, /onboarding/[token], share-token KIND_TO_PATH vs middleware PUBLIC_PATH_PREFIXES, investor delete/purge interaction.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-01] [High] Live-portfolio, statement-PDF and onboarding-tracker links bounce clients to /login** ✔ _verified_ — `src/middleware.ts:87-127` vs `src/lib/share-tokens/index.ts:51-59` — `KIND_TO_PATH` mints `portfolio_view` links at `/portfolio/<token>` (and the page links `/portfolio/<token>/pdf`) and `onboarding_progress` links at `/onboarding/<token>`, but neither `/portfolio/` nor `/onboarding/` is in `PUBLIC_PATH_PREFIXES` (no other prefix matches them). A client opening the "Share live portfolio" WhatsApp link from `/api/investors/[id]/portfolio-link` or `/api/client-forms/send`, or the onboarding tracker from `OnboardingActions`, gets a 307 to the firm's login page. Both features are dead for every client. This is the same failure CLAUDE.md describes for `/f/`. _Fix:_ add `"/portfolio/"` and `"/onboarding/"` (token routes only, not `/onboarding/choose-plan`) to `PUBLIC_PATH_PREFIXES`, and add a test that every `KIND_TO_PATH` value is public.
- **[PUB-02] [Medium] Hard-deleting a client who was ever given a portal link fails** — `drizzle/migrations/0195_product_coherence.sql:4`, `src/app/api/investors/[id]/route.ts:600-628` — `investor_portal_access.investor_id REFERENCES investors(id)` has no `ON DELETE CASCADE`. Migration 0131's cascade walk ran once, before this table existed, and neither the DELETE route nor `lib/investors/purge.ts` removes the rows (they do remove `share_tokens`). Permanent delete or purge then fails with an FK violation (500) for any client who has a portal-access row, including revoked ones. `business_impacts` (same migration) has the same problem. _Fix:_ add a migration that makes these FKs `ON DELETE CASCADE` (or re-run the 0131 walk), and delete portal rows in delete/purge.
- **[PUB-03] [Medium] Portal and portfolio links keep working after the client is trashed or merged** — `src/lib/portal/access.ts:47-66`, `src/app/portal/[token]/page.tsx:18-26`, `src/app/portfolio/[token]/page.tsx:46-48` — the access lookup and the investor read check neither `deleted_at` nor `merged_into_id`. Soft-delete keeps `share_tokens`, and merge only sets `status='inactive', merged_into_id` (merge.ts:277). So a trashed client's 90-day portal link still serves their data. A merged-away record's link shows ₹0 and "Portfolio details will appear after the next registrar import", because its folios moved to the survivor. _Fix:_ in `resolvePortalAccess` / the page loaders, require `deleted_at IS NULL AND merged_into_id IS NULL`, or redirect to the survivor; revoke the links on trash and merge.
- **[PUB-04] [Medium] Portal service requests bypass `openServiceRequest`** — `src/app/api/portal/[token]/service-requests/route.ts:50-90` — the route copies the ticket logic instead of calling the one shared opener, which CLAUDE.md requires. Every portal ticket gets a flat 7-day resolution and 24-hour response SLA (the canonical values are 2 days for `statement_request`, 14 for `nominee_update`, 3 for `capital_gain_report`). The client also gets no acknowledgement with an ETA, unlike the CRM and complaint forms. _Fix:_ call `openServiceRequest(tx, { …, channel: "form", createdBy: { by: "client_portal", … }, eventMeta: { source: "investor_portal", portalAccessId } })`.
- **[PUB-05] [Medium] "Create & copy" can revoke the old link and lose the new one** — `src/components/investors/PortalAccessButton.tsx:31-45` — the POST revokes every live link and issues a new token, then calls `navigator.clipboard.writeText` after an `await fetch`. Safari/iOS (and the mobile WebView) reject clipboard writes that are not inside a user gesture, so the catch shows "Portal link failed". The new URL is never shown and can't be recovered (only its hash is stored). The RM has just switched off the client's old link, and every retry does the same again. _Fix:_ show the returned URL in the dialog with a copy button (and a WhatsApp share), and treat a clipboard failure as non-fatal.
- **[PUB-06] [Low] Portal and portfolio show abandoned and achieved goals** — `src/app/portal/[token]/page.tsx:27-29`, `src/app/portfolio/[token]/page.tsx:51-55` — neither query filters `goals.status`. A client sees goals the RM marked `abandoned`, and the portal's "Goals" tile counts them. _Fix:_ filter to the live statuses (`not_started/on_track/behind_track/active/on_hold`), with achieved goals in their own section if wanted.
- **[PUB-07] [Low] A request submitted on the portal does not appear in "Recent requests"** — `src/components/portal/PortalServiceRequestForm.tsx:36-38` — on success the form only sets local state. The server-rendered list beside it never refreshes (no `router.refresh()`), so the client sees "No service requests yet" next to "Request received". _Fix:_ call `router.refresh()` after a 201.

**Improvements**
- Portal values come from the import-time `folios.schemes[].value` snapshot, while `/portfolio/<token>` uses the live valuation engine, so the same client can be sent two different "current value" figures. Use one source, `valueInvestorCached`.
- The portal header selects `tenants.logoUrl` but never renders it; brand the portal like the other public pages.
- `accessUrl` is built from `req.nextUrl.origin`; build it from `APP_URL` like `createShareToken` does.
- Revoking portal access is one click with no `confirmDialog()`.
- Holdings key `${folio.id}:${schemeCode}` collides when a folio has two plans with a null or identical code (React key warning).

### Public client forms (/r, /p, /d, /f, /fd, /lf, /complaint, /induction, /lc, /preview, knowledge-base)
_Checked:_ every public page listed plus PublicProposalApproval, PublicDocsForm submit path, lead-form GET, discovery submit, induction letter route, /lc click redirect, KB article page, /preview/dashboard.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-08] [High] Clients answer the firm's OLDEST risk questionnaire, scored against the NEWEST** _(Same issue as PLN-16.)_ — `src/app/r/[token]/page.tsx:52-56` vs `src/app/api/public/risk-profile/submit/route.ts:199-205` — the page loads templates with `.orderBy(versionNumber)` (ascending, no limit, no `is_active` filter) and renders the first row, which is v1. Every publish inserts a new version (`api/risk-profile-template/route.ts:70-84`). Submit loads `desc(versionNumber)` (also ignoring `is_active`) and scores the answers against that. Once a firm edits its questionnaire, clients see the v1 questions. Submit then either fails with "Missing answers for: …" (keys changed) or silently scores v1 answers with v2 weights and bands. That records a wrong risk category, which drives the MF proposal suitability gate. The OTP flag on the same page already reads the latest version, so the page disagrees with itself. _Fix:_ use `loadActiveTemplate(tx, tenantId)` (`is_active = true`, desc) on both the page and submit, and store the template version on the response.
- **[PUB-09] [Medium] /lf lead form shows "Form not found" to everyone under modest traffic** — `src/app/lf/[slug]/page.tsx:15-27`, `src/app/api/public/lead-forms/[slug]/route.ts:36` — the page fetches its own public API over `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`. Every render therefore reaches the GET from the server's egress IP, and `enforce(…, 60/min)` keys on the client IP. All visitors share one bucket per slug, so past 60 views a minute the 429 maps to `"not-found"`. A firm embedding the form on its website loses leads exactly when traffic peaks. The slug is also interpolated unencoded, so `/lf/..%2F..%2Fhealth` fetches another API and crashes the client component. _Fix:_ read the form directly through a shared loader (no self-HTTP), and rate-limit only the POST.
- **[PUB-10] [Low] Returning to a submitted Financial Health Check shows an error instead of the thank-you** — `src/app/fd/[token]/page.tsx:20-24,38` — `resolveShareToken(token)` is called without `allowUsed`. Submit claims and finalises the token (`api/public/discovery/[token]/route.ts:79,103`), so a client re-opening the link gets "This link has already been used", and the `already` branch below is unreachable. Raw messages such as "Token not found" and "Invalid token" are also shown verbatim on /r, /f, /d, /fd and /portfolio. _Fix:_ pass `{ allowUsed: true }` here and map resolver errors to client-friendly wording.
- **[PUB-11] [Low] Stray public demo page** — `src/app/preview/dashboard/page.tsx:36-60`, `src/middleware.ts:68` — `/preview` is in `PUBLIC_PATHS` and serves an unlinked, indexable mock dashboard with hard-coded names ("Pritesh", "Aina Wealth Advisory"). The sidebar opens 256px wide on a 375px phone, and the collapsed width class `w-18` is not in the Tailwind scale, so collapse does nothing. _Fix:_ remove the route (or gate it behind `NODE_ENV !== "production"`) and drop it from `PUBLIC_PATHS`.

**Improvements**
- No root `src/app/error.tsx`: a DB blip on /portal, /r or /portfolio falls through to `global-error`, which replaces the whole document. The branded `not-found` sends clients to "Go to dashboard" (the firm login). Add a client-safe root error boundary and a neutral 404 for token routes.
- `global-error.tsx` never reports the error (no `captureError` in a `useEffect`), so root-layout crashes are invisible.
- `inductionEnrolments.letterToken` is stored and compared in plaintext (every other token is SHA-256 hashed), and a letter with null `letterIssuedAt` never expires.

### Marketing site, SEO, robots/sitemap
_Checked:_ src/app/page.tsx (nav, footer, anchors), layout.tsx metadata, privacy/terms/refund metadata, sitemap.ts, robots.ts.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-12] [Medium] Terms, Privacy and Refund are linked from nowhere a visitor or new customer sees** — `src/app/page.tsx:629-631`, `src/components/auth/AuthScreen.tsx:338-343` — the home footer has no legal links ("Data security (DPDP)" points to `#faq`), and neither login nor signup links them. Signup also never asks the user to accept the Terms; its only consent line is the mutual fund risk disclaimer. The middleware comment says these pages exist because Razorpay onboarding and DPDP require them to be publicly linked, and only the KB and legal shells link them. _Fix:_ add Terms · Privacy · Refund to the home footer and the auth screens, plus "By creating a workspace you agree to the Terms and Privacy Policy" on signup.
- **[PUB-13] [Low] Every page inherits canonical "/" and `index: true`** — `src/app/layout.tsx:44,60-64`, `src/app/robots.ts:13` — `alternates.canonical: "/"` sits in the ROOT metadata. /privacy, /terms, /refund, /signup and /login don't override it, so search engines fold them into the home page. Token pages (/portal, /portfolio, /p, /r, /onboarding) inherit `robots: index`. robots.txt omits `/portal/`, `/portfolio/`, `/onboarding/`, `/f/`, `/fd/`, `/lc/`, `/induction/`, `/preview`, `/reset-password` and `/accept-invite`. A capability link that gets posted publicly (a client's live portfolio, with name and holdings) is indexable. Only /complaint sets `noindex`. _Fix:_ move the canonical into `page.tsx` metadata, set `robots: { index: false }` on every token route layout, and extend the disallow list.
- **[PUB-14] [Low] No way to sign in from the home header on a phone** — `src/app/page.tsx:145-160` — the nav and the "Log in" button are both `hidden md:flex` with no mobile menu. Below 768px the only route to login is the footer. _Fix:_ keep "Log in" visible at all widths or add a hamburger menu.

**Improvements**
- The sitemap omits /privacy, /terms and /refund.
- `robots.ts` `host` takes a hostname, not a full URL; most engines ignore it.

### Auth screens (login, signup, forgot/reset, accept-invite)
_Checked:_ AuthScreen (login, 2FA, signup), ForgotPasswordForm, ResetPasswordForm + /api/auth/reset, accept-invite page + /api/employees/accept-invite, login/forgot status handling.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-15] [Low] Reset-password copy and error mapping are wrong** — `src/app/reset-password/page.tsx:34`, `src/components/auth/ResetPasswordForm.tsx:56-60` — the panel promises "Other sessions stay as they are", but `/api/auth/reset` stamps `sessions_invalid_after` and signs out every device. The form maps only 400/410 to the "expired → send a new link" screen. A reused link (409 "token already used") shows the raw string with no way forward. An invited or offboarded user can also complete a reset ("Signs you in immediately") and is then bounced to /login with "account inactive", because reset never checks `users.status`. _Fix:_ fix the bullet text, treat 409 as expired, and refuse (generically) to reset non-active users.
- **[PUB-16] [Low] Accept-invite swallows server failures** — `src/app/accept-invite/page.tsx:27-33` — `await res.json()` has no catch, so a non-JSON 429/502 throws inside a `try { } finally { }` with no catch: nothing is shown and the button just re-enables. 409/410 ("invite already used", "invite expired") surface as raw lowercase API strings with no "go to sign in" or "ask for a new invite" path. The page is also the only unbranded auth screen and has no confirm-password field. _Fix:_ add a catch, `.json().catch(() => ({}))`, friendly states for used/expired, and reuse `AuthPanelLayout` + `PasswordInput`.
- **[PUB-17] [Low] Switching login ⇄ signup mid-request re-enables submit, and the password carries over** — `src/components/auth/AuthScreen.tsx:94-104` — `switchMode` sets `busy=false` while a login or signup request is in flight, so the other form can be submitted concurrently. The login and signup forms share one `password` state, so a typed login password pre-fills "create password". _Fix:_ ignore mode switches while busy and clear `password` on switch.

**Improvements**
- Signup has no client-side `minLength={8}` or ARN format hint. The placeholder says "8+ characters" but a short password round-trips to a server 400.
- Login/forgot/reset branding reads `x-forwarded-host` directly (spoofable, cosmetic only); reuse a single trusted-host helper.

### Mobile app API (api/mobile/*, lib/mobile, api/me*, api/devices, push)
_Checked:_ GET/POST /api/mobile/business, GET /api/mobile/call-contacts, GET/POST /api/mobile/call-summary, GET/POST /api/mobile/targets, lib/mobile/{access,record-access,targets}, GET /api/me, /api/me/profile, /api/me/devices(+[id]), /api/me/session/register, /api/me/sign-out-everywhere, POST/DELETE /api/devices, lib/push/expo.ts, lib/notifications/{mobile,create}.ts, workflow send_push, native-bridge.ts, plus the mobile consumers in mobile/src and mobile/app.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-18] [Medium] Push registrations outlive the session, so a signed-out or lost phone keeps getting the user's alerts** — `src/app/api/me/sign-out-everywhere/route.ts:24-38`, `src/app/api/me/devices/[id]/route.ts:20-33`, `src/app/api/devices/route.ts:42-62` — "Sign out everywhere" and "log out this device" revoke `user_sessions` but never touch `devices`. The app's `unregisterDevice` needs a still-valid Bearer token (mobile/src/api.ts:92), so it fails exactly when the session was revoked or expired. `deliverMobileAlerts` and `send_push` only check `u.status='active'` and `disabled_at`. A user who signs out a stolen phone keeps pushing task and client alerts to it. Because `devices` is unique per `(tenant_id, token)`, a shared handset that signs into another firm keeps the first firm's row live too. _Fix:_ disable or delete `devices` rows when sessions are revoked (per-user, or per-sid by storing the sid on the device row). On register, delete the same token in other tenants via a system-context cleanup.
- **[PUB-19] [Medium] Lock-screen pushes carry client names and amounts** — `src/lib/notifications/create.ts:14,41`, `src/app/api/tasks/route.ts:383-389`, `src/lib/workflow/actions/send_push.ts:6-8` — `task.assigned`, `task.reminder` and `task.overdue` are pushed with title `New task: ${body.title}`. Task titles routinely hold client names and figures ("Call Ramesh Shah – SIP ₹25,000 rejected"), and the send_push docs even suggest "SIP rejected for Sharma family". iOS and Android show these on the lock screen by default. Only `meeting.reminder` was deliberately made generic. _Fix:_ send a generic title/body ("You have a new task — open Aina CRM") in the push, keep the detail in the in-app notification, or mark the payload for content-hidden display.
- **[PUB-20] [Medium] Mobile access is a separate role-only model that disagrees with the web app** — `src/lib/mobile/access.ts:3-21`, `src/app/api/mobile/call-contacts/route.ts:11-17` — `mobileFirmAccess` and the branch/self scopes look only at role names. They ignore per-user `permissions` (an RM granted `investor.view.all` sees only own clients on mobile), the `reporting_manager_id` team rule used by every dashboard (a team lead sees only themselves), and the ops branch lock (`opsAccess`): a branch-scoped `ops` user gets every client's phone number firm-wide from call-contacts. In the other direction, a branch head sees the whole branch's activities on mobile but only their own on web (`api/activities/route.ts:79` vs `:72`). _Fix:_ derive mobile scope from the same helpers as web (`investorScopeSql` / `leadScopeSql` / `resolveDataAccess`).
- **[PUB-21] [Medium] Call-contacts returns merged-away duplicates and the whole book unpaginated** — `src/app/api/mobile/call-contacts/route.ts:18-26` — investors are filtered only on `deleted_at IS NULL`. A merged-away record (status `inactive`, `merged_into_id` set, not deleted) is returned alongside its survivor with the same phone. The handset matcher (`simCallSync.ts`) can then attach the call to the inactive duplicate, where it vanishes from the live client's timeline. The endpoint also returns every client and lead phone in one response, tens of thousands of rows for an owner, on every sync. _Fix:_ add `merged_into_id IS NULL AND status NOT IN ('closed','exited')`, and page or delta-sync by `updated_at`.
- **[PUB-22] [Low] Business-history filters miss most product types; the app shows only the first 50** — `src/app/api/mobile/business/route.ts:38`, `mobile/app/(app)/m/business-entries/index.tsx:15` — "life" omits `money_back`. "health" is an exact match, so `group_health`, `critical_illness` and `personal_accident` never appear. "other" matches only `product_type='other'`, so `direct_equity/pms/aif/bond/ncd` entries and travel/home/fire/marine policies cannot be filtered at all. The API returns `hasMore`, but the screen never requests page 1+. _Fix:_ filter on the line/product taxonomy in `lib/insurance/book.ts`, and add infinite scroll.
- **[PUB-23] [Low] Call summary fails on long calls or malformed model output; targets POST 500s on bad JSON** — `src/app/api/mobile/call-summary/route.ts:15,64-66`, `src/app/api/mobile/targets/route.ts:63` — the Zod per-field caps (discussion ≤1200 and so on) are never given to the model, which is told only "total ≤2800". A detailed call therefore gets "could not be summarized reliably" (502). If the model returns invalid JSON, `JSON.parse` throws and the request ends as a 500. `targets` POST uses `await req.json()` with no catch. _Fix:_ state the per-field limits in the prompt (or truncate instead of rejecting), wrap `JSON.parse`, and use `.json().catch(() => null)`.
- **[PUB-24] [Low] /api/me keeps showing the old name after a profile edit** — `src/app/api/me/route.ts:53-55` vs `src/app/api/me/profile/route.ts:121` — `user.name` comes from the JWT, while PATCH /api/me/profile updates `users.name`. The mobile header (`useMe`) shows the old name until the next login. _Fix:_ read `name` from the same `users` row the avatar is already loaded from.
- **[PUB-25] [Low] Stale push tokens are never pruned** — `src/lib/push/expo.ts:86-104` — only push *tickets* are inspected. Expo reports `DeviceNotRegistered` mostly in *receipts* (`/getReceipts`), which are never fetched, so uninstalled-app tokens stay live forever. Combined with PUB-18, `disabled_at` is almost never set. The device-id arrays are also built with `sql.raw` string concatenation instead of a bound `uuid[]` parameter. _Fix:_ store ticket ids and poll receipts in the worker, and bind arrays as parameters.

**Improvements**
- Mobile targets compute the SIP monthly factor and the insurance "booked" definitions inline instead of using `sipMonthlySql` and `lib/insurance/book.ts`, so the app's actuals can differ from the RM dashboard. Targets live under `mobile.YYYY-MM.*` keys that no web screen shows.
- Mobile business POST books every life policy as `life_other` with no `nextPremiumDueDate`, so no renewal reminder is ever raised for it.
- `nativePickImage` returns `null` for both cancel and error (e.g. camera permission denied), so the caller can't tell the user why nothing happened. The header comment points to a non-existent `mobile/components/DashboardWebView.tsx`.
- `/api/me/session/register`'s "legacy, untracked session" branch is unreachable, because `verifySession` already rejects any token without a `sid`.
- `GET /api/me/profile` omits `phone`, which PATCH accepts.

### Health, workers, dev tooling
_Checked:_ GET /api/health, GET /api/health/workers, src/workers/index.ts (scheduleDaily, interval jobs, signal handling, process handlers), src/workers/queues.ts, web-side Q.* producers, scripts/dev-session.mjs.

**Bugs**
- **[PUB-26] [Medium] Worker shutdown kills in-flight work instead of draining it** — `src/workers/index.ts:369-382` — `shutdown` calls each `stop()` synchronously (they only clear intervals; `rta-import-worker` fires `void worker.close()`), then `process.exit(0)` immediately. A deploy or PM2 restart mid-tick aborts a workflow step between the adapter send and the status write, or an RTA import mid-parse. The stalled-run reaper or BullMQ stall recovery then re-runs it, which double-sends (the known outbox gap) or re-imports. `uncaughtException` is also swallowed (`:364-366`), which keeps a process in an undefined state alive. _Fix:_ make stops return promises, await `worker.close()` and the in-flight ticks with a timeout, close the PG pool and Redis, then exit. Exit non-zero after logging an `uncaughtException`.
- **[PUB-27] [Medium] Worker readiness probe stays green while the event spine is dead** — `src/app/api/health/workers/route.ts:21-29` — `MAX_AGE_HOURS` tracks only daily jobs plus the RTA inbox poller. The outbox poller, workflow runner, campaign dispatcher and scheduled-comms record no heartbeat at all (only `rta-inbox-poller` and `scheduleDaily` call `recordRun`). If those loops wedge (e.g. a `running` flag stuck behind a hung query), or a deployment's `WORKER_GROUPS` omits them, the probe reports healthy while no automation or message fires. _Fix:_ write a heartbeat each tick for outbox/wf/campaign/sched and add them to `MAX_AGE_HOURS` with minute-level budgets.
- **[PUB-28] [Medium] Web requests hang forever when Redis is down** — `src/workers/queues.ts:21-27` — the shared IORedis client uses `maxRetriesPerRequest: null`, which BullMQ requires for Workers, and the web process uses the same client for producers (`Q.rtaImport().add` in /api/rta-imports/upload, /api/rta/import-async, reimport, import-status). With Redis unreachable, those `add`/`getJob` calls queue in ioredis' offline queue indefinitely, so the upload request never returns. There is also no `'error'` listener, which floods logs with "Unhandled error event". _Fix:_ give producers a separate connection with a finite `maxRetriesPerRequest` (or `enableOfflineQueue: false`) and attach an error handler.
- **[PUB-29] [Low] Health endpoints: the admin fallback can never pass, and the public probe leaks internals** — `src/app/api/health/workers/route.ts:37-42`, `src/app/api/health/route.ts:47-56` — without `HEALTH_TOKEN`, `/api/health/workers` calls `getSession()`, which on a non-admin path reads only `mfd_session`. Platform admins hold `aina_session`, so the fallback always returns 403, and `platform_support` is excluded anyway. The token is compared with `!==` and passed in a query string, where it ends up in proxy logs. The anonymous `/api/health` returns the raw DB error message (host, port, auth failure text), heap/RSS figures and the build SHA. _Fix:_ read the admin cookie explicitly (or require the token), compare with `timingSafeEqual` against a header, and return only `{status}` when unauthenticated.
- **[PUB-30] [Low] Monthly compliance sweep can be skipped or doubled** — `src/workers/index.ts:197-222` — the "once a month on the 1st" guard is an in-memory variable with no `recordRun` or catch-up. If the worker is down for the whole 1st, the month's SEBI checklist is never seeded. A restart during the 1st re-runs it (and `MONTHLY_COMPLIANCE_ON_BOOT` adds a third path). _Fix:_ use the `scheduler_state` watermark pattern from `scheduleDaily`, keyed by month.
- **[PUB-31] [Low] dev-session.mjs guards are weaker than documented** — `scripts/dev-session.mjs:23-50` — the header says it runs only when NODE_ENV is development, but the code refuses only `production`, so an unset NODE_ENV passes. The localhost regex passes an SSH tunnel to the production database (`@127.0.0.1:5433`), which is a common way to reach prod. It also always prints `mfd_session`, which is the wrong cookie for a platform admin. _Fix:_ require `NODE_ENV === "development"`, refuse when the DB has more than N tenants or a `production` marker row, and pick the cookie with `sessionCookieForRoles`.

**Improvements**
- `scheduleDaily` uses server-local `setHours`. Docker sets `TZ=Asia/Kolkata`, but the PM2 path in `ecosystem.config.cjs` does not, so birthday and NAV jobs there fire at 06:30 UTC (12:00 IST). Compute the times in Asia/Kolkata explicitly.
- `scheduleDaily` and the hourly jobs have no cross-process lease, so two worker processes with overlapping `WORKER_GROUPS` run every daily job twice.
- `withTimeout` in /api/health never clears its timer, and a timed-out `SELECT 1` still holds a pool connection.

---

_End of review. 545 entries (542 distinct: SET-06, PUB-08 and ORG-25 duplicate ORG-01, PLN-16 and PF-07)._
